import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

export default function AdminAuditLogsTab({ 
  auditLogs = [],
  setAuditLogs
}) {
  // ==========================================
  // 1. STATE'LAR VA XAVFSIZ RO'YXAT
  // ==========================================
  const safeLogs = Array.isArray(auditLogs) ? auditLogs : [];

  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'danger' | 'override' | 'auth' | 'routine'
  const [filterRole, setFilterRole] = useState('all');

  // ==========================================
  // 2. YORDAMCHI FUNKSIYALAR (RANG VA TOIFA ANIQLASH)
  // ==========================================
  const getLogCategory = (log) => {
    const actionLower = String(log?.action || '').toLowerCase();
    const detailsLower = String(log?.details || '').toLowerCase();

    // 1. O'chirish, bekor qilish, stop-list yoki xatolar (Qizil)
    if (
      actionLower.includes("o'chir") ||
      actionLower.includes("ochir") ||
      actionLower.includes("bekor") ||
      actionLower.includes("stop-list") ||
      actionLower.includes("taqiq") ||
      actionLower.includes("xato") ||
      detailsLower.includes("o'chirildi")
    ) {
      return 'danger';
    }

    // 2. Admin ruxsatlari, parol va tahrirlashlar (Sariq / Amber)
    if (
      actionLower.includes("ruxsat") ||
      actionLower.includes("override") ||
      actionLower.includes("tahrir") ||
      actionLower.includes("ko'chir") ||
      actionLower.includes("bo'lin") ||
      actionLower.includes("avtomatik qulflash")
    ) {
      return 'override';
    }

    // 3. Tizimga kirish va chiqish (Ko'k / Indigo)
    if (
      actionLower.includes("kirish") ||
      actionLower.includes("chiqish") ||
      actionLower.includes("login")
    ) {
      return 'auth';
    }

    // 4. Oddiy kundalik amallar: qo'shish, sotuv, status (Yashil)
    return 'routine';
  };

  const getRoleLabel = (role) => {
    const r = String(role || 'guest').toLowerCase();
    if (r === 'admin') return { label: 'ðŸ‘‘ Admin', style: 'bg-red-100 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-400' };
    if (r === 'warehouse') return { label: 'ðŸ“¦ Omborchi', style: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/30 dark:text-amber-400' };
    if (r === 'cashier') return { label: 'ðŸ’³ Kassir', style: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 dark:text-emerald-400' };
    if (r === 'waiter') return { label: 'ðŸ“ Ofitsiant', style: 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 dark:text-indigo-400' };
    if (r === 'kitchen') return { label: 'ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³ Oshpaz', style: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900/30 dark:text-purple-400' };
    return { label: 'ðŸ“± Mijoz / Tizim', style: 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300' };
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 3. STATISTIKA VA FILTRLASH MANTIG'I
  // ==========================================
  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    let dangerCount = 0;
    let overrideCount = 0;
    let authCount = 0;

    safeLogs.forEach(log => {
      const cat = getLogCategory(log);
      if (cat === 'danger') dangerCount += 1;
      else if (cat === 'override') overrideCount += 1;
      else if (cat === 'auth') authCount += 1;
    });

    return {
      total: safeLogs.length,
      dangerCount,
      overrideCount,
      authCount
    };
  }, [safeLogs]);

  const filteredLogs = useMemo(() => {
    const searchLower = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    return safeLogs.filter(log => {
      if (!log) return false;

      // 1. Toifa bo'yicha filtr
      if (filterType !== 'all' && getLogCategory(log) !== filterType) {
        return false;
      }

      // 2. Rol bo'yicha filtr
      if (filterRole !== 'all' && String(log.role || '').toLowerCase() !== filterRole) {
        return false;
      }

      // 3. Matn bo'yicha qidiruv
      if (!searchLower) return true;

      const userNameMatch = String(log.userName || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchLower);
      const actionMatch = String(log.action || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchLower);
      const detailsMatch = String(log.details || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchLower);
      const dateMatch = String(log.date || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchLower);

      return userNameMatch || actionMatch || detailsMatch || dateMatch;
    });
  }, [safeLogs, searchTerm, filterType, filterRole]);

  // ==========================================
  // 4. EKSPORT VA TOZALASH FUNKSIYALARI
  // ==========================================
  const handleExportLogs = () => {
    if (filteredLogs.length === 0) {
      alert("Yuklab olish uchun yozuvlar yo'q!");
      return;
    }

    const lines = filteredLogs.map(
      (l, idx) => `${idx + 1}. [${l.date || '-'}] (${l.userName || "Noma'lum"} - ${l.role || 'guest'}) | ${l.action || '-'}: ${l.details || '-'}`
    );
    const content = `CAFE POS APP â€” XAVFSIZLIK VA HARAKATLAR JURNALI\nSana: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}\n==========================================================\n\n` + lines.join('\n');

    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `audit-logs-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.txt`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleClearLogs = () => {
    if (typeof setAuditLogs !== 'function' || safeLogs.length === 0) return;

    if (window.confirm("DIQQAT! Barcha harakatlar tarixini (Audit Logs) o'chirib tozalamoqchimisiz?")) {
      setAuditLogs([
        {
          id: `${Date.now()}`,
          date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
          action: "Tarix tozalandi",
          details: "Admin tomonidan eski audit yozuvlari tozalandi",
          userName: "Admin",
          role: "admin"
        }
      ]);
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 5. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-4 md:p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 animate-fade-in space-y-6">
      
      {/* 5.1. SARLAVHA VA AMALLAR TUGMALARI */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>ðŸ•µï¸â€â™‚ï¸</span> Xodimlar Harakati Tarixi (Audit Logs)
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-500 mt-1">
            Tizimdagi barcha kirish-chiqishlar, o'chirishlar va Admin ruxsatlarining qat'iy nazorati.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleExportLogs}
            disabled={filteredLogs.length === 0}
            className="flex-1 md:flex-none px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-black text-xs transition active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            ðŸ“¥ Yuklab olish (.txt)
          </button>

          {typeof setAuditLogs === 'function' && safeLogs.length > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleClearLogs}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/30 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 font-black text-xs transition active:scale-95"
            >
              ðŸ—‘ Tarixni tozalash
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 5.2. TEZKOR STATISTIKA KARTALARI (BOSILGANDA FILTRLAYDI) */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setFilterType('all')}
          className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 text-left transition ${
            filterType === 'all'
              ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-50/50 dark:bg-indigo-900/20'
              : 'border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/40'
          }`}
        >
          <p className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase text-gray-400">Jami harakatlar</p>
          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white mt-1">{stats.total} ta</p>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setFilterType('danger')}
          className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 text-left transition ${
            filterType === 'danger'
              ? 'border-red-500 bg-red-50/60 dark:bg-red-900/20'
              : 'border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/40'
          }`}
        >
          <p className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase text-red-500">ðŸš¨ O'chirish / Stop-List</p>
          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 mt-1">{stats.dangerCount} ta</p>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setFilterType('override')}
          className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 text-left transition ${
            filterType === 'override'
              ? 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-50/60 dark:bg-amber-900/20'
              : 'border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/40'

          />

          )}

          <div className="text-center py-12">
            <span className="text-5xl opacity-50 mb-3 block">ðŸ“œ</span>

import React, { useState } from 'react';

export default function AdminCategoriesTab({ 
  menuCategories = [], 
  setMenuCategories,
  menuItems = [],
  setMenuItems,
  addAuditLog
}) {
  // ==========================================
  // 1. STATE'LAR VA XAVFSIZ MASSIVLAR
  // ==========================================
  const [isCatModalOpen, setIsCatModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingIndex, setEditingIndex] = useState(null);
  const [catName, setCatName] = useState('');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  const safeCategories = Array.isArray(menuCategories) && menuCategories.length > 0 
    ? menuCategories 
    : ['Asosiy'];
  const safeMenuItems = Array.isArray(menuItems) ? menuItems : [];

  // Har bir toifa ichida nechta taom borligini hisoblash
  const getCategoryItemCount = (categoryName) => {
    return safeMenuItems.filter(item => item && item.category === categoryName).length;
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 2. MODAL OCHISH VA SAQLASH MANTIG'I
  // ==========================================
  const handleOpenModal = (index = null) => {
    if (index !== null) {
      setEditingIndex(index);
      setCatName(safeCategories[index] || '');
    } else {
      setEditingIndex(null);
      setCatName('');
    }
    setIsCatModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSaveCategory = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const safeCat = catName.trim();
    if (!safeCat) {
      alert("Iltimos, toifa nomini kiriting!");
      return;
    }

    // Bir xil nomli toifa qo'shilishidan himoya
    const isDuplicate = safeCategories.some(
      (c, i) => String(c).toLowerCase() === safeCat.toLowerCase() && i !== editingIndex
    );
    if (isDuplicate) {
      alert("Bu nomdagi toifa allaqachon mavjud!");
      return;
    }

    if (editingIndex !== null) {
      // TAHRIRLASH VA TAOMLARNI AVTOMATIK YANGILASH (CASCADING UPDATE)
      const oldCategoryName = safeCategories[editingIndex];
      const newCats = [...safeCategories];
      newCats[editingIndex] = safeCat;

      if (typeof setMenuCategories === 'function') {
        setMenuCategories(newCats);
      }

      // Shu toifaga tegishli barcha taomlarning toifa nomini ham yangilaymiz
      if (oldCategoryName !== safeCat && typeof setMenuItems === 'function') {
        setMenuItems(prevItems => {
          const list = Array.isArray(prevItems) ? prevItems : [];
          return list.map(item => 
            item && item.category === oldCategoryName 
              ? { ...item, category: safeCat } 
              : item
          );
        });
      }

      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog("Toifa tahrirlandi", `"${oldCategoryName}" -> "${safeCat}"`);
      }
    } else {
      // YANGI TOIFA QO'SHISH
      if (typeof setMenuCategories === 'function') {
        setMenuCategories([...safeCategories, safeCat]);
      }

      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog("Yangi toifa qo'shildi", `"${safeCat}" toifasi yaratildi`);
      }
    }

    setIsCatModalOpen(false);
    setCatName('');
    setEditingIndex(null);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 3. TOIFANI O'CHIRISH VA TARTIBLASH MANTIG'I
  // ==========================================
  const handleDeleteCategory = (index, name) => {
    if (safeCategories.length <= 1) {
      alert("Tizimda kamida bitta asosiy toifa qolishi shart!");
      return;
    }

    const linkedItemsCount = getCategoryItemCount(name);
    const fallbackCategory = safeCategories.find((_, i) => i !== index) || 'Asosiy';

    const confirmMessage = linkedItemsCount > 0
      ? `"${name}" toifasida ${linkedItemsCount} ta taom mavjud.\n\nToifani o'chirsangiz, undagi taomlar avtomatik "${fallbackCategory}" toifasiga o'tkaziladi. Davom etasizmi?`
      : `Rostdan ham "${name}" toifasini o'chirmoqchimisiz?`;

    if (window.confirm(confirmMessage)) {
      if (typeof setMenuCategories === 'function') {
        setMenuCategories(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
      }

      // Agar ichida taomlar bo'lsa, ularni zaxira toifaga o'tkazamiz (yo'qolib qolmasligi uchun)
      if (linkedItemsCount > 0 && typeof setMenuItems === 'function') {
        setMenuItems(prevItems => {
          const list = Array.isArray(prevItems) ? prevItems : [];
          return list.map(item =>
            item && item.category === name
              ? { ...item, category: fallbackCategory }
              : item
          );
        });
      }

      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog(
          "Toifa o'chirildi", 
          `"${name}" o'chirildi (${linkedItemsCount} ta taom "${fallbackCategory}" ga o'tkazildi)`
        );
      }
    }
  };

  // Toifani menyuda yuqoriga yoki pastga surish
  const handleMoveCategory = (index, direction) => {
    const targetIndex = index + direction;
    if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= safeCategories.length) return;

    const reordered = [...safeCategories];
    const temp = reordered[index];
    reordered[index] = reordered[targetIndex];
    reordered[targetIndex] = temp;

    if (typeof setMenuCategories === 'function') {
      setMenuCategories(reordered);
    }
  };

  const filteredCategories = safeCategories
    .map((cat, originalIndex) => ({ name: cat, originalIndex }))
    .filter(item => 
      !searchQuery.trim() || 
      String(item.name).toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase())
    );

  // ==========================================
  // 4. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  return (
    <>
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl p-4 md:p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 animate-fade-in max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-5">
        
        {/* SARLAVHA VA QO'SHISH TUGMASI */}
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
              ðŸ“ Toifalar (Kategoriyalar) Boshqaruvi
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mt-1">
              Menyu bo'limlarini tahrirlash, tartiblash yoki yangi toifa qo'shish
            </p>
          </div>
          <button 
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleOpenModal(null)} 
            className="w-full md:w-auto bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-black transition active:scale-95 shadow-md shadow-emerald-600/20 flex justify-center items-center gap-2"
          >
            <span>+</span> Yangi Toifa Qo'shish
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* QIDIRUV SATRI */}
        {safeCategories.length > 3 && (
          <div className="relative">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="ðŸ” Toifa nomini qidiring..."
              className="w-full pl-4 pr-10 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/50 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 font-black"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

export default function AdminDashboard({ 
  orders = [],
  menuItems = [],
  users = [],
  tables = [],
  inventory = [],
  auditLogs = [],
  onNavigateTab
}) {
  // ==========================================
  // 1. STATE VA XAVFSIZ MA'LUMOTLAR
  // ==========================================
  const [periodFilter, setPeriodFilter] = useState('all'); // 'today' | 'all'

  const safeOrders = Array.isArray(orders) ? orders : [];
  const safeMenu = Array.isArray(menuItems) ? menuItems : [];
  const safeInventory = Array.isArray(inventory) ? inventory : [];

  const todayDateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString();

  // Chek summasini xavfsiz olish (finalTotal -> total -> subtotal)
  const getOrderAmount = (order) => {
    if (!order) return 0;
    const raw = order.finalTotal ?? order.total ?? order.subtotal ?? 0;
    return Number(raw) || 0;
  };

  // Xizmat haqini xavfsiz olish
  const getOrderServiceFee = (order) => {
    if (!order) return 0;
    if (order.serviceFeeAmount !== undefined) {
      return Number(order.serviceFeeAmount) || 0;
    }
    const sub = Number(order.subtotal || 0);
    const feePct = Number(order.serviceFee || 0);
    return Math.round((sub * feePct) / 100);
  };

  // Chegirma summasini xavfsiz olish
  const getOrderDiscount = (order) => {
    if (!order) return 0;
    return Number(order.discountAmount ?? order.discount ?? 0) || 0;
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 2. DAVR BO'YICHA FILTRLASH (BUGUN / BARCHASI)
  // ==========================================
  const periodOrders = useMemo(() => {
    if (periodFilter === 'today') {
      return safeOrders.filter(o => o && (!o.date || o.date === todayDateStr));
    }
    return safeOrders;
  }, [safeOrders, periodFilter, todayDateStr]);

  // To'langan cheklar, Qarz cheklar va Hozirgi faol (ochiq) cheklar
  const completedOrders = useMemo(() => {
    return periodOrders.filter(o => o?.status === "To'langan");
  }, [periodOrders]);

  const debtOrders = useMemo(() => {
    return periodOrders.filter(
      o => o?.status === 'Qarz' || o?.paymentMethod === 'Qarz'
    );
  }, [periodOrders]);

  const activeOpenOrders = useMemo(() => {
    return safeOrders.filter(
      o => o && o.status !== "To'langan" && o.status !== 'Qarz'
    );
  }, [safeOrders]);

  // ==========================================
  // 3. ASOSIY MOLIYA KO'RSATKICHLARI (KPI)
  // ==========================================
  const totalRevenue = useMemo(() => {
    return completedOrders.reduce((sum, order) => sum + getOrderAmount(order), 0);
  }, [completedOrders]);

  const totalDebtAmount = useMemo(() => {
    return debtOrders.reduce((sum, order) => sum + getOrderAmount(order), 0);
  }, [debtOrders]);

  const activeTablesRevenue = useMemo(() => {
    return activeOpenOrders.reduce((sum, order) => sum + getOrderAmount(order), 0);
  }, [activeOpenOrders]);

  const totalDiscount = useMemo(() => {
    return [...completedOrders, ...debtOrders].reduce(
      (sum, order) => sum + getOrderDiscount(order), 
      0
    );
  }, [completedOrders, debtOrders]);

  const totalServiceFee = useMemo(() => {
    return completedOrders.reduce(
      (sum, order) => sum + getOrderServiceFee(order), 
      0
    );
  }, [completedOrders]);

  const totalOrdersCount = completedOrders.length;
  const averageReceipt = totalOrdersCount > 0 ? Math.round(totalRevenue / totalOrdersCount) : 0;

  // ==========================================
  // 4. TO'LOV TURLARI BO'YICHA TUSHUM (6 XIL USUL)
  // ==========================================
  const paymentStats = useMemo(() => {
    const stats = {
      Naqd: 0,
      Plastik: 0,
      Payme: 0,
      Click: 0,
      Aralash: 0,
      Qarz: totalDebtAmount
    };

    completedOrders.forEach(order => {
      const method = order?.paymentMethod || 'Naqd';
      const amount = getOrderAmount(order);

      // Agar aralash to'lov tafsilotlari bo'lsa (splitPayment / mixedDetails)
      if (method === 'Aralash' && order?.mixedDetails) {
        stats.Aralash += amount;
      } else if (stats[method] !== undefined) {
        stats[method] += amount;
      } else {
        stats[method] = (stats[method] || 0) + amount;
      }
    });

    return stats;
  }, [completedOrders, totalDebtAmount]);

  // ==========================================
  // 5. TOP-5 TAOMLAR VA OFITSIANTLAR REYTINGI
  // ==========================================
  const popularItems = useMemo(() => {
    const itemsStats = {};
    [...completedOrders, ...debtOrders].forEach(order => {
      if (Array.isArray(order?.items)) {
        order.items.forEach(item => {
          if (!item?.name) return;
          const qty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 0;
          const price = Number(item.price ?? item.variants?.[0]?.price ?? 0) || 0;

          if (!itemsStats[item.name]) {
            itemsStats[item.name] = { count: 0, revenue: 0 };
          }
          itemsStats[item.name].count += qty;
          itemsStats[item.name].revenue += price * qty;
        });
      }
    });

    return Object.entries(itemsStats)
      .sort((a, b) => b[1].count - a[1].count)
      .slice(0, 5);
  }, [completedOrders, debtOrders]);

  const waiterRanking = useMemo(() => {
    const waiterMap = {};
    completedOrders.forEach(order => {
      const wName = order?.waiter || "Noma'lum";
      if (!waiterMap[wName]) {
        waiterMap[wName] = { ordersCount: 0, revenue: 0 };
      }
      waiterMap[wName].ordersCount += 1;
      waiterMap[wName].revenue += getOrderAmount(order);
    });

    return Object.entries(waiterMap)
      .sort((a, b) => b[1].revenue - a[1].revenue)
      .slice(0, 5);
  }, [completedOrders]);

  // Stop-listdagi taomlar va kam qolgan ombor mahsulotlari
  const stopListItemsCount = safeMenu.filter(m => m?.is_available === false).length;
  const lowStockCount = safeInventory.filter(
    i => Number(i?.quantity ?? i?.qty ?? 0) <= Number(i?.minLimit ?? 5)
  ).length;

  // ==========================================
  // 6. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================

    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      
      {/* YUQORI PANEL: SARLAVHA VA VAQT FILTRI */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/50 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>ðŸ“Š</span> Restoran Moliyaviy Ko'rsatkichlari
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
            Jonli tushum, to'lov turlari, eng xaridorgir taomlar va zal holati
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-1 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 w-full sm:w-auto">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setPeriodFilter('today')}
            className={`flex-1 sm:flex-initial px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-black transition ${
              periodFilter === 'today'
                ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400'
            }`}
          >
            ðŸ“… Bugungi ({todayDateStr})
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setPeriodFilter('all')}
            className={`flex-1 sm:flex-initial px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-black transition ${
              periodFilter === 'all'
                ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400'
            }`}
          >
            ðŸ—‚ Barcha vaqt
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 1-QATOR: ASOSIY KPI KARTOCHKALAR */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/20 p-6 rounded-3xl border border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-800/50 shadow-sm">
          <p className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
            ðŸ’° Jami Sof Tushum
          </p>
          <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
            {totalRevenue.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-base">s</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-700/70 dark:text-emerald-300/70 mt-2">
            Yopilgan cheklar bo'yicha
          </p>

                        {waiterName}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-400">
                        {wStats.ordersCount} ta yopilgan chek
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

    </div>

  const roleOptions = [
    { value: 'waiter', label: 'ðŸ“ Ofitsiant (4 xonali PIN)', pinLen: 4, badge: 'Ofitsiant' },

    { value: 'admin', label: 'ðŸ‘‘ Admin (6 xonali PIN)', pinLen: 6, badge: 'Admin' }
  ];

    return (role === 'admin' || role === 'warehouse') ? 6 : 4;

    const found = roleOptions.find(r => r.value === role);

        name: '', 
        role: 'waiter',

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';

// Birliklar konvertatsiyasi (Retseptdagi gr/kg/ml/litr ni ombordagi birlikka aniq o'girish)
const RECIPE_UNITS = [
  { value: 'gr', label: 'gr', family: 'weight', factor: 1 },
  { value: 'kg', label: 'kg', family: 'weight', factor: 1000 },
  { value: 'tonna', label: 'tonna', family: 'weight', factor: 1000000 },
  { value: 'ml', label: 'ml', family: 'volume', factor: 1 },
  { value: 'litr', label: 'litr', family: 'volume', factor: 1000 },
  { value: 'dona', label: 'dona', family: 'count', factor: 1 },
  { value: 'porsiya', label: 'pors', family: 'count', factor: 1 },
  { value: 'boglam', label: "bog'lam", family: 'count', factor: 1 },
  { value: 'qadoq', label: 'qadoq', family: 'count', factor: 1 }
];

const normalizeUnit = (uStr) => {
  const u = String(uStr || 'kg').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['g', 'gr', 'gram', 'gramm'].includes(u)) return 'gr';
  if (['kg', 'kilogram', 'kilogramm', 'kilo'].includes(u)) return 'kg';
  if (['t', 'ton', 'tonna', 'tn'].includes(u)) return 'tonna';
  if (['ml', 'millilitr'].includes(u)) return 'ml';
  if (['l', 'lt', 'litr', 'liter'].includes(u)) return 'litr';
  if (['pors', 'porsiya'].includes(u)) return 'porsiya';
  if (["bog'lam", 'boglam', 'bog'].includes(u)) return 'boglam';
  if (['pachka', 'qadoq'].includes(u)) return 'qadoq';
  return 'dona';
};

const getUnitMeta = (uStr) => {
  const norm = normalizeUnit(uStr);
  return RECIPE_UNITS.find(u => u.value === norm) || {
    value: norm,
    label: norm,
    family: 'count',
    factor: 1
  };
};

// Retseptdagi miqdorni ombordagi asosiy birlikka o'girish (Masalan: 250 gr -> 0.25 kg)
const convertRecipeQtyToInventoryUnit = (qty, recipeUnit, invUnit) => {
  const num = Number(qty) || 0;
  const rMeta = getUnitMeta(recipeUnit || invUnit);
  const iMeta = getUnitMeta(invUnit);

  if (rMeta.value === iMeta.value) return num;
  if (rMeta.family !== iMeta.family) return num;

  const inBase = num * rMeta.factor;
  return Number((inBase / iMeta.factor).toFixed(6));
};

// Ichki xavfsiz xotira o'qigich (Tashqi useSticky fayliga ehtiyoj yo'q)
const readLocalBackup = (key, fallback = []) => {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
};

export default function AdminMenuTab({ 
  menuItems = [], 
  setMenuItems, 
  menuCategories = [],
  inventory = [],
  addAuditLog
}) {
  // Agar AdminPanel dan inventory prop uzatilmagan bo'lsa, localStorage dan olamiz
  const [localInventory, setLocalInventory] = useState(() => readLocalBackup('pos_inventory', []));

  useEffect(() => {
    setLocalInventory(readLocalBackup('pos_inventory', []));
  }, []);

  const safeCategories = Array.isArray(menuCategories) && menuCategories.length > 0 
    ? menuCategories 
    : readLocalBackup('pos_categories', ['Asosiy', 'Ichimlik', 'Shirinlik']);

  const safeInventory = Array.isArray(inventory) && inventory.length > 0 
    ? inventory 
    : (Array.isArray(localInventory) ? localInventory : []);

  const safeMenuItems = Array.isArray(menuItems) ? menuItems : [];

  // Qidiruv va Filtr statelari
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState('Barchasi');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'active' | 'stop'

  // Modal statelari
  const [isMenuModalOpen, setIsMenuModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState(null);
  
  const [newItemData, setNewItemData] = useState({
    name: '', 
    category: safeCategories[0] || 'Asosiy', 
    is_available: true
  });

  const [variantInputs, setVariantInputs] = useState([
    { id: 'v_default', name: '1 porsiya', price: '' }
  ]);
  
  const [recipeInputs, setRecipeInputs] = useState([]);

  // ==========================================
  // 1. TAOM TANNARXI VA SOF FOYDASINI HISOBLASH
  // ==========================================
  const calculateRecipeFoodCost = (recipeList) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(recipeList) || recipeList.length === 0) return 0;

    let totalCost = 0;
    recipeList.forEach(r => {
      if (!r || !(r.inventoryId ?? r.productId)) return;
      const rawQty = Number(r.rawQty ?? r.qty ?? r.amount ?? 0) || 0;
      if (rawQty <= 0) return;

      const invObj = safeInventory.find(
        i => i && String(i.id) === String(r.inventoryId ?? r.productId)
      );
      if (!invObj) return;

      const invCostPrice = Number(invObj.costPrice ?? invObj.price ?? 0) || 0;
      const invUnit = normalizeUnit(invObj.unit || 'kg');
      const recUnit = normalizeUnit(r.recipeUnit || invUnit);

      const qtyInInvUnit = r.rawQty !== undefined
        ? convertRecipeQtyToInventoryUnit(r.rawQty, recUnit, invUnit)
        : (r.recipeUnit ? convertRecipeQtyToInventoryUnit(rawQty, recUnit, invUnit) : Number(r.qty ?? r.amount ?? 0));

      totalCost += qtyInInvUnit * invCostPrice;
    });

    return Math.round(totalCost);
  };

  const modalProfitAnalysis = useMemo(() => {
    const sellingPrice = Number(variantInputs[0]?.price) || 0;
    const foodCost = calculateRecipeFoodCost(recipeInputs);
    const netProfit = sellingPrice - foodCost;
    const foodCostPercent = sellingPrice > 0 ? Number(((foodCost / sellingPrice) * 100).toFixed(1)) : 0;
    const marginPercent = sellingPrice > 0 ? Number(((netProfit / sellingPrice) * 100).toFixed(1)) : 0;

    return {
      sellingPrice,
      foodCost,
      netProfit,
      foodCostPercent,
      marginPercent
    };
  }, [variantInputs, recipeInputs, safeInventory]);

  // ==========================================
  // 2. JADVAL FILTRLASH MANTIG'I
  // ==========================================
  const filteredMenuItems = useMemo(() => {
    return safeMenuItems.filter(item => {
      if (!item) return false;

      const matchesSearch = !searchQuery.trim() || 
        item.name?.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase());

      const matchesCat = filterCategory === 'Barchasi' || item.category === filterCategory;

      const isAvailable = item.is_available !== false;
      const matchesStatus = 
        filterStatus === 'all' ? true :
        filterStatus === 'active' ? isAvailable : !isAvailable;

      return matchesSearch && matchesCat && matchesStatus;
    });
  }, [safeMenuItems, searchQuery, filterCategory, filterStatus]);

  // ==========================================
  // 3. MODALNI OCHISH VA BOSHQARISH
  // ==========================================
  const handleOpenMenuModal = (item = null) => {
    setLocalInventory(readLocalBackup('pos_inventory', []));

    if (item) {
      setEditingItem(item);
      setNewItemData({
        name: item.name || '',
        category: item.category || safeCategories[0] || 'Asosiy',
        is_available: item.is_available !== false
      });

      if (Array.isArray(item.variants) && item.variants.length > 0) {
        setVariantInputs(
          item.variants.map((v, idx) => ({
            id: v.id || `v_${idx}_${Date.now()}`,
            name: v.name || '1 porsiya',
            price: v.price !== undefined ? String(v.price) : ''
          }))
        );
      } else {
        setVariantInputs([
          { id: `v_${Date.now()}`, name: '1 porsiya', price: item.price ? String(item.price) : '' }
        ]);
      }

      if (Array.isArray(item.recipe) && item.recipe.length > 0) {
        setRecipeInputs(
          item.recipe.map(r => {
            const invObj = safeInventory.find(i => String(i.id) === String(r.inventoryId ?? r.productId));
            const baseU = normalizeUnit(invObj?.unit || r.unit || 'kg');
            return {
              inventoryId: String(r.inventoryId ?? r.productId ?? r.id ?? ''),
              qty: String(r.rawQty ?? r.qty ?? r.amount ?? ''),
              recipeUnit: normalizeUnit(r.recipeUnit || baseU)
            };
          })
        );
      } else {
        setRecipeInputs([]);
      }
    } else {
      setEditingItem(null);
      setNewItemData({ 
        name: '', 
        category: safeCategories[0] || 'Asosiy', 
        is_available: true 
      });
      setVariantInputs([
        { id: `v_${Date.now()}`, name: '1 porsiya', price: '' }
      ]);
      setRecipeInputs([]); 
    }
    setIsMenuModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleAddVariantInput = () => {
    setVariantInputs(prev => [
      ...prev,
      { id: `v_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`, name: '0.5 porsiya', price: '' }
    ]);
  };

  const handleVariantChange = (index, field, value) => {
    setVariantInputs(prev => prev.map((v, i) => (i === index ? { ...v, [field]: value } : v)));
  };

  const handleRemoveVariantInput = (index) => {
    if (variantInputs.length <= 1) {
      alert("Taomda kamida bitta narx (porsiya) bo'lishi shart!");
      return;
    }
    setVariantInputs(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handleAddRecipeInput = () => {
    setRecipeInputs(prev => [...prev, { inventoryId: '', qty: '', recipeUnit: 'gr' }]);
  };

  const handleRecipeChange = (index, field, value) => {
    setRecipeInputs(prev =>
      prev.map((r, i) => {
        if (i !== index) return r;
        if (field === 'inventoryId') {
          const foundInv = safeInventory.find(inv => String(inv.id) === String(value));
          const defUnit = foundInv ? normalizeUnit(foundInv.unit) : 'kg';
          return { ...r, inventoryId: value, recipeUnit: defUnit };
        }
        return { ...r, [field]: value };
      })
    );
  };

  const handleRemoveRecipeInput = (index) => {
    setRecipeInputs(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 4. TAOMNI SAQLASH, STOP-LIST VA O'CHIRISH
  // ==========================================
  const handleSaveMenuItem = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault(); 
    
    const safeName = newItemData.name.trim();
    if (!safeName) {
      alert("Iltimos, taom nomini kiriting!");
      return;
    }

    const cleanedVariants = variantInputs
      .map((v, idx) => ({
        id: v.id || `v_${Date.now()}_${idx}`,
        name: (v.name || '').trim() || '1 porsiya',
        price: Number(v.price)
      }))
      .filter(v => !isNaN(v.price) && v.price > 0);

    if (cleanedVariants.length === 0) {
      alert("Iltimos, taom narxini to'g'ri kiriting (0 dan katta raqam)!"); 
      return;
    }

    const cleanedRecipe = recipeInputs
      .filter(r => r.inventoryId && Number(r.qty) > 0)
      .map(r => {
        const matchedInv = safeInventory.find(i => String(i.id) === String(r.inventoryId));
        const normalizedId = matchedInv ? matchedInv.id : r.inventoryId;
        const invBaseUnit = normalizeUnit(matchedInv?.unit || 'kg');
        const chosenRecUnit = normalizeUnit(r.recipeUnit || invBaseUnit);
        const rawQtyNum = Number(r.qty);

        const qtyInBaseUnit = convertRecipeQtyToInventoryUnit(rawQtyNum, chosenRecUnit, invBaseUnit);

        return {
          inventoryId: normalizedId,
          productId: normalizedId,
          name: matchedInv?.name || '',
          unit: invBaseUnit,
          recipeUnit: chosenRecUnit,
          rawQty: rawQtyNum,
          qty: qtyInBaseUnit,
          amount: qtyInBaseUnit
        };
      });

    const computedCost = calculateRecipeFoodCost(cleanedRecipe);

    if (editingItem) {
      if (typeof setMenuItems === 'function') {
        setMenuItems(prev => prev.map(item => 
          item.id === editingItem.id ? {
            ...item, 
            name: safeName, 
            category: newItemData.category || safeCategories[0], 
            recipe: cleanedRecipe,
            costPrice: computedCost,
            is_available: newItemData.is_available,
            price: cleanedVariants[0].price,
            variants: cleanedVariants
          } : item
        ));
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog(
          "Taom tahrirlandi", 
          `"${safeName}" (Sotish: ${cleanedVariants[0].price.toLocaleString()} s | Tannarx: ${computedCost.toLocaleString()} s)`
        );
      }
    } else {
      const newItem = {
        id: Date.now(), 
        name: safeName, 
        category: newItemData.category || safeCategories[0], 
        is_available: newItemData.is_available, 
        recipe: cleanedRecipe, 
        costPrice: computedCost,
        price: cleanedVariants[0].price,
        variants: cleanedVariants
      };
      if (typeof setMenuItems === 'function') {
        setMenuItems(prev => [...prev, newItem]);
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog(
          "Yangi taom qo'shildi", 
          `"${safeName}" (Sotish: ${cleanedVariants[0].price.toLocaleString()} s | Tannarx: ${computedCost.toLocaleString()} s)`
        );
      }
    }
    setIsMenuModalOpen(false);
  };

  const handleToggleAvailability = (item) => {
    if (typeof setMenuItems !== 'function') return;
    const nextStatus = item.is_available === false ? true : false;

    setMenuItems(prev => prev.map(m => 
      m.id === item.id ? { ...m, is_available: nextStatus } : m
    ));

    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
      addAuditLog(
        nextStatus ? "Sotuvga qaytarildi" : "Stop-Listga olindi",
        `"${item.name}" -> ${nextStatus ? 'Sotuvda mavjud' : 'Vaqtincha tugadi'}`
      );
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteMenuItem = (id, itemName) => {
    if (window.confirm(`Rostdan ham "${itemName}" taomini menyudan butunlay o'chirmoqchimisiz?`)) {
      if (typeof setMenuItems === 'function') {
        setMenuItems(prev => prev.filter(item => item.id !== id));
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog("Taom o'chirildi", `"${itemName}" menyudan o'chirildi`);
      }
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 5. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  return (
    <>
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl p-4 md:p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 animate-fade-in space-y-5">
        
        {/* SARLAVHA VA YANGI QO'SHISH TUGMASI */}
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
              ðŸ” Taomlar, Texnologik Karta va Tannarx (Food Cost)
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mt-1">
              Har bir porsiyaning xomashyo tannarxi, sof foydasi va rentabellik foizi
            </p>
          </div>
          <button 
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleOpenMenuModal(null)} 
            className="w-full md:w-auto bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-black transition active:scale-95 shadow-md flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <span>+</span> Yangi Taom Qo'shish
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* QIDIRUV VA FILTR PANELI */}
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-3 justify-between items-stretch lg:items-center bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/50 p-3 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="ðŸ” Taom nomini qidiring..."
              className="w-full pl-4 pr-9 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 font-black"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
            <select
              value={filterCategory}
              onChange={e => setFilterCategory(e.target.value)}
              className="px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-xs font-black text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              <option value="Barchasi">ðŸ“ Barcha toifalar</option>
              {safeCategories.map(c => (
                <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <div className="flex bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-gray-800 p-1 rounded-xl">
              {[
                { id: 'all', label: 'Barchasi' },
                { id: 'active', label: 'ðŸŸ¢ Sotuvda' },
                { id: 'stop', label: 'ðŸ”´ Stop-List' }
              ].map(st => (
                <button
                  key={st.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setFilterStatus(st.id)}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-black transition ${
                    filterStatus === st.id
                      ? 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-white shadow-sm'
                      : 'text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400'
                  }`}
                >
                  {st.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* TAOMLAR JADVALI */}
        <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/50 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
            <thead>
              <tr className="bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 border-b dark:border-gray-700">
                <th className="p-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Taom va Toifasi</th>
                <th className="p-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Holati</th>
                <th className="p-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Sotish Narxi</th>
                <th className="p-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Tannarx (Food Cost)</th>
                <th className="p-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Sof Foyda (1 pors)</th>
                <th className="p-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider text-right">Amallar</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100 dark:divide-gray-700">
              {filteredMenuItems.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan="6" className="text-center py-10 font-bold text-gray-400">
                    Menyuda taomlar topilmadi.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                filteredMenuItems.map(item => {
                  const isAvailable = item.is_available !== false;
                  const variants = Array.isArray(item.variants) && item.variants.length > 0
                    ? item.variants
                    : [{ name: '1 porsiya', price: Number(item.price || 0) }];

                  const mainSellPrice = Number(variants[0]?.price || item.price || 0);
                  const liveFoodCost = calculateRecipeFoodCost(item.recipe) || Number(item.costPrice || 0);
                  const netProfit = mainSellPrice - liveFoodCost;
                  const foodCostPct = mainSellPrice > 0 ? Math.round((liveFoodCost / mainSellPrice) * 100) : 0;
                  const marginPct = mainSellPrice > 0 ? Math.round((netProfit / mainSellPrice) * 100) : 0;

                  return (
                    <tr 
                      key={item.id} 
                      className={`hover:bg-white dark:hover:bg-gray-800 transition ${
                        !isAvailable ? 'opacity-65 bg-red-50/30 dark:bg-red-900/10' : ''
                      }`}
                    >
                      <td className="p-4">
                        <p className="font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 text-base">
                          {item.name}
                        </p>
                        <span className="inline-block mt-1 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold">
                          {item.category}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="p-4">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleToggleAvailability(item)}
                          className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition active:scale-95 ${
                            isAvailable
                              ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-200'
                              : 'bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/40 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-200'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {isAvailable ? 'ðŸŸ¢ Sotuvda' : 'ðŸ”´ Stop-List'}
                        </button>
                      </td>

                      <td className="p-4">
                        <div className="space-y-1">
                          {variants.map((v, vIdx) => (
                            <div key={vIdx} className="text-xs">
                              <span className="font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 text-sm">
                                {Number(v.price || 0).toLocaleString()} s
                              </span>
                              <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-bold ml-1">
                                ({v.name})
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      
                      <td className="p-4">
                        {Array.isArray(item.recipe) && item.recipe.length > 0 ? (
                          <div className="space-y-1">
                            <span className="font-black text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 block">
                              {liveFoodCost.toLocaleString()} s
                            </span>
                            <span className={`inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black ${
                              foodCostPct <= 35
                                ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-900/40 dark:text-emerald-300'
                                : foodCostPct <= 55
                                ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/40 dark:text-amber-300'
                                : 'bg-red-100 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/40 dark:text-red-300'
                            }`}>
                              Food Cost: {foodCostPct}% ({item.recipe.length} masalliq)
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-gray-400 text-[11px] font-bold">
                            Retsept kiritilmagan
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="p-4">
                        {Array.isArray(item.recipe) && item.recipe.length > 0 && liveFoodCost > 0 ? (
                          <div>
                            <span className={`font-black text-sm block ${
                              netProfit > 0 ? 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400' : 'text-red-600'
                            }`}>
                              {netProfit > 0 ? `+${netProfit.toLocaleString()}` : netProfit.toLocaleString()} s
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400">
                              Foyda marjasi: {marginPct}%
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">â€”</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      
                      <td className="p-4 text-right space-x-2 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <button 
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleOpenMenuModal(item)} 
                          className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs transition"
                        >
                          Tahrirlash
                        </button>
                        <button 
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleDeleteMenuItem(item.id, item.name)} 
                          className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/30 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs transition"
                        >
                          O'chirish
                        </button>
                      </td>

                    </tr>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MODAL */}
      {isMenuModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 w-full max-w-2xl rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4 my-8 relative max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
            
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b pb-3 dark:border-gray-700 sticky top-0 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 z-10">
              <h3 className="font-black text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
                {editingItem ? 'âœï¸ Taom va Tannarxni Tahrirlash' : 'ðŸ½ Yangi Taom va Tannarx Kalkulyatsiyasi'}
              </h3>
              <button 
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsMenuModalOpen(false)} 
                className="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-500 hover:text-red-500 font-bold transition"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSaveMenuItem} className="space-y-5 pt-2">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Taom Nomi *
                  </label>
                  <input 
                    type="text" 
                    required 
                    value={newItemData.name} 
                    onChange={e => setNewItemData({ ...newItemData, name: e.target.value })} 
                    className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600 outline-none focus:border-amber-500 text-gray-900 dark:text-white font-bold" 
                    placeholder="Masalan: Qozonkabob" 
                  />
                </div>
                
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Toifasi *
                  </label>
                  <select 
                    value={newItemData.category} 
                    onChange={e => setNewItemData({ ...newItemData, category: e.target.value })} 
                    className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600 outline-none text-gray-900 dark:text-white font-bold cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {safeCategories.map(c => (
                      <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Porsiyalar va Sotish Narxi */}
              <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/40 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Porsiyalar va Sotish Narxi (so'mda) *
                  </label>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleAddVariantInput}
                    className="text-[10px] font-black text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-900/40 dark:text-indigo-300 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg uppercase hover:bg-indigo-200 transition"
                  >
                    + Porsiya qo'shish
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-2.5">
                  {variantInputs.map((vInp, idx) => (
                    <div key={vInp.id || idx} className="flex gap-2 items-center">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={vInp.name}
                        onChange={e => handleVariantChange(idx, 'name', e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="1 porsiya / 0.5 pors"
                        className="w-2/5 p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600 outline-none text-xs font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white"
                      />
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        required
                        min="1"
                        value={vInp.price}
                        onChange={e => handleVariantChange(idx, 'price', e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Sotish narxi (masalan: 45000)"
                        className="flex-1 p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600 outline-none focus:border-amber-500 text-xs font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white"
                      />
                      {variantInputs.length > 1 && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
