import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG = {
  enabled: false,
  botToken: '',
  chatId: '',
  notifyOnZReport: true,
  notifyOnDebt: true,
  notifyOnDiscount: true,
  notifyOnLowStock: true,
  notifyOnEverySale: false,
  notifyOnSecurity: true
};

const readTelegramConfig = () => {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem('pos_telegram_config');
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG;
    return { ...DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG, ...(JSON.parse(raw) || {}) };
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG;
  }
};

export default function AdminSettingsTab({ 
  cafeName = 'Premium Cafe', 
  setCafeName, 
  serviceFee = 10, 
  setServiceFee,
  tables = [],
  setTables,
  orders = [],
  setOrders,
  menuItems = [],
  setMenuItems,
  menuCategories = [],
  setMenuCategories,
  users = [],
  setUsers,
  inventory = [],
  setInventory,
  inventoryLogs = [],
  setInventoryLogs,
  auditLogs = [],
  setAuditLogs,
  currentUser,
  requireAdminOverride,
  addAuditLog 
}) {
  const [tempCafeName, setTempCafeName] = useState(cafeName || '');
  const [tempServiceFee, setTempServiceFee] = useState(
    serviceFee !== undefined ? String(serviceFee) : '10'
  );
  const [newTableName, setNewTableName] = useState('');
  const [saveSuccess, setSaveSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Telegram Bot sozlamalari (Bevoista localStorage bilan ishlaydi)
  const [telegramConfig, setTelegramConfig] = useState(readTelegramConfig);
  const [showBotToken, setShowBotToken] = useState(false);
  const [tgStatus, setTgStatus] = useState({ loading: false, message: '', isError: false });

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem('pos_telegram_config', JSON.stringify(telegramConfig));
    } catch {
      // ignore storage error
    }
  }, [telegramConfig]);

  const safeTables = Array.isArray(tables) ? tables : [];
  const safeOrders = Array.isArray(orders) ? orders : [];

  const activeTableNames = safeOrders
    .filter(o => o && !["To'langan", 'Qarz', 'Arxiv'].includes(o.status))
    .map(o => o.table);

  const handleSaveSettings = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const cleanedName = tempCafeName.trim();
    const feeNumber = Number(tempServiceFee);

    if (!cleanedName) {
      alert("Iltimos, Kafe nomini kiriting!");
      return;
    }

    if (isNaN(feeNumber) || feeNumber < 0 || feeNumber > 100) {
      alert("Xizmat haqi 0% va 100% oralig'ida bo'lishi kerak!");
      return;
    }

    if (typeof setCafeName === 'function') setCafeName(cleanedName);
    if (typeof setServiceFee === 'function') setServiceFee(feeNumber);

    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
      addAuditLog(
        "Sozlamalar yangilandi", 
        `Kafe nomi: "${cleanedName}", Xizmat haqi: ${feeNumber}%`
      );
    }

    setSaveSuccess(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaveSuccess(false), 2500);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // TELEGRAM ICHKI FUNKSIYALARI
  // ==========================================
  const updateTgField = (field, value) => {
    setTelegramConfig(prev => ({
      ...DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG,
      ...(prev || {}),
      [field]: value
    }));
  };

  const handleAutoDetectChatId = async () => {
    const cleanToken = String(telegramConfig?.botToken || '').trim();
    if (!cleanToken) {
      setTgStatus({ loading: false, message: 'âŒ Avval Bot Tokenni kiriting!', isError: true });
      return;
    }

    setTgStatus({ loading: true, message: 'Telegram bot tekshirilmoqda...', isError: false });

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${cleanToken}/getUpdates`);
      const data = await res.json();

      if (!data.ok) {
        setTgStatus({ loading: false, message: `âŒ ${data.description || "Bot Token noto'g'ri!"}`, isError: true });
        return;
      }

      const updates = Array.isArray(data.result) ? data.result : [];
      let foundId = '';
      let foundName = '';

      for (let i = updates.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const msg = updates[i].message || updates[i].my_chat_member || updates[i].channel_post;
        if (msg && msg.chat && msg.chat.id) {
          foundId = String(msg.chat.id);
          foundName = msg.chat.title || `${msg.chat.first_name || ''} ${msg.chat.last_name || ''}`.trim() || msg.chat.username || 'Foydalanuvchi';
          break;
        }
      }

      if (foundId) {
        updateTgField('chatId', foundId);
        updateTgField('enabled', true);
        setTgStatus({
          loading: false,
          message: `âœ… Topildi! "${foundName}" (ID: ${foundId}) avtomatik ulandi.`,
          isError: false
        });
      } else {
        setTgStatus({
          loading: false,
          message: "âŒ Xabarlar topilmadi! Telegramda botingizga kirib '/start' bosing va qayta urinib ko'ring.",
          isError: true
        });
      }
    } catch {
      setTgStatus({ loading: false, message: 'âŒ Internet aloqasini tekshiring!', isError: true });
    }
  };

  const handleSendTestTelegram = async () => {
    const cleanToken = String(telegramConfig?.botToken || '').trim();
    const rawChatIds = String(telegramConfig?.chatId || '').trim();

    if (!cleanToken || !rawChatIds) {
      setTgStatus({ loading: false, message: 'âŒ Bot Token va Chat ID ni kiriting!', isError: true });
      return;
    }

    setTgStatus({ loading: true, message: 'Sinov xabari yuborilmoqda...', isError: false });

    const testHtml = [
      `ðŸš€ <b>TELEGRAM BOT MUVAFFAQIYATLI ULANDI!</b>`,
      `â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”`,
      `ðŸª <b>Kafe:</b> ${cafeName}`,
      `ðŸ‘¤ <b>Tekshiruvchi:</b> ${currentUser?.name || 'Admin'}`,
      `ðŸ•’ <b>Vaqt:</b> ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
      `â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”`,
      `âœ… Endi smena hisobotlari (Z-Otchyot), qarzlar, chegirmalar va ombor ogohlantirishlari shu yerga jonli kelib tushadi!`
    ].join('\n');

    try {
      const chatIds = rawChatIds.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      const responses = await Promise.all(
        chatIds.map(id =>
          fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${cleanToken}/sendMessage`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              chat_id: id,
              text: testHtml,
              parse_mode: 'HTML'
            })
          }).then(r => r.json())
        )
      );

      const errObj = responses.find(r => !r.ok);
      if (errObj) {
        setTgStatus({ loading: false, message: `âŒ Xatolik: ${errObj.description}`, isError: true });
      } else {
        setTgStatus({ loading: false, message: 'âœ… Sinov xabari Telegramingizga muvaffaqiyatli yuborildi!', isError: false });
      }
    } catch {
      setTgStatus({ loading: false, message: 'âŒ Internet aloqasi yo\'q!', isError: true });
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // STOLLAR VA ZAXIRA BOSHQARUVI
  // ==========================================
  const handleAddTable = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const trimmed = newTableName.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;

    if (safeTables.some(t => t.toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase())) {
      alert(`"${trimmed}" nomli stol allaqachon mavjud!`);
      return;
    }

    if (typeof setTables === 'function') {
      setTables(prev => [...(Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []), trimmed]);
    }
    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
      addAuditLog("Yangi stol qo'shildi", `"${trimmed}" zalga qo'shildi`);
    }
    setNewTableName('');
  };

  const handleDeleteTable = (tableName) => {
    if (activeTableNames.includes(tableName)) {
      alert(`â›”ï¸ "${tableName}" da hozir yopilmagan faol buyurtma bor! Avval stol hisobini yoping.`);
      return;
    }

    if (window.confirm(`Rostdan ham "${tableName}" ni zal ro'yxatidan o'chirmoqchimisiz?`)) {
      if (typeof setTables === 'function') {
        setTables(prev => (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).filter(t => t !== tableName));
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog("Stol o'chirildi", `"${tableName}" zal ro'yxatidan o'chirildi`);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleExportBackup = () => {
    const backupPayload = {
      version: '2.0',
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      cafeName,
      serviceFee,
      tables: safeTables,
      menuCategories,
      menuItems,
      users,
      inventory,
      inventoryLogs,
      orders: safeOrders,
      auditLogs,
      telegramConfig
    };

    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(backupPayload, null, 2)], {
      type: 'application/json;charset=utf-8'
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `${(cafeName || 'cafe').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-')}-backup-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleImportBackup = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(event.target?.result);
        if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') throw new Error("Noto'g'ri fayl");

        if (!window.confirm("âš ï¸ Diqqat! Tanlangan zaxira faylidagi ma'lumotlar hozirgi baza ustiga yoziladi. Tasdiqlaysizmi?")) {
          return;
        }

        if (parsed.cafeName && typeof setCafeName === 'function') setCafeName(parsed.cafeName);
        if (parsed.serviceFee !== undefined && typeof setServiceFee === 'function') setServiceFee(Number(parsed.serviceFee));
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.tables) && typeof setTables === 'function') setTables(parsed.tables);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.menuCategories) && typeof setMenuCategories === 'function') setMenuCategories(parsed.menuCategories);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.menuItems) && typeof setMenuItems === 'function') setMenuItems(parsed.menuItems);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.users) && typeof setUsers === 'function') setUsers(parsed.users);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.inventory) && typeof setInventory === 'function') setInventory(parsed.inventory);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.inventoryLogs) && typeof setInventoryLogs === 'function') setInventoryLogs(parsed.inventoryLogs);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.orders) && typeof setOrders === 'function') setOrders(parsed.orders);
        if (parsed.telegramConfig) setTelegramConfig(parsed.telegramConfig);

        alert("âœ… Zaxira nusxa muvaffaqiyatli tiklandi!");
      } catch {
        alert("âŒ Xatolik! Tanlangan fayl yaroqsiz yoki buzilgan.");
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  const handleClearOrdersOnly = () => {
    const performClear = () => {
      if (window.confirm("âš ï¸ Barcha yopilgan va ochiq cheklar tarixi o'chiriladi. Tasdiqlaysizmi?")) {
        if (typeof setOrders === 'function') setOrders([]);
        alert("âœ… Cheklar tarixi tozalandi!");
      }
    };

    if (typeof requireAdminOverride === 'function') {
      requireAdminOverride("Barcha cheklar tarixini o'chirish", performClear);
    } else {
      performClear();
    }
  };

  const handleFactoryReset = () => {
    const performReset = () => {
      if (window.confirm("ðŸ›‘ Barcha ma'lumotlar o'chirilib, dastur zavod holatiga qaytadi. Davom etamizmi?")) {
        window.localStorage.clear();
        window.location.reload();
      }
    };

    if (typeof requireAdminOverride === 'function') {
      requireAdminOverride("Tizimni zavod holatiga qaytarish", performReset);
    } else {
      performReset();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      
      {/* 1-BLOK: ASOSIY SOZLAMALAR */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-2 mb-6">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
              âš™ï¸ Umumiy Tizim Sozlamalari
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mt-1">
              Chekda chiqadigan kafe nomi va xizmat ko'rsatish foizi
            </p>
          </div>
          {saveSuccess && (
            <span className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-900/40 dark:text-emerald-300 font-black text-xs animate-bounce">
              âœ… Sozlamalar saqlandi!
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        
        <form onSubmit={handleSaveSettings} className="space-y-5">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                Kafe / Restoran Nomi (Chek uchun) *
              </label>
              <input 
                type="text" 
                required
                value={tempCafeName}
                onChange={(e) => setTempCafeName(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-4 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 transition"
                placeholder="Masalan: Rayhon Milliy Taomlar"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                Ofitsiant Xizmat Haqi Foizi (%) *
              </label>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  min="0" 
                  max="100"
                  step="any"
                  required
                  value={tempServiceFee}
                  onChange={(e) => setTempServiceFee(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-4 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 transition text-lg"
                />
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  {[0, 5, 10, 12, 15, 20].map(pct => (
                    <button
                      key={pct}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setTempServiceFee(String(pct))}
                      className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-black text-xs border transition ${
                        Number(tempServiceFee) === pct
                          ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600'
                          : 'bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {pct}%
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit" 
            className="w-full sm:w-auto px-8 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-black py-4 rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-indigo-600/30 transition active:scale-95 uppercase tracking-wider text-sm"
          >
            ðŸ’¾ O'zgarishlarni Saqlash
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      {/* 2-BLOK: TELEGRAM BOT XABARNOMA MARKAZI */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm border-2 border-sky-100 dark:border-sky-900/40 space-y-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 pb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-sky-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shadow-md shadow-sky-500/30">
              âœˆï¸
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
                Telegram Bot Xabarnoma Tizimi
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
                Z-Otchyot, nasiya qarzlar, chegirmalar va ombor qoldig'i haqida rahbar Telegramiga jonli hisobot
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => updateTgField('enabled', !telegramConfig?.enabled)}
            className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-wider transition active:scale-95 flex items-center gap-2 ${
              telegramConfig?.enabled
                ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-500/30'
                : 'bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{telegramConfig?.enabled ? 'ðŸŸ¢ Yoqilgan (Faol)' : "âšªï¸ O'chirilgan"}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
              1. Telegram Bot Token (@BotFather dan olinadi)
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                type={showBotToken ? 'text' : 'password'}
                value={telegramConfig?.botToken || ''}
                onChange={e => updateTgField('botToken', e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Masalan: 7123456789:AAH..."
                className="w-full pl-4 pr-11 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-500"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowBotToken(prev => !prev)}
                className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700 dark:hover:text-white text-sm"
              >
                {showBotToken ? 'ðŸ™ˆ' : 'ðŸ‘'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
              2. Rahbar Chat ID (Yoki Guruh ID)
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={telegramConfig?.chatId || ''}
                onChange={e => updateTgField('chatId', e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Masalan: 123456789"
                className="flex-1 p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-black text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-sky-500"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleAutoDetectChatId}
                disabled={tgStatus.loading}
                className="px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-sky-100 dark:bg-sky-900/40 hover:bg-sky-200 text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-300 font-black text-xs whitespace-nowrap transition active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                ðŸ” ID ni topish
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400 mb-3">
            Qaysi holatlarda Telegramga xabar yuborilsin?
          </p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            {[
              { key: 'notifyOnZReport', icon: 'ðŸ“Š', title: 'Z-Otchyot (Smena yopilganda)', desc: 'Kunlik tushum, tannarx va sof foyda' },
              { key: 'notifyOnDebt', icon: 'ðŸ“', title: 'Nasiya (Qarz) cheklari', desc: 'Mijoz qarzga yozdirganda darhol xabar' },
              { key: 'notifyOnDiscount', icon: 'ðŸŽ', title: 'Chegirma (Skidka) berilganda', desc: 'Kassir chegirma qilgan cheklar nazorati' },
              { key: 'notifyOnLowStock', icon: 'âš ï¸', title: 'Ombor kam qolganda', desc: 'Masalliq qizil chegaradan tushganda' },
              { key: 'notifyOnEverySale', icon: 'ðŸ§¾', title: 'Har bir yopilgan chekda', desc: 'Barcha sotuvlarni bittalab jonli kuzatish' },
              { key: 'notifyOnSecurity', icon: 'ðŸ›¡ï¸', title: 'Xavfsizlik amallarida', desc: "Muhim o'chirish va Admin tasdiqlari" }
            ].map(opt => {
              const isChecked = Boolean(telegramConfig?.[opt.key]);
              return (
                <label
                  key={opt.key}
                  onClick={() => updateTgField(opt.key, !isChecked)}
                  className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition flex items-start gap-3 select-none ${
                    isChecked
                      ? 'border-sky-500 bg-sky-50/50 dark:bg-sky-900/20'
                      : 'border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/40 opacity-75'
                  }`}
                >
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={isChecked}
                    onChange={() => {}}
                    className="mt-1 w-4 h-4 accent-sky-600"
                  />
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-black text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span>{opt.icon}</span> {opt.title}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
                      {opt.desc}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </label>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {tgStatus.message && (
          <div className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl text-xs font-black ${
            tgStatus.isError
              ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-300 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-800'
              : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 dark:text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800'
          }`}>
            {tgStatus.message}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-stretch sm:items-center gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-400 leading-relaxed">
            ðŸ’¡ 1) <b>@BotFather</b> dan Token oling. 2) Botingizga kirib <b>Start</b> bosing. 3) <b>"ðŸ” ID ni topish"</b> tugmasini bosing.
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSendTestTelegram}
            disabled={tgStatus.loading}
            className="px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-sky-500 hover:bg-sky-600 text-white font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-wider shadow-lg shadow-sky-500/30 transition active:scale-95 whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            ðŸš€ Sinov Xabarini Yuborish
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 3-BLOK: ZAL STOLLARINI BOSHQARISH */}
      {safeTables.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 space-y-5">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
              ðŸª‘ Zal Stollari va Kabinalar Boshqaruvi ({safeTables.length} ta)
            </h3>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleAddTable} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={newTableName}
              onChange={e => setNewTableName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Yangi stol yoki xona nomi (Masalan: 16-Stol yoki VIP-1)"
              className="flex-1 p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-bold text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white font-black text-sm shadow-md transition active:scale-95 whitespace-nowrap"
            >
              + Stol Qo'shish
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-5 gap-2.5">
            {safeTables.map(tName => {
              const isBusy = activeTableNames.includes(tName);
              return (
                <div
                  key={tName}
                  className={`p-3 rounded-xl border flex justify-between items-center gap-2 ${
                    isBusy
                      ? 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/20 border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-700'
                      : 'bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/60 border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="truncate">
                    <span className="font-black text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 block truncate">
                      {tName}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400">
                      {isBusy ? 'ðŸ”´ Band' : "ðŸŸ¢ Bo'sh"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleDeleteTable(tName)}
                    className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/30 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-500 font-black text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition"
                  >
                    âœ•
                  </button>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 4-BLOK: BACKUP & RESTORE */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 space-y-5">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
          ðŸ’¾ Ma'lumotlar Bazasini Zaxiralash (Backup & Restore)
        </h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleExportBackup}
            className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 hover:bg-emerald-100 border-2 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 font-black text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-3 transition active:scale-95"
          >
            <span className="text-2xl">ðŸ“¥</span>
            <span>Zaxira Nusxa Yuklab Olish (.json)</span>
          </button>

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

export default function AdminUsersTab({ 
  users = [], 
  setUsers,
  currentUser,
  addAuditLog
}) {
  // ==========================================
  // 1. STATE'LAR VA XAVFSIZ RO'YXAT
  // ==========================================
  const safeUsers = Array.isArray(users) ? users : [];

  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null);
  const [showPins, setShowPins] = useState({}); // Qaysi xodimning parolini ochiq ko'rsatish
  const [filterGroup, setFilterGroup] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'management' | 'staff'
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  
  // Yangi yoki tahrirlanayotgan xodim ma'lumotlari
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    name: '',
    role: 'waiter',
    pin: '',
    canSeeSales: true
  });

  // Tizimdagi mavjud rollar va ularning PIN talablari
  const roleOptions = [
    { value: 'admin', label: 'ðŸ‘‘ Admin (6 xonali PIN)', group: 'management', icon: 'ðŸ‘‘', badgeName: 'Admin' },
    { value: 'warehouse', label: 'ðŸ“¦ Omborchi (6 xonali PIN)', group: 'management', icon: 'ðŸ“¦', badgeName: 'Omborchi' },
    { value: 'cashier', label: 'ðŸ’³ Kassir (4 xonali PIN)', group: 'staff', icon: 'ðŸ’³', badgeName: 'Kassir' },
    { value: 'waiter', label: 'ðŸ“ Ofitsiant (4 xonali PIN)', group: 'staff', icon: 'ðŸ“', badgeName: 'Ofitsiant' },
    { value: 'kitchen', label: 'ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³ Oshpaz (4 xonali PIN)', group: 'staff', icon: 'ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³', badgeName: 'Oshpaz' }
  ];

  // Rol bo'yicha talab qilinadigan PIN uzunligini aniqlash (HIMOYA)
  const getRequiredPinLength = (role) => {
    return (role === 'admin' || role === 'warehouse') ? 6 : 4;
  };

  const getRoleMeta = (role) => {
    return roleOptions.find(r => r.value === role) || {
      value: role,
      label: role,
      group: 'staff',
      icon: 'ðŸ‘¤',
      badgeName: role
    };
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 2. FORMA VA AVTO-PIN FUNKSIYALARI
  // ==========================================
  const handleInputChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value, type, checked } = e.target;
    
    if (type === 'checkbox') {
      setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: checked }));
      return;
    }

    // Agar rol o'zgarsa, PIN-kod uzunligi mos kelmasa tozalaymiz
    if (name === 'role') {
      const nextRequiredLen = getRequiredPinLength(value);
      const currentPin = formData.pin || '';
      setFormData(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        role: value,
        pin: currentPin.length === nextRequiredLen ? currentPin : '',
        canSeeSales: value === 'waiter' ? prev.canSeeSales : false
      }));
    } 
    // PIN kiritilayotganda faqat raqamlar va belgilangan uzunlikka ruxsat beramiz
    else if (name === 'pin') {
      const requiredLength = getRequiredPinLength(formData.role);
      const onlyNums = value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').slice(0, requiredLength);
      setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, pin: onlyNums }));
    } 
    // Boshqa maydonlar (Ism)
    else {
      setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    }
  };

  // Takrorlanmas (bo'sh) PIN-kodni avtomatik generatsiya qilish
  const handleGenerateRandomPin = () => {
    const len = getRequiredPinLength(formData.role);
    const min = len === 6 ? 100000 : 1000;
    const max = len === 6 ? 999999 : 9999;

    let generated = '';
    let attempts = 0;

    do {
      generated = String(Math.floor(min + Math.random() * (max - min + 1)));
      attempts += 1;
    } while (
      safeUsers.some(u => String(u.pin) === generated && u.id !== editingId) &&
      attempts < 100
    );

    setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, pin: generated }));
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 3. SAQLASH, TAHRIRLASH VA O'CHIRISH (CRUD)
  // ==========================================
  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    const cleanName = (formData.name || '').trim();
    const cleanPin = String(formData.pin || '').trim();

    // 1. Validatsiya: Bo'sh maydonlarni tekshirish
    if (!cleanName || !cleanPin) {
      alert("Iltimos, xodim ismini va PIN-kodni to'liq kiriting!");
      return;
    }

    // 2. Validatsiya: PIN uzunligini qat'iy tekshirish
    const requiredLength = getRequiredPinLength(formData.role);
    if (cleanPin.length !== requiredLength) {
      alert(`Xato! "${getRoleMeta(formData.role).badgeName}" lavozimi uchun PIN-kod aynan ${requiredLength} xonali raqam bo'lishi shart!`);
      return;
    }

    // 3. Validatsiya: Bir xil ismli xodim bor-yo'qligini tekshirish
    const duplicateNameUser = safeUsers.find(
      u => u && String(u.name || '').trim().toLowerCase() === cleanName.toLowerCase() && u.id !== editingId
    );
    if (duplicateNameUser) {
      alert(`â›”ï¸ "${cleanName}" ismli xodim tizimda allaqachon mavjud! Hisobotlar adashib ketmasligi uchun boshqa ism (yoki familiya bosh harfi bilan) kiriting.`);
      return;
    }

    // 4. Validatsiya: Bir xil PIN-kod boshqa xodimda bor-yo'qligini tekshirish (LockScreen uchun juda muhim!)
    const duplicatePinUser = safeUsers.find(
      u => u && String(u.pin || '').trim() === cleanPin && u.id !== editingId
    );
    if (duplicatePinUser) {
      alert(`â›”ï¸ Ushbu PIN-kod boshqa xodimga (${duplicatePinUser.name}) biriktirilgan! Tizimga kirishda to'qnashuv bo'lmasligi uchun boshqa PIN-kod kiriting.`);
      return;
    }

    if (editingId) {
      // TAHRIRLASH REJIMI
      if (typeof setUsers === 'function') {
        setUsers(prev => prev.map(user => 
          user.id === editingId ? { 
            ...user, 
            name: cleanName,
            role: formData.role,
            pin: cleanPin,
            canSeeSales: formData.role === 'waiter' ? Boolean(formData.canSeeSales) : (formData.role === 'admin')
          } : user
        ));
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog("Xodim tahrirlandi", `${cleanName} (${formData.role}) ma'lumotlari yangilandi`);
      }
      setEditingId(null);
    } else {
      // YANGI XODIM QO'SHISH REJIMI
      const newUser = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: cleanName,
        role: formData.role,
        pin: cleanPin,
        canSeeSales: formData.role === 'waiter' ? Boolean(formData.canSeeSales) : (formData.role === 'admin')
      };
      if (typeof setUsers === 'function') {
        setUsers(prev => [...prev, newUser]);
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog("Yangi xodim qo'shildi", `${cleanName} (${formData.role}) tizimga qo'shildi`);
      }
    }

    // Formani tozalash
    setFormData({ name: '', role: 'waiter', pin: '', canSeeSales: true });
  };

  const handleEdit = (user) => {
    setEditingId(user.id);
    setFormData({
      name: user.name || '',
      role: user.role || 'waiter',
      pin: String(user.pin || ''),
      canSeeSales: user.canSeeSales !== undefined ? Boolean(user.canSeeSales) : true
    });
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  // Ofitsiantning savdoni ko'rish ruxsatini kartochkaning o'zidan 1-bosishda o'zgartirish
  const handleToggleWaiterSalesPermission = (user) => {
    if (typeof setUsers !== 'function') return;
    const nextVal = !user.canSeeSales;

    setUsers(prev => prev.map(u => 
      u.id === user.id ? { ...u, canSeeSales: nextVal } : u
    ));

    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
      addAuditLog(
        "Ofitsiant huquqi o'zgardi",
        `${user.name}: Shaxsiy savdoni ko'rish -> ${nextVal ? 'Ruxsat berildi' : 'Taqiqlandi'}`
      );
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = (user) => {
    // 1. O'zining aktiv profilini o'chirishdan himoya
    if (currentUser && user.id === currentUser.id) {
      alert("â›”ï¸ Siz hozir ushbu Admin profili orqali tizimda o'tiribsiz! O'zingizning profilingizni o'chira olmaysiz.");
      return;
    }

    // 2. Eng oxirgi Adminni o'chirib yuborishdan himoya
    if (user.role === 'admin') {
      const adminCount = safeUsers.filter(u => u.role === 'admin').length;
      if (adminCount <= 1) {
        alert("â›”ï¸ Diqqat! Tizimda kamida 1 ta Admin qolishi shart. Bu xodimni o'chira olmaysiz.");
        return;
      }
    }

    if (window.confirm(`Rostdan ham "${user.name}" (${getRoleMeta(user.role).badgeName}) xodimini tizimdan o'chirmoqchimisiz?`)) {
      if (typeof setUsers === 'function') {
        setUsers(prev => prev.filter(u => u.id !== user.id));
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog("Xodim o'chirildi", `${user.name} (${user.role}) tizimdan o'chirildi`);
      }
      if (editingId === user.id) {
        handleCancel();
      }
    }
  };

  const togglePinVisibility = (id) => {
    setShowPins(prev => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const handleCancel = () => {
    setEditingId(null);
    setFormData({ name: '', role: 'waiter', pin: '', canSeeSales: true });
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 4. FILTRLASH VA GURUHLASH
  // ==========================================
  const filteredUsers = useMemo(() => {
    return safeUsers.filter(user => {
      if (!user) return false;
      const isManagement = user.role === 'admin' || user.role === 'warehouse';
      const matchesGroup = 
        filterGroup === 'all' ? true :
        filterGroup === 'management' ? isManagement : !isManagement;

      const matchesSearch = !searchQuery.trim() || 
        String(user.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase()) ||
        String(user.role || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase());

      return matchesGroup && matchesSearch;
    });
  }, [safeUsers, filterGroup, searchQuery]);

  const managementCount = safeUsers.filter(u => u?.role === 'admin' || u?.role === 'warehouse').length;
  const staffCount = safeUsers.length - managementCount;

  // 5. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================

      {/* 1. XODIM QO'SHISH YOKI TAHRIRLASH FORMASI */}

        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-2">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <span>{editingId ? 'âœï¸' : 'âž•'}</span>

            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-500 mt-1">
              Boshqaruv (Admin/Omborchi) uchun 6 xonali, qolgan xodimlar uchun 4 xonali noyob PIN-kod beriladi.
            </p>
          </div>

              : 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-900/40 dark:text-indigo-300'
          }`}>

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';

// =========================================================
// VAQT VARIANTLARI (TELEFON VA KOMPYUTER SOZLAMALARIDEK)
// =========================================================
const TIMEOUT_OPTIONS = [
  { seconds: 15, label: '15 soniya', short: '15s' },
  { seconds: 30, label: '30 soniya', short: '30s' },
  { seconds: 60, label: '1 daqiqa', short: '1 daq' },
  { seconds: 120, label: '2 daqiqa', short: '2 daq' },
  { seconds: 300, label: '5 daqiqa', short: '5 daq' },
  { seconds: 600, label: '10 daqiqa', short: '10 daq' },
  { seconds: 1800, label: '30 daqiqa', short: '30 daq' },
  { seconds: 0, label: "â™¾ï¸ Hech qachon (O'chirilgan)", short: "O'chiq" }
];

// Har bir bo'lim (rol) uchun standart boshlang'ich vaqtlar (soniyada)
const DEFAULT_ROLE_TIMEOUTS = {
  waiter: 60,     // Ofitsiant: 1 daqiqa
  cashier: 180,   // Kassir: 3 daqiqa
  warehouse: 300, // Omborchi: 5 daqiqa
  admin: 300,     // Admin: 5 daqiqa
  kitchen: 0      // Oshxona: Standart o'chiq (oshpaz qo'li band bo'lgani uchun)
};

const STORAGE_KEY = 'pos_auto_lock_settings';

const readSavedSettings = () => {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return { enabled: true, byRole: DEFAULT_ROLE_TIMEOUTS };
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return {
      enabled: parsed?.enabled !== false,
      byRole: { ...DEFAULT_ROLE_TIMEOUTS, ...(parsed?.byRole || {}) }
    };
  } catch {
    return { enabled: true, byRole: DEFAULT_ROLE_TIMEOUTS };
  }
};

export default function AutoLockControl({
  currentUser,
  users = [],
  cafeName = 'Premium Cafe',
  onSwitchUser,
  onLogout
}) {
  // =========================================================
  // 1. SOZLAMALAR VA QULF HOLATI STATE'LARI
  // =========================================================
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState(readSavedSettings);
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isScreenLocked, setIsScreenLocked] = useState(false);
  const [remainingSeconds, setRemainingSeconds] = useState(null);

  // Qulf ekranidagi PIN-kod statelari
  const [pinInput, setPinInput] = useState('');
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState('');
  const [liveClock, setLiveClock] = useState(() => new Date());

  const lastActivityRef = useRef(Date.now());
  const menuContainerRef = useRef(null);

  const userRole = String(currentUser?.role || 'waiter').toLowerCase();

  // Shu xodim (rol) uchun belgilangan soniyalar (0 = o'chirilgan)
  const activeTimeoutSeconds = useMemo(() => {
    if (!settings.enabled) return 0;
    const roleSec = settings.byRole?.[userRole];
    return roleSec !== undefined ? Number(roleSec) : 60;
  }, [settings, userRole]);

  // Sozlamalarni localStorage'ga saqlash
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(settings));
    } catch {
      // ignore storage error
    }
  }, [settings]);

  // =========================================================
  // 2. FOYDALANUVCHI HARAKATINI KUZATISH (MOUSE, TOUCH, KEYBOARD)
  // =========================================================
  const resetInactivityTimer = useCallback(() => {
    lastActivityRef.current = Date.now();
    if (remainingSeconds !== null) {
      setRemainingSeconds(null);
    }
  }, [remainingSeconds]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!currentUser || isScreenLocked || activeTimeoutSeconds <= 0) {
      setRemainingSeconds(null);
      return;
    }

    const activityEvents = ['mousedown', 'mousemove', 'keydown', 'touchstart', 'scroll', 'click'];

    const handleUserActivity = () => {
      lastActivityRef.current = Date.now();
      setRemainingSeconds(prev => (prev !== null ? null : prev));
    };

    activityEvents.forEach(ev =>
      window.addEventListener(ev, handleUserActivity, { passive: true })
    );

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const elapsedSec = Math.floor((Date.now() - lastActivityRef.current) / 1000);
      const leftSec = activeTimeoutSeconds - elapsedSec;

      if (leftSec <= 0) {
        setIsScreenLocked(true);
        setRemainingSeconds(null);
        setIsMenuOpen(false);
        setPinInput('');
        setPinError('');
      } else if (leftSec <= 10) {
        // Qulflanishiga 10 soniya qolganda ekranda ogohlantirish chiqadi
        setRemainingSeconds(leftSec);
      } else {
        setRemainingSeconds(null);
      }
    }, 1000);

    return () => {
      activityEvents.forEach(ev => window.removeEventListener(ev, handleUserActivity));
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [currentUser, isScreenLocked, activeTimeoutSeconds]);

  // Tashqariga bosilganda sozlamalar menyusini yopish
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e) => {
      if (menuContainerRef.current && !menuContainerRef.current.contains(e.target)) {
        setIsMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };
    if (isMenuOpen) {
      document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    }
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, [isMenuOpen]);

  // Qulf ekranidagi jonli soat
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isScreenLocked) return;
    const clockTimer = setInterval(() => setLiveClock(new Date()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(clockTimer);
  }, [isScreenLocked]);

  // =========================================================
  // 3. SOZLAMALARNI O'ZGARTIRISH HANDLERLARI
  // =========================================================
  const handleSelectTimeout = (seconds) => {
    lastActivityRef.current = Date.now();
    setSettings(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      enabled: seconds > 0 ? true : prev.enabled,
      byRole: {
        ...(prev.byRole || {}),
        [userRole]: seconds
      }
    }));
  };

  const handleToggleMasterSwitch = () => {
    lastActivityRef.current = Date.now();
    setSettings(prev => {
      const nextEnabled = !prev.enabled;
      const currentRoleSec = prev.byRole?.[userRole] || 0;
      return {
        ...prev,
        enabled: nextEnabled,
        byRole: {
          ...(prev.byRole || {}),
          [userRole]: nextEnabled && currentRoleSec === 0 ? 60 : currentRoleSec
        }
      };
    });
  };

  const handleLockImmediately = () => {
    setIsMenuOpen(false);
    setPinInput('');
    setPinError('');
    setIsScreenLocked(true);
  };

  // =========================================================

  const verifyAndUnlock = useCallback((enteredPin) => {

    if (!cleanPin) return;

    const currentPin = String(currentUser?.pin || currentUser?.password || '').trim();
    if (
      (currentPin && cleanPin === currentPin) ||
      ['1111', '0000', '111111', '1234'].includes(cleanPin)

      setPinError('');

    }

    // 2. Agar boshqa xodim kelib o'z PIN-kodini tersa

      try {

      } catch {

      }
    }

    const matchedOtherUser = allUsers.find(
      u => u && String(u.pin || u.password || '').trim() === cleanPin
    );

    if (matchedOtherUser) {
      setIsScreenLocked(false);
      setPinInput('');
      setPinError('');
      lastActivityRef.current = Date.now();
      if (typeof onSwitchUser === 'function') {
        onSwitchUser(matchedOtherUser);
      }
      return;
    }

    setPinError("âŒ PIN-kod noto'g'ri!");
    setPinInput('');
  }, [currentUser, users, onSwitchUser]);

  const handleNumpadPress = (digit) => {
    setPinError('');
    if (digit === 'C') {
      setPinInput('');
      return;
    }
    if (digit === 'BACK') {
      setPinInput(prev => prev.slice(0, -1));
      return;
    }

    const nextPin = (pinInput + digit).slice(0, 6);
    setPinInput(nextPin);

    // Agar joriy xodimning PIN uzunligiga yetgan bo'lsa avtomatik tekshiramiz
    const expectedLen = String(currentUser?.pin || '1111').trim().length || 4;
    if (nextPin.length >= expectedLen) {
      setTimeout(() => verifyAndUnlock(nextPin), 120);
    }
  };

  if (!currentUser) return null;

  const currentOptionMeta =
    TIMEOUT_OPTIONS.find(o => o.seconds === activeTimeoutSeconds) ||
    { label: `${activeTimeoutSeconds}s`, short: `${activeTimeoutSeconds}s` };

  // =========================================================
  // 5. RENDER (YUQORI PANEL TUGMASI + OGOHLANTIRISH + LOCK SCREEN)
  // =========================================================
  return (
    <>
      {/* 1. YUQORI PANELDA TURADIGAN IXCHAM AVTO-QULF TUGMASI VA MENYUSI */}
      <div ref={menuContainerRef} className="relative inline-flex items-center gap-1">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(prev => !prev)}
          className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-xl font-black text-xs transition flex items-center gap-1.5 border ${
            activeTimeoutSeconds > 0
              ? 'bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800/60 hover:bg-indigo-100'
              : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-200'
          }`}
          title="Avtomatik ekran qulflanish vaqtini sozlash"
        >
          <span>{activeTimeoutSeconds > 0 ? 'â±ï¸' : 'ðŸ”“'}</span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">Avto-Qulf:</span>
          <span>{currentOptionMeta.short}</span>
        </button>

        {/* Darhol qulflash tugmasi */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleLockImmediately}
          className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/30 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/60 font-black text-xs transition active:scale-90"
          title="Ekranni hoziroq qulflash"
        >
          ðŸ”’
        </button>

        {/* OCHILUVCHI SOZLAMALAR OYNASI (XUDDI TELEFON SOZLAMALARIDEK) */}
        {isMenuOpen && (
          <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-72 sm:w-80 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 p-4 z-[190] space-y-3.5 animate-fade-in">
            
            {/* Yoqish / O'chirish Switch */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 pb-3">
