                const ordItems = Array.isArray(ord.items) ? ord.items : [];
                const ordSubtotal = ordItems.reduce(
                  (s, it) => s + (Number(it.price || 0) * Number(it.quantity || it.qty || 1)),
                  0
                );
                const ordService = Math.round((ordSubtotal * serviceFeePercent) / 100);
                const ordTotal = ordSubtotal + ordService;

                return (
                  <div
                    key={ord.id || idx}
                    className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 space-y-4"
                  >
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 pb-3">
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">
                          Buyurtma #{String(ord.id).slice(-4)}
                        </span>
                        <h4 className="font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white text-base">
                          {ord.table || activeTable} {ord.time ? `â€¢ ${ord.time}` : ''}
                        </h4>
                      </div>

                      <span className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-black uppercase ${
                        ord.status === 'Tayyor'
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white animate-pulse'
                          : ord.status === 'Yetkazildi'
                          ? 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-900/40 dark:text-indigo-300'
                          : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/40 dark:text-amber-300'
                      }`}>
                        {ord.status === 'Tayyor' 
                          ? 'ðŸ¥˜ Tayyor!' 
                          : ord.status === 'Yetkazildi' 
                          ? 'âœ… Yetkazildi' 
                          : 'â³ Oshxonada tayyorlanmoqda'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      {ordItems.map((it, i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="flex justify-between items-start text-sm">
                          <div>
                            <span className="font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                              {it.name}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-xs font-black text-amber-600 ml-2">
                              x{it.quantity || it.qty || 1}
                            </span>
                            {it.note && (
                              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-500">
                                ðŸ’¬ {it.note}
                              </p>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <span className="font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
                            {(Number(it.price || 0) * Number(it.quantity || it.qty || 1)).toLocaleString()} s
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>

                    <div className="border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 pt-3 space-y-1 text-xs font-bold">
                      {serviceFeePercent > 0 && (
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-gray-400">
                          <span>Xizmat ko'rsatish ({serviceFeePercent}%):</span>
                          <span>{ordService.toLocaleString()} so'm</span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-base font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 pt-1">
                        <span>Jami hisob:</span>
                        <span>{ordTotal.toLocaleString()} so'm</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('menu')}
                className="w-full py-4 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-black text-sm uppercase tracking-wider shadow-lg shadow-indigo-600/30 transition active:scale-95"
              >
                + Yana qo'shimcha buyurtma berish
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </main>
      )}

      {/* --- SUZUVCHI OFITSIANT CHAQIRISH TUGMASI (FAB) --- */}
      <button 
        type="button"
        onClick={handleCallWaiterClick}
        disabled={isWaiterCalled}
        title="Ofitsiantni chaqirish"
        className={`fixed bottom-[95px] right-4 w-14 h-14 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-xl transition z-30 flex-col ${
          isWaiterCalled
            ? 'bg-gray-400 text-white cursor-not-allowed'
            : 'bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white shadow-indigo-600/40 active:scale-90'
        }`}
      >
        <span className="text-xl">ðŸ””</span>
        {isWaiterCalled && (
          <span className="text-[9px] font-black">{cooldownTime}s</span>
        )}
      </button>

      {/* --- PASTKI SAVATCHA BAR --- */}
      {cart.length > 0 && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full bg-white/95 dark:bg-gray-800/95 backdrop-blur-md shadow-[0_-4px_20px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] p-3.5 sm:p-4 z-40 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto">
            <button 
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsCartModalOpen(true)}
              className="w-full bg-emerald-500 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white py-4 rounded-2xl font-black text-base sm:text-lg shadow-lg shadow-emerald-500/30 transition active:scale-95 flex justify-between items-center px-5"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <span className="text-2xl">ðŸ›’</span>
                <span>Savatchani ko'rish</span>
                <span className="bg-white text-emerald-600 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs sm:text-sm font-black">
                  {totalCartItems} ta
                </span>
              </div>
              <span>{totalCartAmount.toLocaleString()} so'm</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =======================================================
          MODAL 1: TAOM QO'SHISH, PORSIYA VA IZOH TANLASH
          ======================================================= */}
      {selectedItemForAdd && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center sm:p-4 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-5 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 pb-4">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-black text-xl sm:text-2xl text-gray-900 dark:text-white">

                </h3>
                <p className="font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-500 text-lg sm:text-xl mt-1">
                  {currentModalUnitPrice.toLocaleString()} so'm
                </p>
              </div>
              <button 
                type="button"
                onClick={handleCloseAddModal} 
                className="w-10 h-10 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 font-black text-xl active:scale-90 transition"

                âœ•
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Porsiya (Variant) tanlash */}
            {activeVariants.length > 1 && (
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-2 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Porsiyani tanlang:
                </label>

                  {activeVariants.map((variant, idx) => (
                    <button
                      key={idx}
                      type="button"

                          : 'border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300'

                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Miqdor tanlash */}
            <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-6 py-1">
              <button 
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setAddQty(Math.max(1, addQty - 1))}

              >
                -
              </button>
              
              <span className="text-4xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white w-12 text-center">
                {addQty}
              </span>
              
              <button 
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setAddQty(addQty + 1)}
                className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-900/30 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl font-black text-amber-600 active:scale-90 transition"
              >
                +
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Tezkor izoh chiplari + Maxsus izoh */}
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Oshpazga izoh (Ixtiyoriy)
              </label>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {QUICK_NOTE_TAGS.map(tag => {
                  const isSelected = addNote.toLowerCase().includes(tag.toLowerCase());
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={tag}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleToggleQuickNote(tag)}
                      className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                          : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isSelected ? `âœ“ ${tag}` : `+ ${tag}`}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              <input 
                type="text" 
                value={addNote}
                onChange={e => setAddNote(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Yoki o'zingiz izoh yozing..."
                className="w-full p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600 outline-none focus:border-amber-500 text-gray-900 dark:text-white text-sm font-bold"
              />
            </div>

            <button 
              type="button"
              onClick={handleAddToCart}
              className="w-full bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white py-4 rounded-2xl font-black text-lg shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/30 active:scale-95 transition flex justify-between items-center px-6"
            >
              <span>Savatga qo'shish</span>
              <span>{currentModalTotalPrice.toLocaleString()} so'm</span>
            </button>
            
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =======================================================
          MODAL 2: SAVATNI KO'RISH VA BUYURTMANI TASDIQLASH
          ======================================================= */}
      {isCartModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center sm:p-4 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl shadow-2xl flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
            
            <div className="p-5 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center shrink-0">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-black text-xl text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
                  ðŸ›’ Sizning Savatingiz
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">{activeTable}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleClearCart}
                  className="text-xs font-black text-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/30 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl"
                >
                  Tozalash
                </button>
                <button 
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsCartModalOpen(false)} 
                  className="w-9 h-9 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 font-black text-lg active:scale-90 transition"
                >
                  âœ•
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-3 hide-scrollbar">
              {cart.map(item => (
                <div 
                  key={item.cartItemId} 
                  className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-600"
                >
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                    <div className="pr-3">
                      <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white text-base leading-tight">
                        {item.name}
                      </h4>
                      <p className="text-sm font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 mt-1">
                        {(Number(item.price || 0) * Number(item.quantity || 1)).toLocaleString()} so'm
                      </p>
                      {item.note && (
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-500 mt-1.5 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg inline-block">
                          ðŸ’¬ {item.note}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    
                    <button 
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleRemoveFromCart(item.cartItemId)}
                      className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 font-black text-lg p-1"
                    >
                      ðŸ—‘
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-1.5 rounded-xl w-max shadow-sm border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
                    <button 
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleUpdateCartQty(item.cartItemId, -1)}
                      className="w-9 h-9 flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded-lg font-black text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 active:scale-90 transition"
                    >
                      -
                    </button>
                    
                    <span className="w-7 text-center font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white text-base">
                      {item.quantity}
                    </span>
                    
                    <button 
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleUpdateCartQty(item.cartItemId, 1)}
                      className="w-9 h-9 flex items-center justify-center bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-600 rounded-lg font-black active:scale-90 transition"
                    >
                      +
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';

const QUICK_NOTE_TAGS = [
  'Piyozsiz',
  'Achchiq bo\'lmasin',
  'Sous alohida',
  'Muzsiz',
  'Issiqroq bo\'lsin',
  'Tuzi kamroq'
];

export default function CustomerQRView({ 
  cafeName = 'Restoran Menyusi', 
  table = '1-Stol', 
  menuItems = [], 
  menuCategories = [], 
  orders = [],
  serviceFee = 0,
  isDarkMode = false, 
  onSubmitOrder, 
  onCallWaiter, 
  onExitGuest 
}) {
  // ==========================================
  // 1. XAVFSIZ MA'LUMOTLARNI TAYYORLASH
  // ==========================================
  const safeMenuItems = Array.isArray(menuItems) ? menuItems : [];
  const safeOrders = Array.isArray(orders) ? orders : [];

  const categories = useMemo(() => {
    const rawCats = Array.isArray(menuCategories) && menuCategories.length > 0
      ? menuCategories
      : safeMenuItems.map(item => item?.category);
    const uniqueCats = [...new Set(rawCats.filter(Boolean))];
    return ['Barchasi', ...uniqueCats];
  }, [menuCategories, safeMenuItems]);

  // ==========================================
  // 2. STATE'LAR (Holatni boshqarish)
  // ==========================================
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('menu'); // 'menu' | 'my_orders'
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('Barchasi');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  // Savatchani sahifa yangilanganda yo'qolmasligi uchun localStorage bilan bog'laymiz
  const cartStorageKey = `qr_draft_cart_${table}`;
  const [cart, setCart] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(cartStorageKey);
      return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  });

  // Modallar va Bildirishnomalar
  const [isCartModalOpen, setIsCartModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedItemForAdd, setSelectedItemForAdd] = useState(null);
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState(null);

  // Taom qo'shish modalining ichki statelari
  const [selectedVariantIdx, setSelectedVariantIdx] = useState(0);
  const [addQty, setAddQty] = useState(1);
  const [addNote, setAddNote] = useState('');

  // Mijoz ushbu sessiyada yuborgan buyurtmalar tarixi (agar App.jsx orders prop bermasa zaxira sifatida)
  const [localSubmittedOrders, setLocalSubmittedOrders] = useState([]);

  // Ofitsiant chaqirish Anti-Spam taymeri
  const [isWaiterCalled, setIsWaiterCalled] = useState(false);
  const [cooldownTime, setCooldownTime] = useState(0);

  // Savatchani xotirada saqlab borish
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(cartStorageKey, JSON.stringify(cart));
    } catch {
      // Xotira to'lgan bo'lsa xato bermasligi uchun
    }
  }, [cart, cartStorageKey]);

  // Qo'ng'iroqcha taymerini xavfsiz boshqarish (Memory Leak'siz)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isWaiterCalled || cooldownTime <= 0) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setCooldownTime(prev => {
        if (prev <= 1) {
          setIsWaiterCalled(false);
          clearInterval(timer);
          return 0;
        }
        return prev - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isWaiterCalled, cooldownTime]);

  // ==========================================
  // 3. YORDAMCHI FUNKSIYALAR
  // ==========================================
  const showToast = (msg) => {
    setToastMessage(msg);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToastMessage(prev => (prev === msg ? null : prev));
    }, 3500);
  };

  const getItemVariants = (item) => {
    if (Array.isArray(item?.variants) && item.variants.length > 0) {
      return item.variants.map(v => ({
        name: v?.name || 'Standart',
        price: Number(v?.price || 0)
      }));
    }
    return [{ name: 'Standart', price: Number(item?.price || 0) }];
  };

  // Filtrlangan menyu (Faqat aktiv taomlar va qidiruvga moslari)
  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    return safeMenuItems.filter(item => {
      if (!item || item.is_available === false || item.available === false) {
        return false;
      }
      if (searchQuery.trim() !== '') {
        return item.name?.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase());
      }
      if (activeCategory === 'Barchasi') {
        return true;
      }
      return item.category === activeCategory;
    });
  }, [safeMenuItems, activeCategory, searchQuery]);

  // Shu stolga tegishli faol buyurtmalar (App.jsx orders'dan yoki lokal tarixdan)
  const myActiveOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const globalTableOrders = safeOrders.filter(
      o => o && o.table === table && o.status !== "To'langan" && o.status !== 'Qarz'
    );
    return globalTableOrders.length > 0 ? globalTableOrders : localSubmittedOrders;
  }, [safeOrders, table, localSubmittedOrders]);

  // ==========================================
  // 4. SAVAT (CART) MANTIG'I (SMART MERGE BILAN)
  // ==========================================
  const handleOpenAddModal = (item) => {
    setSelectedItemForAdd(item);
    setSelectedVariantIdx(0);
    setAddQty(1);
    setAddNote('');
  };

  const handleCloseAddModal = () => {
    setSelectedItemForAdd(null);
    setSelectedVariantIdx(0);
    setAddQty(1);
    setAddNote('');
  };

  const handleToggleQuickNote = (tag) => {
    setAddNote(prev => {
      const current = prev.trim();
      if (current.toLowerCase().includes(tag.toLowerCase())) {
        return current
          .replace(new RegExp(`${tag},?\\s*`, 'i'), '')
          .trim();
      }
      return current ? `${current}, ${tag}` : tag;
    });
  };

  const handleAddToCart = () => {
    if (!selectedItemForAdd) return;

    const variants = getItemVariants(selectedItemForAdd);
    const chosenVariant = variants[selectedVariantIdx] || variants[0];
    const unitPrice = Number(chosenVariant?.price || 0);
    const cleanNote = addNote.trim();
    const qtyToAdd = Math.max(1, Number(addQty || 1));

    const displayName = variants.length > 1
      ? `${selectedItemForAdd.name} (${chosenVariant.name})`
      : selectedItemForAdd.name;

    // Bir xil taom, bir xil porsiya va bir xil izoh bo'lsa, ularni birlashtiramiz
    const mergeKey = `${selectedItemForAdd.id}_${chosenVariant.name}_${cleanNote.toLowerCase()}`;

    setCart(prev => {
      const existingIndex = prev.findIndex(i => i.mergeKey === mergeKey);
      if (existingIndex > -1) {
        const updated = [...prev];
        const newQuantity = Number(updated[existingIndex].quantity || 1) + qtyToAdd;
        updated[existingIndex] = {
          ...updated[existingIndex],
          quantity: newQuantity,
          qty: newQuantity
        };
        return updated;
      }

      return [
        ...prev,
        {
          cartItemId: `${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
          mergeKey,
          id: selectedItemForAdd.id,
          name: displayName,
          baseName: selectedItemForAdd.name,
          variantName: chosenVariant.name,
          price: unitPrice,
          quantity: qtyToAdd,
          qty: qtyToAdd,
          note: cleanNote,
          category: selectedItemForAdd.category
        }
      ];
    });

    showToast(`âœ… "${displayName}" savatga qo'shildi`);
    handleCloseAddModal();
  };

  const handleUpdateCartQty = (cartItemId, delta) => {
    setCart(prev =>
      prev
        .map(item => {
          if (item.cartItemId === cartItemId) {
            const newQty = Number(item.quantity || 1) + delta;
            return newQty > 0 ? { ...item, quantity: newQty, qty: newQty } : null;
          }
          return item;
        })
        .filter(Boolean)
    );
  };

  const handleRemoveFromCart = (cartItemId) => {
    setCart(prev => prev.filter(item => item.cartItemId !== cartItemId));
  };

  const handleClearCart = () => {
    setCart([]);
    setIsCartModalOpen(false);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 5. BUYURTMANI YUBORISH VA OFITSIANT CHAQIRISH
  // ==========================================
  const totalCartAmount = useMemo(() => {
    return cart.reduce(
      (sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.price || 0) * Number(item.quantity || 1)),
      0
    );
  }, [cart]);

  const totalCartItems = useMemo(() => {
    return cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.quantity || 1), 0);
  }, [cart]);

  const serviceFeePercent = Number(serviceFee || 0);
  const cartServiceFee = Math.round((totalCartAmount * serviceFeePercent) / 100);
  const cartGrandTotal = totalCartAmount + cartServiceFee;

  const handleSubmitOrder = () => {
    if (cart.length === 0) return;

    if (typeof onSubmitOrder === 'function') {
      // App.jsx dagi funksiya (cart) yoki (table, cart) kutishiga moslashuvchan chaqiruv
      onSubmitOrder(cart, table);
    }

    // Lokal buyurtmalar tarixiga ham yozib qo'yamiz
    const newSubmittedOrder = {
      id: Date.now(),
      table,
      status: 'Yangi',
      time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
      items: [...cart],
      subtotal: totalCartAmount,
      total: cartGrandTotal
    };
    setLocalSubmittedOrders(prev => [newSubmittedOrder, ...prev]);

    setCart([]);
    localStorage.removeItem(cartStorageKey);
    setIsCartModalOpen(false);
    setActiveTab('my_orders');
    showToast("ðŸš€ Buyurtmangiz oshxonaga yuborildi!");
  };

  const handleCallWaiterClick = () => {
    if (isWaiterCalled) return;

    if (typeof onCallWaiter === 'function') {
      onCallWaiter(table);
    }

    setIsWaiterCalled(true);
    setCooldownTime(60);
    showToast("ðŸ”” Ofitsiant chaqirildi! Tez orada stolingizga keladi.");
  };

  // 6. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  const activeVariants = selectedItemForAdd ? getItemVariants(selectedItemForAdd) : [];
  const currentModalUnitPrice = Number(activeVariants[selectedVariantIdx]?.price || 0);
  const currentModalTotalPrice = currentModalUnitPrice * addQty;

  return (

      {toastMessage && (
        <div className="fixed top-20 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-[100] bg-gray-900/95 dark:bg-white/95 text-white dark:text-gray-900 px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl font-black text-xs sm:text-sm flex items-center gap-2 animate-bounce">
          <span>{toastMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* --- 6.1. HEADER (YUQORI PANEL) --- */}

          <div>
            <h1 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-500 leading-tight">
              {cafeName}
            </h1>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-black bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/40 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">
                ðŸª‘ {table}

                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-400">
                  â€¢ Xizmat: {serviceFeePercent}%

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';

// =========================================================
// STANDART SLA SOZLAMALARI (DAQIQALARDA)
// =========================================================
const DEFAULT_SLA_CONFIG = {
  warningMinutes: 10,  // 10 daqiqadan keyin SARIQ (Shoshiling)
  criticalMinutes: 20, // 20 daqiqadan keyin QIZIL + TREVOGA (Kechikdi)
  soundEnabled: true,  // Ovozli signalizatsiya
  autoSortByWait: true // Eng ko'p kutib qolgan buyurtmalarni tepaga chiqarish
};

const readLocalJSON = (key, fallback) => {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
};

const writeLocalJSON = (key, value) => {
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
  } catch {
    // ignore storage errors
  }
};

// =========================================================
// WEB AUDIO API OVOZ GENERATORI (MP3 FAYLLAR KERAK EMAS)
// =========================================================
const playKitchenSound = (type = 'new_order') => {
  try {
    const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AudioCtx) return;
    const ctx = new AudioCtx();

    if (type === 'new_order') {
      // Yoqimli 2 tonli "Ding-Dong" (Yangi buyurtma tushganda)
      const now = ctx.currentTime;
      const osc1 = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain1 = ctx.createGain();
      osc1.type = 'sine';
      osc1.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, now); // E5
      osc1.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, now + 0.18); // A5
      gain1.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, now);
      gain1.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.65);
      osc1.connect(gain1);
      gain1.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc1.start(now);
      osc1.stop(now + 0.65);
    } else if (type === 'sla_breach') {
      // Kechikish trevogasi (3 marta tezkor ogohlantiruvchi signal)
      [0, 0.22, 0.44].forEach((offset) => {
        const now = ctx.currentTime + offset;
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = ctx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'triangle';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(920, now);
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(520, now + 0.1);
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, now);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.18);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(ctx.destination);
        osc.start(now);
        osc.stop(now + 0.18);
      });
    }
  } catch {
    // Brauzer avto-ovozni bloklagan bo'lsa xato bermaydi
  }
};

export default function KitchenMode({
  orders,
  setOrders,
  menuItems,
  setMenuItems,
  menuCategories,
  currentUser,
  addAuditLog
}) {
  // =========================================================
  // 1. ZAXIRA XOTIRA VA PROP HIMOYASI
  // =========================================================
  const [localOrders, setLocalOrders] = useState(() => readLocalJSON('pos_orders', []));
  const [localMenu, setLocalMenu] = useState(() => readLocalJSON('pos_menu', []));
  const [localCategories] = useState(() => readLocalJSON('pos_categories', ['Asosiy', 'Ichimlik', 'Shirinlik']));

  // Har bir buyurtmaning boshlangan vaqtini saqlovchi xarita (Refreshda ham yo'qolmaydi)
  const [orderStartTimes, setOrderStartTimes] = useState(() => readLocalJSON('pos_kds_start_times', {}));
  // Chek ichidagi alohida pishgan taomlarni belgilash xaritasi
  const [checkedSubItems, setCheckedSubItems] = useState(() => readLocalJSON('pos_kds_checked_items', {}));
  // Ovozli trevogasi o'chirilgan (mute qilingan) buyurtmalar
  const [mutedOrderIds, setMutedOrderIds] = useState([]);
  // SLA sozlamalari
  const [slaConfig, setSlaConfig] = useState(() => ({
    ...DEFAULT_SLA_CONFIG,
    ...(readLocalJSON('pos_kds_sla_config', {}) || {})
  }));
  // Kunlik SLA statistikasi (Nechta vaqtida, nechta kechikib chiqdi)
  const [slaHistory, setSlaHistory] = useState(() =>
    readLocalJSON('pos_kds_sla_history', { onTimeCount: 0, delayedCount: 0, totalCookSeconds: 0 })
  );

  // UI Statelari
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('orders'); // 'orders' | 'stoplist'
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'delayed' | 'new' | 'cooking'
  const [stationCategory, setStationCategory] = useState('Barchasi'); // Sex filtri
  const [stopSearch, setStopSearch] = useState('');
  const [stopCategoryFilter, setStopCategoryFilter] = useState('Barchasi');
  const [isSlaModalOpen, setIsSlaModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [nowMs, setNowMs] = useState(() => Date.now());

  // Oldingi buyurtmalar sonini eslab qolish (yangi buyurtma tushganda ovoz chiqarish uchun)
  const prevOrderIdsRef = useRef(null);
  const lastAlertedBreachIdsRef = useRef(new Set());

  const safeOrders = Array.isArray(orders) ? orders : localOrders;
  const safeMenuItems = Array.isArray(menuItems) ? menuItems : localMenu;
  const safeCategories = Array.isArray(menuCategories) && menuCategories.length > 0
    ? menuCategories
    : localCategories;

  const updateOrdersState = useCallback((updater) => {
    if (typeof setOrders === 'function') {
      setOrders(updater);
    } else {
      setLocalOrders(prev => {
        const next = typeof updater === 'function' ? updater(prev) : updater;
        writeLocalJSON('pos_orders', next);
        return next;
      });
    }
  }, [setOrders]);

  const updateMenuState = useCallback((updater) => {
    if (typeof setMenuItems === 'function') {
      setMenuItems(updater);
    } else {
      setLocalMenu(prev => {
        const next = typeof updater === 'function' ? updater(prev) : updater;
        writeLocalJSON('pos_menu', next);
        return next;
      });
    }
  }, [setMenuItems]);

  // =========================================================
  // 2. JONLI SEKUNDOMER (HAR 1 SONIYADA YANGILANADI)
  // =========================================================
  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setNowMs(Date.now());
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    writeLocalJSON('pos_kds_sla_config', slaConfig);
  }, [slaConfig]);

  useEffect(() => {
    writeLocalJSON('pos_kds_checked_items', checkedSubItems);
  }, [checkedSubItems]);

  useEffect(() => {
    writeLocalJSON('pos_kds_sla_history', slaHistory);
  }, [slaHistory]);

  // =========================================================
  // 3. BUYURTMANING ANIQ BOSHLANGAN VAQTINI ANIQLASH
  // =========================================================
  const getOrderStartMs = useCallback((order) => {
    if (!order) return nowMs;
    const idKey = String(order.id);

    // 1. Agar order ichida tayyor timestamp bo'lsa
    if (order.createdAt && !isNaN(Number(order.createdAt))) {
      return Number(order.createdAt);
    }
    if (order.startTimeMs && !isNaN(Number(order.startTimeMs))) {
      return Number(order.startTimeMs);
    }

    // 2. Agar orderStartTimes xotirasida saqlangan bo'lsa
    if (orderStartTimes[idKey]) {
      return Number(orderStartTimes[idKey]);
    }

    // 3. Agar order.id ning o'zi 13 xonali timestamp bo'lsa (Masalan: 1729158000000 yoki ord_1729158000000)
    const idDigits = String(order.id).match(/\d{13}/);
    if (idDigits) {
      const parsedIdMs = Number(idDigits[0]);
      if (parsedIdMs > 1700000000000 && parsedIdMs <= Date.now() + 60000) {
        return parsedIdMs;
      }
    }

    // 4. Agar order.time ("14:35") bo'lsa, bugungi sana bilan hisoblaymiz
    if (typeof order.time === 'string' && /^\d{1,2}:\d{2}/.test(order.time.trim())) {
      const [hh, mm] = order.time.trim().split(':').map(Number);
      if (!isNaN(hh) && !isNaN(mm)) {
        const d = new Date();
        d.setHours(hh, mm, 0, 0);
        if (d.getTime() <= Date.now()) {
          return d.getTime();
        }
      }
    }

    return nowMs;
  }, [orderStartTimes, nowMs]);

  // Oshxonaga tegishli faol buyurtmalar ('Yangi' va 'Tayyorlanmoqda')
  const kitchenActiveOrders = useMemo(() => {
    return safeOrders.filter(
      o => o && (o.status === 'Yangi' || o.status === 'Tayyorlanmoqda')
    );
  }, [safeOrders]);

  // Yangi tushgan buyurtmalarga boshlang'ich vaqt muhrini qo'yish va ovoz chalish
  useEffect(() => {
    let updatedMap = null;
    const currentIds = new Set();

    kitchenActiveOrders.forEach(o => {
      const key = String(o.id);
      currentIds.add(key);
      if (!orderStartTimes[key]) {
        if (!updatedMap) updatedMap = { ...orderStartTimes };
        updatedMap[key] = getOrderStartMs(o);
      }
    });

    if (updatedMap) {
      setOrderStartTimes(updatedMap);
      writeLocalJSON('pos_kds_start_times', updatedMap);
    }

    // Yangi buyurtma qo'shilganini tekshirish va "Ding-Dong" chalish
    if (prevOrderIdsRef.current !== null) {
      const hasBrandNew = kitchenActiveOrders.some(
        o => !prevOrderIdsRef.current.has(String(o.id))
      );
      if (hasBrandNew && slaConfig.soundEnabled) {
        playKitchenSound('new_order');
      }
    }
    prevOrderIdsRef.current = currentIds;
  }, [kitchenActiveOrders, orderStartTimes, getOrderStartMs, slaConfig.soundEnabled]);

  // =========================================================
  // 4. HAR BIR BUYURTMA UCHUN SLA HOLATINI HISOBLASH
  // =========================================================
  const enrichedOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const warnSec = (Number(slaConfig.warningMinutes) || 10) * 60;
    const critSec = (Number(slaConfig.criticalMinutes) || 20) * 60;

    return kitchenActiveOrders.map(order => {
      const startMs = getOrderStartMs(order);
      const elapsedSeconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor((nowMs - startMs) / 1000));
      const mins = Math.floor(elapsedSeconds / 60);
      const secs = elapsedSeconds % 60;
      const formattedTimer = `${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}`;

      let slaLevel = 'normal'; // 'normal' | 'warning' | 'critical'
      if (elapsedSeconds >= critSec) {
        slaLevel = 'critical';
      } else if (elapsedSeconds >= warnSec) {
        slaLevel = 'warning';
      }

      const slaProgressPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((elapsedSeconds / critSec) * 100));

      // Sex filtri uchun taomlarni tekshirish
      const items = Array.isArray(order.items) ? order.items : [];
      const filteredItemsByStation = stationCategory === 'Barchasi'
        ? items
        : items.filter(it => {
            if (it?.category === stationCategory) return true;
            const foundMenu = safeMenuItems.find(
              m => m && (String(m.id) === String(it?.id) || m.name === it?.baseName || m.name === it?.name)
            );
            return foundMenu?.category === stationCategory;
          });

      return {
        ...order,
        elapsedSeconds,
        formattedTimer,
        slaLevel,
        slaProgressPct,
        displayItems: filteredItemsByStation
      };
    }).filter(o => o.displayItems.length > 0);
  }, [kitchenActiveOrders, getOrderStartMs, nowMs, slaConfig, stationCategory, safeMenuItems]);

  // Kechikish chegarasiga (Critical) o'tgan buyurtmalar uchun ovozli trevoga chalish
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!slaConfig.soundEnabled) return;

    let shouldBeep = false;
    enrichedOrders.forEach(o => {
      const idStr = String(o.id);
      if (
        o.slaLevel === 'critical' &&
        !mutedOrderIds.includes(idStr) &&
        !lastAlertedBreachIdsRef.current.has(idStr)
      ) {
        shouldBeep = true;
        lastAlertedBreachIdsRef.current.add(idStr);
      }
    });

    if (shouldBeep) {
      playKitchenSound('sla_breach');
    }
  }, [enrichedOrders, mutedOrderIds, slaConfig.soundEnabled]);

  // Filtrlash va Saralash
  const visibleOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const filtered = enrichedOrders.filter(o => {
      if (statusFilter === 'delayed') return o.slaLevel === 'critical' || o.slaLevel === 'warning';
      if (statusFilter === 'new') return o.status === 'Yangi';
      if (statusFilter === 'cooking') return o.status === 'Tayyorlanmoqda';
      return true;
    });

    return filtered.sort((a, b) => {
      if (slaConfig.autoSortByWait) {
        return b.elapsedSeconds - a.elapsedSeconds; // Eng ko'p kutganlar eng tepada
      }
      return a.elapsedSeconds - b.elapsedSeconds;
    });
  }, [enrichedOrders, statusFilter, slaConfig.autoSortByWait]);

  // =========================================================
  // 5. UMUMIY QOZON HISOBI (BATCH AGGREGATION) VA KPI
  // =========================================================
  const batchSummary = useMemo(() => {
    const map = {};
    enrichedOrders.forEach(order => {
      order.displayItems.forEach((item, idx) => {
        if (!item?.name) return;
        const subKey = `${order.id}_${idx}`;
        // Agar oshpaz bu porsiyani allaqachon "pishdi" deb belgilagan bo'lsa, qozon hisobidan kamaytiramiz
        if (checkedSubItems[subKey]) return;

        const name = String(item.name).trim();
        const qty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 0;
        if (!map[name]) {
          map[name] = { count: 0, tables: new Set(), hasUrgent: false };
        }
        map[name].count += qty;
        map[name].tables.add(order.table);
        if (order.slaLevel === 'critical') {
          map[name].hasUrgent = true;
        }
      });
    });

    return Object.entries(map).sort((a, b) => b[1].count - a[1].count);
  }, [enrichedOrders, checkedSubItems]);

  const kitchenKpi = useMemo(() => {
    const totalActive = enrichedOrders.length;
    const criticalCount = enrichedOrders.filter(o => o.slaLevel === 'critical').length;
    const warningCount = enrichedOrders.filter(o => o.slaLevel === 'warning').length;
    const newCount = enrichedOrders.filter(o => o.status === 'Yangi').length;

    const totalCompletedToday = (slaHistory.onTimeCount || 0) + (slaHistory.delayedCount || 0);
    const avgCookSeconds = totalCompletedToday > 0
      ? Math.round((slaHistory.totalCookSeconds || 0) / totalCompletedToday)
      : 0;
    const avgCookMins = Math.floor(avgCookSeconds / 60);
    const avgCookSecs = avgCookSeconds % 60;

    const slaComplianceRate = totalCompletedToday > 0
      ? Math.round((slaHistory.onTimeCount / totalCompletedToday) * 100)
      : 100;

    return {
      totalActive,
      criticalCount,
      warningCount,
      newCount,
      totalCompletedToday,
      avgCookFormatted: `${avgCookMins}d ${avgCookSecs}s`,
      slaComplianceRate
    };
  }, [enrichedOrders, slaHistory]);

  // =========================================================
  // 6. OSHPAZ HARAKATLARI (STATUS O'ZGARTIRISH VA SUB-ITEM)
  // =========================================================
  const handleToggleSubItem = (orderId, itemIndex) => {
    const key = `${orderId}_${itemIndex}`;
    setCheckedSubItems(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [key]: !prev[key]
    }));
  };

  const handleAdvanceOrderStatus = (order) => {
    if (!order) return;
    const currentStatus = order.status;
    const nextStatus = currentStatus === 'Yangi' ? 'Tayyorlanmoqda' : 'Tayyor';
    const nowTimeStr = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });

    // Agar taom 'Tayyor' bo'layotgan bo'lsa, SLA statistikasiga yozamiz
    if (nextStatus === 'Tayyor') {
      const critSec = (Number(slaConfig.criticalMinutes) || 20) * 60;
      const isDelayed = (order.elapsedSeconds || 0) >= critSec;

      setSlaHistory(prev => ({
        onTimeCount: (prev.onTimeCount || 0) + (isDelayed ? 0 : 1),
        delayedCount: (prev.delayedCount || 0) + (isDelayed ? 1 : 0),
        totalCookSeconds: (prev.totalCookSeconds || 0) + (order.elapsedSeconds || 0)
      }));
    }

    updateOrdersState(prev =>
      (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(o => {
        if (!o || String(o.id) !== String(order.id)) return o;
        return {
          ...o,
          status: nextStatus,
          cookingStartedAt: nextStatus === 'Tayyorlanmoqda' ? nowTimeStr : o.cookingStartedAt,
          readyTime: nextStatus === 'Tayyor' ? nowTimeStr : o.readyTime,
          cookDurationSeconds: nextStatus === 'Tayyor' ? order.elapsedSeconds : o.cookDurationSeconds
        };
      })
    );

    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
      addAuditLog(
        nextStatus === 'Tayyorlanmoqda' ? "Oshxona: Pishirish boshlandi" : "Oshxona: Taom tayyor bo'ldi",
        `${order.table} (${order.formattedTimer} ichida -> ${nextStatus})`
      );
    }
  };

  const handleMuteOrderAlarm = (orderId) => {
    const idStr = String(orderId);
    setMutedOrderIds(prev => (prev.includes(idStr) ? prev : [...prev, idStr]));
  };

  // Stop-List funksiyasi
  const handleToggleDishAvailability = (dish) => {
    if (!dish) return;
    const nextAvailable = dish.is_available === false ? true : false;

    updateMenuState(prev =>
      (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(item =>
        item && String(item.id) === String(dish.id)
          ? { ...item, is_available: nextAvailable }
          : item
      )
    );

    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
      addAuditLog(
        nextAvailable ? "Oshxona: Taom sotuvga qaytarildi" : "Oshxona: Stop-Listga olindi",
        `"${dish.name}" (${ currentUser?.name || 'Oshpaz' })`
      );
    }
  };

  const filteredStopListDishes = useMemo(() => {
    return safeMenuItems.filter(item => {
      if (!item) return false;
      if (stopCategoryFilter !== 'Barchasi' && item.category !== stopCategoryFilter) return false;
      if (stopSearch.trim() !== '') {
        return String(item.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(stopSearch.trim().toLowerCase());
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [safeMenuItems, stopCategoryFilter, stopSearch]);

  const stopListCount = useMemo(() => {
    return safeMenuItems.filter(i => i && i.is_available === false).length;
  }, [safeMenuItems]);

  // =========================================================
  // 7. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // =========================================================
  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto pb-12 space-y-5 animate-fade-in">
      
      {/* =======================================================
          TEPADAGI SLA TELEMETRIYA VA BOSHQARUV PANELI
          ======================================================= */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-4 md:p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 space-y-4">
        
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row justify-between items-start lg:items-center gap-4">
          {/* Asosiy 2 ta Tab: Buyurtmalar va Stop-List */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 w-full lg:w-auto">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('orders')}
              className={`flex-1 sm:flex-initial px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl font-black text-xs sm:text-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${
                activeTab === 'orders'
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/30'
                  : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300'
              }`}
            >
              <span>ðŸ”¥</span>
              <span>Faol Buyurtmalar ({kitchenKpi.totalActive})</span>
              {kitchenKpi.criticalCount > 0 && (
                <span className="bg-red-600 text-white text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full animate-pulse">
                  {kitchenKpi.criticalCount} kechikdi!
                </span>
              )}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('stoplist')}
              className={`flex-1 sm:flex-initial px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl font-black text-xs sm:text-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${
                activeTab === 'stoplist'
                  ? 'bg-red-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-red-600/30'
                  : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300'
              }`}
            >
              <span>ðŸ›‘</span>
              <span>Stop-List Boshqaruvi</span>
              {stopListCount > 0 && (
                <span className="bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                  {stopListCount} ta tugagan
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Ovoz va SLA Sozlamalari Tugmalari */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 w-full lg:w-auto justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                const nextSound = !slaConfig.soundEnabled;
                setSlaConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, soundEnabled: nextSound }));
                if (nextSound) playKitchenSound('new_order');
              }}
              className={`px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-xs transition flex items-center gap-1.5 border ${
                slaConfig.soundEnabled
                  ? 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800'
                  : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-500 border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{slaConfig.soundEnabled ? 'ðŸ”Š Signal Yoqilgan' : "ðŸ”‡ Signal O'chiq"}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsSlaModalOpen(true)}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 hover:bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 font-black text-xs border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800 transition flex items-center gap-1.5"
            >
              <span>â±ï¸ SLA Normativ:</span>
              <span className="text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">{slaConfig.warningMinutes}d</span>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">{slaConfig.criticalMinutes}d</span>
              <span>âš™ï¸</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 4 TA JONLI SLA MONITORING KARTASI */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 pt-1">
          <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/60 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-200/80 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-400 block">
                â±ï¸ O'rtacha Pishirish Vaqti
              </span>
              <p className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 mt-0.5">
                {kitchenKpi.avgCookFormatted}
              </p>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400">
                Bugun: {kitchenKpi.totalCompletedToday} ta chek chiqdi
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-2xl">âš¡</span>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/60 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-200/80 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-emerald-600 block">
                ðŸŽ¯ SLA Sifat Ko'rsatkichi
              </span>
              <p className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">
                {kitchenKpi.slaComplianceRate}% vaqtida
              </p>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400">
                Normativ: {slaConfig.criticalMinutes} daqiqagacha
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-2xl">ðŸŸ¢</span>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-amber-50/70 dark:bg-amber-900/20 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/50 flex justify-between items-center">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-amber-600 block">
                ðŸŸ¡ Shoshilinch ({slaConfig.warningMinutes}+ daqiqa)
              </span>
              <p className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 mt-0.5">
                {kitchenKpi.warningCount} ta buyurtma
              </p>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-700/70 dark:text-amber-300/70">
                Tezlashtirish kerak
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-2xl">â³</span>
          </div>

          <div className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl border flex justify-between items-center transition ${
            kitchenKpi.criticalCount > 0
              ? 'bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/40 border-red-400 dark:border-red-600 animate-pulse'
              : 'bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/60 border-gray-200/80 dark:border-gray-700'
          }`}>
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-red-600 block">
                ðŸ”´ Kechikkan ({slaConfig.criticalMinutes}+ daqiqa)
              </span>
              <p className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 mt-0.5">
                {kitchenKpi.criticalCount} ta buyurtma
              </p>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-red-500">
                {kitchenKpi.criticalCount > 0 ? 'ðŸš¨ Zudlik bilan chiqaring!' : 'Kechikkanlar yo\'q'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-2xl">ðŸš¨</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =======================================================
          1-EKRAN: OSHXONA BUYURTMALARI VA SLA TAYMERLARI
          ======================================================= */}
      {activeTab === 'orders' && (
        <>
          {/* UMUMIY QOZON HISOBI (BATCH SUMMARY) */}
          {batchSummary.length > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500/10 via-orange-500/10 to-red-500/10 dark:from-amber-900/20 dark:to-red-900/20 p-4 rounded-3xl border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800/50">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span>ðŸ²</span> Umumiy Qozon Hisobi (Hozir pishirilishi kerak bo'lgan jami porsiyalar)
                </h3>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500">
                  {batchSummary.length} xil taom
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {batchSummary.map(([dishName, info]) => (
                  <div
                    key={dishName}
                    className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-2xl border flex items-center gap-2.5 shadow-sm ${
                      info.hasUrgent
                        ? 'bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/40 border-red-300 dark:border-red-700'
                        : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border-amber-200 dark:border-gray-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className="font-black text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
                      {dishName}
                    </span>
                    <span className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-xl font-black text-xs text-white ${
                      info.hasUrgent ? 'bg-red-600' : 'bg-amber-500'
                    }`}>
                      {info.count} pors
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* SEX VA STATUS FILTRLARI */}
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-stretch md:items-center gap-3 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
            {/* Holat va SLA filtri */}
            <div className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto hide-scrollbar">
              {[
                { id: 'all', label: `Barchasi (${enrichedOrders.length})` },
                { id: 'delayed', label: `ðŸš¨ Kechikayotganlar (${kitchenKpi.criticalCount + kitchenKpi.warningCount})` },
                { id: 'new', label: `ðŸ†• Yangi (${kitchenKpi.newCount})` },
                { id: 'cooking', label: 'ðŸ”¥ Pishirilmoqda' }
              ].map(f => (
                <button
                  key={f.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setStatusFilter(f.id)}
                  className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-black whitespace-nowrap transition ${
                    statusFilter === f.id
                      ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                      : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400'
                  }`}
                >
                  {f.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Oshxona Sexi (Kategoriya) filtri */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto hide-scrollbar">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-400 whitespace-nowrap pl-1">
                ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³ Sex:
              </span>
              {['Barchasi', ...safeCategories].map(cat => (
                <button
                  key={cat}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setStationCategory(cat)}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black whitespace-nowrap transition ${
                    stationCategory === cat
                      ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white shadow-sm'
                      : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400'
                  }`}
                >
                  {cat}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* BUYURTMA KARTOCHKALARI GRIDI */}
          {visibleOrders.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl p-16 text-center border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
              <span className="text-6xl block mb-4">ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³âœ¨</span>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-800 dark:text-white">
                Oshxonada kutilayotgan buyurtmalar yo'q!
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 mt-1">
                Yangi buyurtma tushishi bilan ovozli signal chalinadi va sekundomer avtomatik ishga tushadi.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
              {visibleOrders.map(order => {
                const isCritical = order.slaLevel === 'critical';
                const isWarning = order.slaLevel === 'warning';
                const isMuted = mutedOrderIds.includes(String(order.id));

                // Chek ichida nechta taom pishib bo'lganini hisoblash
                const totalSubItems = order.displayItems.length;
                const completedSubItems = order.displayItems.filter(
                  (_, idx) => checkedSubItems[`${order.id}_${idx}`]
                ).length;

                const cardBorderStyle = isCritical
                  ? 'border-red-500 dark:border-red-500 ring-4 ring-red-500/20'
                  : isWarning
                  ? 'border-amber-400 dark:border-amber-500 ring-2 ring-amber-400/20'
                  : order.status === 'Yangi'
                  ? 'border-indigo-400 dark:border-indigo-500'
                  : 'border-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-600';

                const headerBgStyle = isCritical
                  ? 'bg-red-600 text-white'
                  : isWarning
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white'
                  : order.status === 'Yangi'
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                  : 'bg-emerald-600 text-white';

                return (
                  <div
                    key={order.id}
                    className={`bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl border-2 shadow-md overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${cardBorderStyle}`}
                  >
                    <div>
                      {/* KARTOCHKA TEPASI: STOL, JONLI SEKUNDOMER VA STATUS */}
                      <div className={`p-4 ${headerBgStyle} transition-colors`}>
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2">
                          <div>
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                              <h3 className="text-xl font-black tracking-tight">
                                ðŸª‘ {order.table}
                              </h3>
                              {isCritical && (
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-white text-red-600 text-[10px] font-black uppercase animate-bounce">
                                  ðŸš¨ KECHIKDI!
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-xs font-bold opacity-90 mt-0.5">
                              ðŸ‘¤ {order.waiterName || order.waiter || 'Ofitsiant'} â€¢ #{String(order.id).slice(-4)}
                            </p>
                          </div>

                          {/* KATTA JONLI SLA SEKUNDOMERI */}
                          <div className="text-right">
                            <div className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-2xl font-mono font-black text-lg sm:text-xl shadow-inner flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                              isCritical
                                ? 'bg-red-900/60 text-white animate-pulse'
                                : 'bg-black/20 text-white'
                            }`}>
                              <span>â±ï¸</span>
                              <span>{order.formattedTimer}</span>
                            </div>
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase opacity-85 block mt-1">
                              {isCritical
                                ? 'Me\'yordan oshdi!'
                                : isWarning
                                ? 'Shoshiling!'
                                : 'Vaqt me\'yorida'}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Vizual SLA vaqt to'lish chizig'i */}
                        <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-black/20 rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-3">
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-white transition-all duration-1000"
                            style={{ width: `${order.slaProgressPct}%` }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* KECHIKKAN BUYURTMA UCHUN SIGNALNI O'CHIRISH PANELI */}
                      {isCritical && !isMuted && (
                        <div className="bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/30 px-4 py-2 border-b border-red-200 dark:border-red-800 flex justify-between items-center">
                          <span className="text-[11px] font-black text-red-600 dark:text-red-300">
                            ðŸ”” Buyurtma {slaConfig.criticalMinutes} daqiqadan oshib ketdi!
                          </span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleMuteOrderAlarm(order.id)}
                            className="text-[10px] font-black bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 text-red-600 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-red-200 hover:bg-red-100 transition"
                          >
                            ðŸ”• Ovozni tinchlantirish
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      {/* TAOMLAR RO'YXATI (USTIGA BOSIB âœ… BELGILASH MUMKIN) */}
                      <div className="p-4 space-y-2.5">
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-[11px] font-black text-gray-400 uppercase">
                          <span>Taomlar (Bosib belgilang)</span>
                          <span>Tayyor: {completedSubItems}/{totalSubItems}</span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="space-y-2">
                          {order.displayItems.map((item, idx) => {
                            const subKey = `${order.id}_${idx}`;
                            const isSubDone = Boolean(checkedSubItems[subKey]);
                            const qty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 1;

                            return (
                              <div
                                key={item.cartItemId || idx}

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

export default function LockScreen({ 
  cafeName = 'Restoran POS', 
  users = [], 
  tables = [],
  orders = [],
  onLogin, 
  onGuestLogin,
  onOpenQrMenu,
  isDarkMode = false 
}) {
  // ==========================================
  // 1. STATE'LAR
  // ==========================================
  const [selectedGroup, setSelectedGroup] = useState(null); // 'management' | 'staff' | null
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  
  // Mijoz QR-Menyusi uchun Stol tanlash modali state'i
  const [isTableModalOpen, setIsTableModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [tableFilter, setTableFilter] = useState('free'); // 'free' (Bo'shlar) | 'all' (Barchasi)

  const requiredPinLength = selectedGroup === 'management' ? 6 : 4;

  // ==========================================
  // 2. BO'SH VA BAND STOLLARNI ANIQLASH MANTIG'I
  // ==========================================
  const allTablesList = useMemo(() => {
    if (Array.isArray(tables) && tables.length > 0) {
      return tables.filter(t => t !== 'Olib ketish');
    }
    try {
      const savedTables = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('pos_tables') || '[]');
      if (Array.isArray(savedTables) && savedTables.length > 0) {
        return savedTables.filter(t => t !== 'Olib ketish');
      }
    } catch {
      // Xatolik bo'lsa standart stollarni qaytaramiz
    }
    return ['1-Stol', '2-Stol', '3-Stol', '4-Stol', '5-Stol', '6-Stol', '7-Stol', '8-Stol', 'VIP-1', 'VIP-2'];
  }, [tables]);

  const activeOrdersList = useMemo(() => {
    if (Array.isArray(orders) && orders.length > 0) {
      return orders;
    }
    try {
      return JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('pos_orders') || '[]');
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }, [orders]);

  // Har bir stolning bo'sh yoki bandligini hisoblaymiz
  const tablesWithStatus = useMemo(() => {
    return allTablesList.map(tableName => {
      const nameStr = typeof tableName === 'object' ? tableName.name : String(tableName);
      const hasActiveOrder = activeOrdersList.some(
        o => o && o.table === nameStr && o.status !== "To'langan" && o.status !== 'Qarz'
      );
      return {
        name: nameStr,
        isOccupied: hasActiveOrder
      };
    });
  }, [allTablesList, activeOrdersList]);

  const freeTables = tablesWithStatus.filter(t => !t.isOccupied);
  const occupiedTables = tablesWithStatus.filter(t => t.isOccupied);

  // ==========================================
  // 3. PIN-KOD ORQALI KIRISH MANTIG'I
  // ==========================================
  const handleSelectGroup = (group) => {
    setSelectedGroup(group);
    setPin('');
    setError('');
  };

  const handleClosePinModal = () => {
    setSelectedGroup(null);
    setPin('');
    setError('');
  };

  const handleNumberClick = (num) => {
    if (pin.length >= requiredPinLength) return;
    const nextPin = pin + num;
    setPin(nextPin);
    setError('');

    if (nextPin.length === requiredPinLength) {
      verifyPin(nextPin);
    }
  };

  const handleBackspace = () => {
    setPin(prev => prev.slice(0, -1));
    setError('');
  };

  const verifyPin = (enteredPin) => {
    const safeUsers = Array.isArray(users) ? users : [];
    const matchedUser = safeUsers.find(u => {
      const userPin = String(u.pin || '').trim();
      const role = String(u.role || '').toLowerCase();
      const isManagementRole = role === 'admin' || role === 'warehouse' || role === 'omborchi';

      if (selectedGroup === 'management') {
        return isManagementRole && userPin === enteredPin;
      } else {
        return !isManagementRole && userPin === enteredPin;
      }
    });

    if (matchedUser) {
      if (typeof onLogin === 'function') {
        onLogin(matchedUser);
      }
      handleClosePinModal();
    } else {
      setError("âŒ PIN-kod noto'g'ri! Qaytadan urinib ko'ring.");
      setTimeout(() => {
        setPin('');
      }, 400);
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 4. MIJOZ QR-MENYUSIGA STOL TANLAB KIRISH
  // ==========================================
  const handleSelectCustomerTable = (tableName) => {
    setIsTableModalOpen(false);
    const callback = onGuestLogin || onOpenQrMenu;
    if (typeof callback === 'function') {
      callback(tableName);
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 5. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  return (
    <div className={`min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 select-none transition-colors duration-300 ${
      isDarkMode ? 'dark bg-gray-900 text-white' : 'bg-gray-50 text-gray-900'
    }`}>
      
      {/* SARLAVHA */}
      <div className="text-center mb-8">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-3xl flex items-center justify-center text-3xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-xl shadow-indigo-600/30">
          ðŸ½ï¸
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black tracking-tight text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
          {cafeName}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mt-1">
          Premium Boshqaruv Tizimi
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* ASOSIY KARTA */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 w-full max-w-md rounded-[32px] p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 space-y-5">
        
        {/* 1. BOSHQARUV (6 XONALI PIN) */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handleSelectGroup('management')}
          className="w-full p-6 rounded-3xl border-2 border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 hover:border-indigo-500 dark:hover:border-indigo-500 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-gray-900/40 hover:bg-indigo-50/30 transition-all active:scale-[0.98] flex flex-col items-center text-center group"
        >
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-3xl mb-3 group-hover:scale-110 transition">
            ðŸ‘”
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-black tracking-wider uppercase text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
            BOSHQARUV
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
            Faqat rahbar va omborchi uchun
          </p>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handleSelectGroup('staff')}
          className="w-full p-6 rounded-3xl border-2 border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 hover:border-emerald-500 dark:hover:border-emerald-500 bg-gray-50/50 dark:bg-gray-900/40 hover:bg-emerald-50/30 transition-all active:scale-[0.98] flex flex-col items-center text-center group"

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';

export default function ManagerOverrideModal({ 
  request,
  actionName, 
  users = [], 
  onSuccess, 
  onCancel,
  onClose
}) {
  // ==========================================
  // 1. PROP'LARNI XAVFSIZ BIRXILLASHTIRISH
  // ==========================================
  const resolvedActionName = actionName || request?.actionName || 'Maxfiy amalni tasdiqlash';
  const resolvedOnSuccess = onSuccess || request?.onSuccess;
  const resolvedOnCancel = onCancel || onClose;

  // Agar users prop bo'sh kelsa, localStorage'dan zaxira sifatida o'qiymiz
  const adminUsers = useMemo(() => {
    let sourceUsers = Array.isArray(users) && users.length > 0 ? users : [];
    if (sourceUsers.length === 0) {
      try {
        const saved = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('pos_users') || '[]');
        if (Array.isArray(saved)) sourceUsers = saved;
      } catch {
        // Xatolik bo'lsa standart adminga tayanamiz
      }
    }

    const filteredAdmins = sourceUsers.filter(
      u => u && String(u.role || '').toLowerCase() === 'admin'
    );

    // Agar bazada umuman admin topilmasa, tizim qulflanib qolmasligi uchun fallback
    return filteredAdmins.length > 0
      ? filteredAdmins
      : [{ id: 1, name: 'Admin', role: 'admin', pin: '777777' }];
  }, [users]);

  // ==========================================
  // 2. STATE'LAR (PIN, XATO VA BLOKIROVKA)
  // ==========================================
  const [adminPin, setAdminPin] = useState('');
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState('');
  const [failedAttempts, setFailedAttempts] = useState(0);
  const [lockoutSeconds, setLockoutSeconds] = useState(0);

  // Blokirovka taymeri (5 marta xato terilganda 15 soniya kutish)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (lockoutSeconds <= 0) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setLockoutSeconds(prev => (prev <= 1 ? 0 : prev - 1));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [lockoutSeconds]);

  // ==========================================
  // 3. PAROLNI TEKSHIRISH MANTIG'I
  // ==========================================
  const verifyPinCode = useCallback((pinToVerify) => {
    if (lockoutSeconds > 0) return;

    const cleanPin = String(pinToVerify || '').trim();
    if (!cleanPin) return;

    const validAdmin = adminUsers.find(
      a => String(a.pin || '').trim() === cleanPin
    );

    if (validAdmin) {
      setErrorMsg('');
      setFailedAttempts(0);
      setAdminPin('');
      if (typeof resolvedOnSuccess === 'function') {
        resolvedOnSuccess(validAdmin);
      }
    } else {
      const nextAttempts = failedAttempts + 1;
      setFailedAttempts(nextAttempts);
      setAdminPin('');

      if (nextAttempts >= 5) {
        setLockoutSeconds(15);
        setErrorMsg("â›”ï¸ 5 marta xato kiritildi! 15 soniya kuting.");
        setFailedAttempts(0);
      } else {
        setErrorMsg(`âŒ Xato PIN-kod! Tasdiqlanmadi (${nextAttempts}/5)`);
      }
    }
  }, [adminUsers, failedAttempts, lockoutSeconds, resolvedOnSuccess]);

  // ==========================================
  // 4. RAQAMLAR KLAVIATURASI VA FIZIK KLAVIATURA
  // ==========================================
  const handleDigitPress = useCallback((digit) => {
    if (lockoutSeconds > 0) return;
    setErrorMsg('');

    setAdminPin(prev => {
      if (prev.length >= 6) return prev;
      const nextPin = prev + String(digit);
      
      // 6 xonali parol to'lishi bilan avtomatik tekshiramiz
      if (nextPin.length === 6) {
        setTimeout(() => verifyPinCode(nextPin), 80);
      }
      return nextPin;
    });
  }, [lockoutSeconds, verifyPinCode]);

  const handleBackspace = useCallback(() => {
    if (lockoutSeconds > 0) return;
    setErrorMsg('');
    setAdminPin(prev => prev.slice(0, -1));
  }, [lockoutSeconds]);

  const handleClear = useCallback(() => {
    if (lockoutSeconds > 0) return;
    setErrorMsg('');
    setAdminPin('');
  }, [lockoutSeconds]);

  const handleManualSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (adminPin.length > 0) {
      verifyPinCode(adminPin);
    }
  };

  // Kompyuter klaviaturasidan raqamlar, Backspace, Enter va Escape bosilishini ushlash
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (typeof resolvedOnCancel === 'function') resolvedOnCancel();
        return;
      }

      if (lockoutSeconds > 0) return;

      if (/^[0-9]$/.test(e.key)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleDigitPress(e.key);
      } else if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleBackspace();
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && adminPin.length >= 4) {
        e.preventDefault();
        verifyPinCode(adminPin);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [adminPin, handleBackspace, handleDigitPress, lockoutSeconds, resolvedOnCancel, verifyPinCode]);

  // ==========================================
  // 5. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  return (
    <div 
      onClick={() => typeof resolvedOnCancel === 'function' && resolvedOnCancel()}
      className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-md z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in select-none"
    >
      <div 
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 w-full max-w-sm rounded-[32px] p-6 md:p-7 shadow-2xl border-2 border-red-100 dark:border-red-900/40 text-center relative overflow-hidden"
      >
        {/* Tepadagi qizil xavfsizlik chizig'i */}
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-red-500 via-amber-500 to-red-600"></div>

        {/* Yopish (X) tugmasi */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => typeof resolvedOnCancel === 'function' && resolvedOnCancel()}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-500 hover:bg-red-100 hover:text-red-500 font-black flex items-center justify-center transition"
          title="Yopish (Esc)"
        >
          âœ•
        </button>
        
        {/* Qalqon Ikonkasi */}
        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/30 text-red-600 dark:text-red-500 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-4xl mx-auto mb-3 shadow-inner">
          ðŸ›¡ï¸
        </div>
        
        <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white">
          Rahbar Tasdig'i Talab Qilinadi
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 mt-0.5 mb-4">
          6 xonali Admin PIN-kodini kiriting
        </p>
        
        {/* Tasdiqlanayotgan amal nomi */}
        <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 mb-5 bg-red-50/70 dark:bg-gray-900/80 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <span className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400 mb-1">
            Tasdiqlanayotgan harakat:
          </span>
          <span className="text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 font-black break-words">
            "{resolvedActionName}"
          </span>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleManualSubmit} className="space-y-5">
          
          {/* Vizual PIN Nuqtachalari (6 xonali) */}
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-center gap-3 my-2">
              {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, idx) => {
                const isFilled = idx < adminPin.length;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={idx}
                    className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full transition-all duration-150 ${
                      errorMsg
                        ? 'bg-red-500 scale-110'
                        : isFilled
                        ? 'bg-red-600 dark:bg-red-500 scale-125 shadow-md shadow-red-500/40'
                        : 'bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700'
                    }`}
                  />
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {/* Xatolik yoki Blokirovka xabari */}
            {lockoutSeconds > 0 ? (
              <p className="text-xs font-black text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 mt-3 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/40 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl animate-pulse">
                â³ Kirish vaqtincha bloklandi: {lockoutSeconds} soniya
              </p>
            ) : errorMsg ? (
              <p className="text-xs font-black text-red-500 mt-3 animate-bounce">
                {errorMsg}
              </p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-400 mt-2">
                Klaviaturadan yoki ekrandagi raqamlardan tering
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Sensorli Ekranlar uchun Raqamlar Klaviaturasi (Numpad) */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9].map(num => (
              <button
                key={num}
                type="button"
                disabled={lockoutSeconds > 0}
                onClick={() => handleDigitPress(num)}
                className="h-13 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700/60 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-900/30 text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white border border-gray-200/80 dark:border-gray-600 transition active:scale-90 disabled:opacity-40"
