import React from 'react';

export default function StatsOverviewCards({ 
  totalRevenue = 0, 
  totalFoodCost = 0,
  totalExpenses = 0,
  totalInkassatsiya = 0,
  totalNetProfit,
  totalOrdersCount = 0, 
  totalServiceFee = 0, 
  totalDiscount = 0,
  periodLabel = 'Joriy davr',
  onOpenReceiptsModal
}) {
  // Xavfsiz raqamlar (Oq oyna va NaN xatosidan 100% himoya)
  const safeRevenue = Number(totalRevenue) || 0;
  const safeFoodCost = Number(totalFoodCost) || 0;
  const safeExpenses = Number(totalExpenses) || 0;
  const safeInkassatsiya = Number(totalInkassatsiya) || 0;
  const safeOrdersCount = Number(totalOrdersCount) || 0;
  const safeServiceFee = Number(totalServiceFee) || 0;
  const safeDiscount = Number(totalDiscount) || 0;

  // Haqiqiy Sof Foyda = Tushum - Xomashyo Tannarxi - Operatsion Xarajatlar
  const safeNetProfit = totalNetProfit !== undefined 
    ? (Number(totalNetProfit) || 0) 
    : (safeRevenue - safeFoodCost - safeExpenses);

  // Analitik ko'rsatkichlar va foizlar
  const netFoodRevenue = Math.max(0, safeRevenue - safeServiceFee);
  const averageCheck = safeOrdersCount > 0 
    ? Math.round(safeRevenue / safeOrdersCount) 
    : 0;

  const foodCostPercent = safeRevenue > 0 
    ? ((safeFoodCost / safeRevenue) * 100).toFixed(1) 
    : '0.0';

  const expensePercent = safeRevenue > 0
    ? ((safeExpenses / safeRevenue) * 100).toFixed(1)
    : '0.0';

  const netMarginPercent = safeRevenue > 0 
    ? ((safeNetProfit / safeRevenue) * 100).toFixed(1) 
    : '0.0';

  const serviceSharePercent = safeRevenue > 0 
    ? ((safeServiceFee / safeRevenue) * 100).toFixed(1) 
    : '0.0';

  const grossBeforeDiscount = safeRevenue + safeDiscount;
  const discountRatePercent = grossBeforeDiscount > 0 
    ? ((safeDiscount / grossBeforeDiscount) * 100).toFixed(1) 
    : '0.0';

  const handleOpenReceipts = () => {
    if (typeof onOpenReceiptsModal === 'function') {
      onOpenReceiptsModal('paid');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4 md:gap-5">
      
      {/* 1. JAMI TUSHUM KARTASI (Bosilganda cheklar ro'yxatini ochadi) */}
      <div 
        onClick={handleOpenReceipts}
        className="bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 to-emerald-100/80 dark:from-emerald-900/30 dark:to-emerald-800/20 p-6 rounded-3xl border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/50 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all relative overflow-hidden group flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer"
        title="Bosib ushbu davrdagi cheklar ro'yxatini ko'ring"
      >
        <div className="absolute right-[-16px] top-[-16px] opacity-10 text-8xl group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform select-none pointer-events-none">
          ðŸ’°
        </div>

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

// =========================================================
// BUYURTMA SANASINI HAR QANDAY FORMATDAN XAVFSIZ O'QISH
// =========================================================
const parseOrderTimeMs = (order) => {
  if (!order) return Date.now();

  if (order.closedAtMs && !isNaN(Number(order.closedAtMs))) return Number(order.closedAtMs);
  if (order.createdAt && !isNaN(Number(order.createdAt))) return Number(order.createdAt);

  const rawDateStr = String(order.closeTime || order.archiveDate || order.date || '').trim();
  if (rawDateStr) {
    // 1) DD.MM.YYYY formatini tekshirish (Masalan: 17.10.2026 yoki 17.10.2026, 14:20)
    const dmyMatch = rawDateStr.match(/(\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{4})/);
    if (dmyMatch) {
      const day = Number(dmyMatch[1]);
      const month = Number(dmyMatch[2]) - 1;
      const year = Number(dmyMatch[3]);
      const timeMatch = rawDateStr.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
      const hh = timeMatch ? Number(timeMatch[1]) : 12;
      const mm = timeMatch ? Number(timeMatch[2]) : 0;
      const constructed = new Date(year, month, day, hh, mm).getTime();
      if (!isNaN(constructed)) return constructed;
    }

    // 2) Standart ISO yoki inglizcha sanani tekshirish
    const parsed = Date.parse(rawDateStr);
    if (!isNaN(parsed)) return parsed;
  }

  // 3) Order ID ichida 13 xonali timestamp bo'lsa
  const idMatch = String(order.id || '').match(/\d{13}/);
  if (idMatch) {
    const idMs = Number(idMatch[0]);
    if (idMs > 1600000000000 && idMs <= Date.now() + 86400000) return idMs;
  }

  return Date.now();
};

const toDateInputValue = (dateObj) => {
  const y = dateObj.getFullYear();
  const m = String(dateObj.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(dateObj.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
};

export default function StatsPopularItems({ 
  popularItems = [], 
  orders = [],
  onCloseShift 
}) {
  // Saralash rejimi: 'count' (Porsiya soni bo'yicha) | 'revenue' (Tushumi bo'yicha)
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState('count');
  const [showAllRanks, setShowAllRanks] = useState(false);

  // YANGI: 2-Skrinshot bo'yicha taomlar uchun maxsus Sana Oralig'i filtri
  // 'sync' (Umumiy filtr bilan) | 'today' (Bugun) | 'week' (Hafta) | 'month' (Shu oy) | 'last_month' (O'tgan oy) | 'custom' (Sana oralig'i)
  const [itemDateMode, setItemDateMode] = useState('sync');
  const [isDatePanelOpen, setIsDatePanelOpen] = useState(false);

  const todayStr = useMemo(() => toDateInputValue(new Date()), []);
  const firstDayOfMonthStr = useMemo(() => {
    const d = new Date();
    d.setDate(1);
    return toDateInputValue(d);
  }, []);

  const [customStartDate, setCustomStartDate] = useState(firstDayOfMonthStr);
  const [customEndDate, setCustomEndDate] = useState(todayStr);

  // ==========================================
  // 1. TANLANGAN SANALAR ORALIG'I BO'YICHA TAOMLARNI HISOBLASH
  // ==========================================
  const computedPopularSource = useMemo(() => {
    // Agar 'sync' tanlangan bo'lsa yoki orders prop berilmagan bo'lsa, StatsMode dan kelgan popularItems ni ishlatamiz
    if (itemDateMode === 'sync' || !Array.isArray(orders) || orders.length === 0) {
      return popularItems;
    }

    const now = new Date();
    let startMs = 0;
    let endMs = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 23, 59, 59, 999).getTime();

    if (itemDateMode === 'today') {
      startMs = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 0, 0, 0, 0).getTime();
    } else if (itemDateMode === 'week') {
      const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 0, 0, 0, 0);
      d.setDate(d.getDate() - 6);
      startMs = d.getTime();
    } else if (itemDateMode === 'month') {
      startMs = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1, 0, 0, 0, 0).getTime();
    } else if (itemDateMode === 'last_month') {
      startMs = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0).getTime();
      endMs = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 0, 23, 59, 59, 999).getTime();
    } else if (itemDateMode === 'custom') {
      if (customStartDate) {
        const [sy, sm, sd] = customStartDate.split('-').map(Number);
        startMs = new Date(sy, sm - 1, sd, 0, 0, 0, 0).getTime();
      }
      if (customEndDate) {
        const [ey, em, ed] = customEndDate.split('-').map(Number);
        endMs = new Date(ey, em - 1, ed, 23, 59, 59, 999).getTime();
      }
    }

    const validOrders = orders.filter(o => {
      if (!o || !["To'langan", 'Arxiv', 'Qarz'].includes(o.status)) return false;
      const tMs = parseOrderTimeMs(o);
      return tMs >= startMs && tMs <= endMs;
    });

    const map = {};
    validOrders.forEach(order => {
      if (!Array.isArray(order.items)) return;
      order.items.forEach(item => {
        if (!item) return;
        const rawName = String(item.name || "Noma'lum taom").trim();
        const qty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 0;
        const price = Number(item.price ?? item.variants?.[0]?.price ?? 0) || 0;

        // Katta-kichik harf xatosini tuzatish (Masalan: "manti" va "Manti" bir xil narxda bo'lsa birlashtiriladi)
        const normalizedKey = `${rawName.toLowerCase()}__${price}`;
        const displayName = rawName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + rawName.slice(1);

        if (!map[normalizedKey]) {
          map[normalizedKey] = {
            name: displayName,
            count: 0,
            revenue: 0
          };
        }
        map[normalizedKey].count += qty;
        map[normalizedKey].revenue += qty * price;
      });
    });

    return Object.values(map).map(st => [st.name, { count: st.count, revenue: st.revenue }]);
  }, [itemDateMode, orders, popularItems, customStartDate, customEndDate]);

  // ==========================================
  // 2. MA'LUMOTLARNI STANDARTLASHTIRISH VA SARALASH
  // ==========================================
  const normalizedItems = useMemo(() => {
    if (!Array.isArray(computedPopularSource)) return [];

    // Bir xil nomdagi taomlarni (masalan "manti" va "Manti") chiroyli birlashtirish
    const mergedByName = {};

    computedPopularSource.forEach((entry, idx) => {
      if (!entry) return;
      let rawName = '';
      let count = 0;
      let revenue = 0;

      if (Array.isArray(entry)) {
        rawName = String(entry[0] || "Noma'lum taom").trim();
        count = Number(entry[1]?.count ?? entry[1]?.quantity ?? entry[1]?.qty ?? 0) || 0;
        revenue = Number(entry[1]?.revenue ?? entry[1]?.total ?? 0) || 0;
      } else if (typeof entry === 'object') {
        rawName = String(entry.name || "Noma'lum taom").trim();
        count = Number(entry.count ?? entry.quantity ?? entry.qty ?? 0) || 0;
        revenue = Number(entry.revenue ?? entry.total ?? 0) || 0;
      }

      if (!rawName || (count <= 0 && revenue <= 0)) return;

      const avgUnit = count > 0 ? Math.round(revenue / count) : 0;
      const mergeKey = `${rawName.toLowerCase()}__${avgUnit}`;
      const prettyName = rawName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + rawName.slice(1);

      if (!mergedByName[mergeKey]) {
        mergedByName[mergeKey] = {
          id: `${mergeKey}_${idx}`,
          name: prettyName,
          count: 0,
          revenue: 0
        };
      }
      mergedByName[mergeKey].count += count;
      mergedByName[mergeKey].revenue += revenue;
    });

    const mapped = Object.values(mergedByName).map(item => ({
      ...item,
      avgPrice: item.count > 0 ? Math.round(item.revenue / item.count) : 0
    }));

    const sorted = mapped.sort((a, b) => {
      if (sortBy === 'revenue') {
        return b.revenue - a.revenue || b.count - a.count;
      }
      return b.count - a.count || b.revenue - a.revenue;
    });

    return showAllRanks ? sorted.slice(0, 15) : sorted.slice(0, 5);
  }, [computedPopularSource, sortBy, showAllRanks]);

  // Umumiy ko'rsatkichlar (Progress bar va pastki xulosa uchun)
  const summaryStats = useMemo(() => {
    const totalPortions = normalizedItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.count, 0);
    const totalTopRevenue = normalizedItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.revenue, 0);
    const maxCount = normalizedItems.reduce((max, item) => Math.max(max, item.count), 0);
    const maxRevenue = normalizedItems.reduce((max, item) => Math.max(max, item.revenue), 0);

    return {
      totalPortions,
      totalTopRevenue,
      maxCount: maxCount || 1,
      maxRevenue: maxRevenue || 1
    };
  }, [normalizedItems]);

  const activeDateBadgeLabel = useMemo(() => {
    switch (itemDateMode) {
      case 'today': return 'Bugun';
      case 'week': return 'Oxirgi 7 kun';
      case 'month': return 'Shu oy';
      case 'last_month': return "O'tgan oy";
      case 'custom': return `${customStartDate} â€” ${customEndDate}`;
      default: return 'Sana oralig\'i';
    }
  }, [itemDateMode, customStartDate, customEndDate]);

  // ==========================================
  // 3. O'RINLAR UCHUN MAXSUS RANGLAR VA IKONALAR
  // ==========================================
  const getRankBadge = (index) => {
    switch (index) {
      case 0:
        return {
          bg: 'bg-yellow-400',
          text: 'text-yellow-950',
          shadow: 'shadow-yellow-400/40',
          bar: 'bg-amber-500',
          icon: 'ðŸ‘‘'
        };
      case 1:
        return {
          bg: 'bg-gray-300 dark:bg-gray-600',
          text: 'text-gray-900 dark:text-white',
          shadow: 'shadow-gray-400/30',
          bar: 'bg-indigo-500',
          icon: 'ðŸ¥ˆ'
        };
      case 2:
        return {
          bg: 'bg-amber-700',
          text: 'text-white',
          shadow: 'shadow-amber-700/40',
          bar: 'bg-amber-700',
          icon: 'ðŸ¥‰'
        };
      default:
        return {
          bg: 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700',
          text: 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300',
          shadow: 'shadow-none',
          bar: 'bg-emerald-500',
          icon: `#${index + 1}`
        };
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 4. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4 md:gap-6 mt-4 md:mt-6">
      
      {/* =======================================================
          CHAP TOMON: TOP TAOMLAR REYTINGI + SANA ORALIG'I FILTRI
          ======================================================= */}
      <div className="xl:col-span-2 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 flex flex-col justify-between">
        
        <div>
          {/* Sarlavha va 3 ta Boshqaruv Punkti: Soni | Tushumi | Sana oralig'i */}
          <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row justify-between items-start lg:items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-lg md:text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                <span>ðŸ”¥</span> Top-{showAllRanks ? '15' : '5'} Eng Xaridorgir Taomlar
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mt-1">
                Restoraningizning eng ko'p sotilgan va eng daromadli pozitsiyalari
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Soni bo'yicha | Tushumi bo'yicha | ðŸ“… Sana oralig'i */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 p-1.5 rounded-2xl w-full lg:w-auto">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSortBy('count')}
                className={`flex-1 sm:flex-initial px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition whitespace-nowrap ${
                  sortBy === 'count'
                    ? 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 shadow-sm'

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';

// =========================================================
// BUYURTMA VA XARAJAT SANASINI UNIVERSAL O'QISH YORDAMCHISI
// =========================================================
const parseRecordTimeMs = (record) => {
  if (!record) return Date.now();

  if (record.closedAtMs && !isNaN(Number(record.closedAtMs))) return Number(record.closedAtMs);
  if (record.createdAt && !isNaN(Number(record.createdAt))) return Number(record.createdAt);

  const rawDateStr = String(record.closeTime || record.archiveDate || record.date || '').trim();
  if (rawDateStr) {
    const dmyMatch = rawDateStr.match(/(\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{4})/);
    if (dmyMatch) {
      const day = Number(dmyMatch[1]);
      const month = Number(dmyMatch[2]) - 1;
      const year = Number(dmyMatch[3]);
      const timeMatch = rawDateStr.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
      const hh = timeMatch ? Number(timeMatch[1]) : 12;
      const mm = timeMatch ? Number(timeMatch[2]) : 0;
      const constructed = new Date(year, month, day, hh, mm).getTime();
      if (!isNaN(constructed)) return constructed;
    }

    const parsed = Date.parse(rawDateStr);
    if (!isNaN(parsed)) return parsed;
  }

  const idMatch = String(record.id || '').match(/\d{13}/);
  if (idMatch) {
    const idMs = Number(idMatch[0]);
    if (idMs > 1600000000000 && idMs <= Date.now() + 86400000) return idMs;
  }

  return Date.now();
};

const toDateInputStr = (dateObj) => {
  const y = dateObj.getFullYear();
  const m = String(dateObj.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(dateObj.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
};

// Standart maosh stavkasi (Agar xodim uchun alohida kiritilmagan bo'lsa)
const DEFAULT_WAITER_RULE = {
  serviceSharePercent: 100, // Yig'ilgan xizmat haqining necha % ofitsiantga tegishli (Masalan: 100% yoki 70%)
  salesBonusPercent: 0,     // Sof taom savdosidan qo'shimcha % bonus
  dailyShiftRate: 0,        // 1 ta chiqqan ish kuni (smena) uchun fiks stavka (so'm)
  fixedMonthlySalary: 0,    // Oylik fiks oklad (so'm)
  manualBonus: 0,           // Rahbar tomonidan qo'shimcha mukofot (+)
  manualDeduction: 0,       // Qo'shimcha ushlanma / avans / jarima (-)
  note: ''
};

const readLocalJSON = (key, fallback) => {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
};

export default function StatsWaiterPayroll({
  scopedOrders = [],
  allOrders = [],
  scopedExpenses = [],
  allExpenses = [],
  users = [],
  serviceFee = 10,
  activePeriodLabel = 'Joriy davr',
  addAuditLog
}) {
  // =========================================================
  // 1. XODIMLAR MAOSH QOIDALARI XOTIRASI (LOCALSTORAGE BILAN)
  // =========================================================
  const [payrollRules, setPayrollRules] = useState(() =>
    readLocalJSON('pos_waiter_payroll_rules', {})
  );
  const [localUsers] = useState(() => readLocalJSON('pos_users', []));

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem('pos_waiter_payroll_rules', JSON.stringify(payrollRules));
    } catch {
      // ignore storage errors
    }
  }, [payrollRules]);

  // =========================================================
  // 2. SANA ORALIG'I VA QIDIRUV STATE'LARI
  // =========================================================
  // 'sync' (StatsMode filtri bilan bir xil) | 'today' | 'week' | 'month' | 'last_month' | 'custom'
  const [dateMode, setDateMode] = useState('sync');
  const [isDateBarOpen, setIsDateBarOpen] = useState(false);

  const todayStr = useMemo(() => toDateInputStr(new Date()), []);
  const firstOfMonthStr = useMemo(() => {
    const d = new Date();
    d.setDate(1);
    return toDateInputStr(d);
  }, []);

  const [customStart, setCustomStart] = useState(firstOfMonthStr);
  const [customEnd, setCustomEnd] = useState(todayStr);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  // Tahrirlash va Cheklarni ko'rish modallari
  const [editingWaiter, setEditingWaiter] = useState(null);
  const [ruleForm, setRuleForm] = useState(DEFAULT_WAITER_RULE);
  const [inspectingWaiter, setInspectingWaiter] = useState(null);

  const safeUsers = useMemo(() => {
    if (Array.isArray(users) && users.length > 0) return users;
    return Array.isArray(localUsers) ? localUsers : [];
  }, [users, localUsers]);

  // =========================================================
  // 3. TANLANGAN DAVR BO'YICHA CHEKLAR VA AVANSLARNI AJRATISH
  // =========================================================
  const { filteredOrders, filteredExpenses, currentPeriodText } = useMemo(() => {
    if (dateMode === 'sync') {
      return {
        filteredOrders: Array.isArray(scopedOrders) ? scopedOrders : [],
        filteredExpenses: Array.isArray(scopedExpenses) ? scopedExpenses : [],
        currentPeriodText: activePeriodLabel
      };
    }

    const now = new Date();
    let startMs = 0;
    let endMs = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 23, 59, 59, 999).getTime();
    let label = 'Tanlangan davr';

    if (dateMode === 'today') {
      startMs = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 0, 0, 0, 0).getTime();
      label = `Bugun (${now.toLocaleDateString()})`;
    } else if (dateMode === 'week') {
      const w = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - 6, 0, 0, 0, 0);
      startMs = w.getTime();
      label = 'Oxirgi 7 kun';
    } else if (dateMode === 'month') {
      const m = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);
      startMs = m.getTime();
      label = 'Shu oy';
    } else if (dateMode === 'last_month') {
      const lmStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);
      const lmEnd = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 0, 23, 59, 59, 999);
      startMs = lmStart.getTime();
      endMs = lmEnd.getTime();
      label = "O'tgan oy";
    } else if (dateMode === 'custom') {
      if (customStart) {
        const [sy, sm, sd] = customStart.split('-').map(Number);
        startMs = new Date(sy, sm - 1, sd, 0, 0, 0, 0).getTime();
      }
      if (customEnd) {
        const [ey, em, ed] = customEnd.split('-').map(Number);
        endMs = new Date(ey, em - 1, ed, 23, 59, 59, 999).getTime();
      }
      label = `${customStart} dan ${customEnd} gacha`;
    }

    const sourceOrders = Array.isArray(allOrders) && allOrders.length > 0 ? allOrders : scopedOrders;
    const sourceExpenses = Array.isArray(allExpenses) && allExpenses.length > 0 ? allExpenses : scopedExpenses;

    const matchingOrders = (Array.isArray(sourceOrders) ? sourceOrders : []).filter(o => {
      if (!o || !["To'langan", 'Arxiv'].includes(o.status)) return false;
      const t = parseRecordTimeMs(o);
      return t >= startMs && t <= endMs;
    });

    const matchingExpenses = (Array.isArray(sourceExpenses) ? sourceExpenses : []).filter(e => {
      if (!e) return false;
      const t = parseRecordTimeMs(e);
      return t >= startMs && t <= endMs;
    });

    return {
      filteredOrders: matchingOrders,
      filteredExpenses: matchingExpenses,
      currentPeriodText: label
    };
  }, [
    dateMode,
    scopedOrders,
    allOrders,
    scopedExpenses,
    allExpenses,
    activePeriodLabel,
    customStart,
    customEnd
  ]);

  // =========================================================
  // 4. HAR BIR OFITSIANT UCHUN ULUSH VA MAOSH HISOB-KITOBI
  // =========================================================
  const waiterPayrollRows = useMemo(() => {
    const map = {};

    // 1. Ro'yxatdan o'tgan barcha ofitsiantlarni bazaga kiritib olamiz
    safeUsers.forEach(u => {
      if (!u || !u.name) return;
      const roleLower = String(u.role || '').toLowerCase();
      if (roleLower === 'waiter' || roleLower === 'ofitsiant') {
        const key = String(u.name).trim();
        map[key] = {
          waiterName: key,
          role: 'Ofitsiant',
          ordersCount: 0,
          portionsCount: 0,
          workedDaysSet: new Set(),
          subTotalSales: 0,
          totalRevenue: 0,
          totalServiceFeeGenerated: 0,
          ordersList: []
        };
      }
    });

    // 2. Yopilgan cheklarni ofitsiantlar bo'yicha taqsimlaymiz
    filteredOrders.forEach(order => {
      if (!order) return;
      const rawWaiter = String(order.waiterName || order.waiter || 'Ofitsiant').trim();

      if (!map[rawWaiter]) {
        map[rawWaiter] = {
          waiterName: rawWaiter,
          role: 'Ofitsiant',
          ordersCount: 0,
          portionsCount: 0,
          workedDaysSet: new Set(),
          subTotalSales: 0,
          totalRevenue: 0,
          totalServiceFeeGenerated: 0,
          ordersList: []
        };
      }

      const items = Array.isArray(order.items) ? order.items : [];
      const calcSub = items.reduce((s, it) => {
        const p = Number(it?.price ?? it?.variants?.[0]?.price ?? 0) || 0;
        const q = Number(it?.quantity ?? it?.qty ?? 1) || 0;
        return s + p * q;
      }, 0);

      const sub = Number(order.subTotal ?? order.subtotal ?? calcSub) || 0;
      const isTakeaway = order.table === 'Olib ketish';
      const pct = isTakeaway ? 0 : Number(order.serviceFeePercent ?? order.serviceFee ?? serviceFee) || 0;
      const feeAmt = Number(order.serviceFeeAmount ?? Math.round((sub * pct) / 100)) || 0;
      const finalAmt = Number(order.finalTotal ?? order.total ?? (sub + feeAmt)) || 0;
      const portions = items.reduce((s, it) => s + (Number(it?.quantity ?? it?.qty ?? 1) || 0), 0);

      const dayKey = toDateInputStr(new Date(parseRecordTimeMs(order)));

      map[rawWaiter].ordersCount += 1;
      map[rawWaiter].portionsCount += portions;
      map[rawWaiter].workedDaysSet.add(dayKey);
      map[rawWaiter].subTotalSales += sub;
      map[rawWaiter].totalRevenue += finalAmt;
      map[rawWaiter].totalServiceFeeGenerated += feeAmt;
      map[rawWaiter].ordersList.push({
        ...order,
        computedSub: sub,
        computedFee: feeAmt,
        computedFinal: finalAmt,
        computedPortions: portions
      });
    });

    // 3. Har bir ofitsiant uchun Maosh Qoidalari va Kassadan olingan Avanslarni hisoblaymiz
    const rows = Object.values(map).map(w => {
      const rule = {
        ...DEFAULT_WAITER_RULE,
        ...(payrollRules[w.waiterName] || {})
      };

      const workedDaysCount = w.workedDaysSet.size;

      // a) Xizmat haqidan ulushi (Masalan: yig'ilgan 10% xizmat haqining 100% yoki 70% qismi)
      const serviceShareEarned = Math.round(
        (w.totalServiceFeeGenerated * (Number(rule.serviceSharePercent) || 0)) / 100
      );

      // b) Taomlar savdosidan qo'shimcha bonus %
      const salesBonusEarned = Math.round(
        (w.subTotalSales * (Number(rule.salesBonusPercent) || 0)) / 100
      );

      // c) Ishlagan smenalari (kunlari) uchun kunlik stavka + Oylik oklad
      const shiftSalaryEarned = workedDaysCount * (Number(rule.dailyShiftRate) || 0);
      const fixedSalary = Number(rule.fixedMonthlySalary) || 0;

      // d) Kassadan "Avans" sifatida shu xodim nomiga yozilgan chiqimlarni avtomatik topish
      const nameLower = w.waiterName.toLowerCase();
      const cashierAdvances = filteredExpenses
        .filter(exp => {
          if (!exp || exp.category !== 'Avans') return false;
          const rec = String(exp.recipient || '').toLowerCase();
          const note = String(exp.note || '').toLowerCase();
          return rec.includes(nameLower) || note.includes(nameLower);
        })
        .reduce((sum, exp) => sum + (Number(exp.amount) || 0), 0);

      const manualBonus = Number(rule.manualBonus) || 0;
      const manualDeduction = Number(rule.manualDeduction) || 0;
      const totalDeductions = cashierAdvances + manualDeduction;

      // Jami hisoblangan maosh (Qo'lga tegadigan sof summa)
      const grossEarned =
        serviceShareEarned +
        salesBonusEarned +
        shiftSalaryEarned +
        fixedSalary +
        manualBonus;

      const netPayableSalary = grossEarned - totalDeductions;

      return {
        ...w,
        workedDaysCount,
        rule,
        serviceShareEarned,
        salesBonusEarned,
        shiftSalaryEarned,
        fixedSalary,
        cashierAdvances,
        manualBonus,
        manualDeduction,
        totalDeductions,
        grossEarned,
        netPayableSalary
      };
    });

    // Qidiruv va Saralash (Eng ko'p maosh/savdo qilganlar tepada)
    return rows
      .filter(r => {
        if (!searchQuery.trim()) return true;
        return r.waiterName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase());
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.totalRevenue - a.totalRevenue || b.netPayableSalary - a.netPayableSalary);
  }, [safeUsers, filteredOrders, filteredExpenses, payrollRules, serviceFee, searchQuery]);

  // Umumiy Jami Ko'rsatkichlar (Footer va Tepasi uchun)
  const totalsSummary = useMemo(() => {
    return waiterPayrollRows.reduce(
      (acc, r) => {
        acc.totalOrders += r.ordersCount;
        acc.totalSales += r.totalRevenue;
        acc.totalServiceCollected += r.totalServiceFeeGenerated;
        acc.totalWaiterServiceShare += r.serviceShareEarned;
        acc.totalAdvances += r.totalDeductions;
        acc.totalNetPayable += r.netPayableSalary;
        return acc;
      },
      {
        totalOrders: 0,
        totalSales: 0,
        totalServiceCollected: 0,
        totalWaiterServiceShare: 0,
        totalAdvances: 0,
        totalNetPayable: 0
      }
    );
  }, [waiterPayrollRows]);

  // =========================================================
  // 5. MODAL HANDLERLARI VA EKSPORT
  // =========================================================
  const handleOpenEditRule = (waiterRow) => {
    setEditingWaiter(waiterRow.waiterName);
    setRuleForm({
      ...DEFAULT_WAITER_RULE,
      ...(payrollRules[waiterRow.waiterName] || {})
    });
  };

  const handleSaveRule = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!editingWaiter) return;

    const cleaned = {
      serviceSharePercent: Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(ruleForm.serviceSharePercent) || 0)),
      salesBonusPercent: Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(ruleForm.salesBonusPercent) || 0)),
      dailyShiftRate: Math.max(0, Number(ruleForm.dailyShiftRate) || 0),
      fixedMonthlySalary: Math.max(0, Number(ruleForm.fixedMonthlySalary) || 0),
      manualBonus: Math.max(0, Number(ruleForm.manualBonus) || 0),
      manualDeduction: Math.max(0, Number(ruleForm.manualDeduction) || 0),
      note: String(ruleForm.note || '').trim()
    };

    setPayrollRules(prev => ({
      ...(prev || {}),
      [editingWaiter]: cleaned
    }));

    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
      addAuditLog(
        'Ofitsiant maosh stavkasi yangilandi',
        `${editingWaiter}: Xizmat ulushi ${cleaned.serviceSharePercent}%, Kunlik: ${cleaned.dailyShiftRate.toLocaleString()} s, Oklad: ${cleaned.fixedMonthlySalary.toLocaleString()} s`
      );
    }

    setEditingWaiter(null);
  };

  const handleExportPayrollTxt = () => {
    const lines = [
      `========================================================`,
      `ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³ OFITSIANTLAR ULUSHI VA OYLIK MAOSH VEDOMOSTI`,
      `Davr: ${currentPeriodText}`,
      `Sana: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
      `========================================================\n`,
      ...waiterPayrollRows.map(
        (r, i) =>
          `${i + 1}. ${r.waiterName} (${r.workedDaysCount} smena | ${r.ordersCount} ta chek | ${r.portionsCount} pors)\n` +
          `   â€¢ Jami savdosi: ${r.totalRevenue.toLocaleString()} so'm\n` +
          `   â€¢ Yig'ilgan xizmat haqi: ${r.totalServiceFeeGenerated.toLocaleString()} so'm\n` +
          `   â€¢ Xizmatdan ulushi (${r.rule.serviceSharePercent}%): +${r.serviceShareEarned.toLocaleString()} so'm\n` +
          (r.salesBonusEarned > 0 ? `   â€¢ Savdodan bonus (${r.rule.salesBonusPercent}%): +${r.salesBonusEarned.toLocaleString()} so'm\n` : '') +
          (r.shiftSalaryEarned + r.fixedSalary > 0 ? `   â€¢ Oklad / Smena puli: +${(r.shiftSalaryEarned + r.fixedSalary).toLocaleString()} so'm\n` : '') +
          (r.manualBonus > 0 ? `   â€¢ Qo'shimcha mukofot: +${r.manualBonus.toLocaleString()} so'm\n` : '') +
          (r.totalDeductions > 0 ? `   â€¢ Olingan Avans / Jarima: -${r.totalDeductions.toLocaleString()} so'm\n` : '') +
          `   ðŸ’° QO'LGA TEGADIGAN SOF MAOSH: ${r.netPayableSalary.toLocaleString()} so'm\n`
      ),
      `--------------------------------------------------------`,
      `JAMI TO'LANADIGAN MAOSH FONDI: ${totalsSummary.totalNetPayable.toLocaleString()} so'm`
    ].join('\n');

    const blob = new Blob([lines], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `ofitsiantlar-maoshi-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.txt`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  // =========================================================
  // 6. RENDER (UI JADVAL VA MODALLAR)
  // =========================================================
  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-5 md:p-6 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border-2 border-amber-100 dark:border-amber-900/40 space-y-5">
      
      {/* 1. TEPA SARLAVHA VA BOSHQARUV TUGMALARI */}
      <div className="flex flex-col xl:flex-row justify-between items-start xl:items-center gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shadow-md shadow-amber-500/25 shrink-0">
            ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-lg md:text-xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white leading-tight">
              Ofitsiantlar Ulushi va Oylik Maosh Hisoblagichi (HR Payroll)
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
              Faol davr: <strong className="text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">{currentPeriodText}</strong> â€¢ Har bir xodimning xizmat haqi ulushi, okladi va avanslari
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 w-full xl:w-auto justify-between xl:justify-end">
          {/* Qidiruv */}
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="ðŸ” Ofitsiant ismi..."
            className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-xs font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500 w-full sm:w-44"
          />

          {/* Sana oralig'i tugmasi */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsDateBarOpen(prev => !prev)}
            className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl font-black text-xs transition flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              dateMode !== 'sync' || isDateBarOpen
                ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'
            }`}
          >
            <span>ðŸ“…</span>
            <span>{dateMode === 'sync' ? 'Maosh davri' : currentPeriodText}</span>
            <span className="text-[10px]">{isDateBarOpen ? 'â–²' : 'â–¼'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Vedomost yuklab olish */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleExportPayrollTxt}
            className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 font-black text-xs border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/50 transition active:scale-95 whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            ðŸ“¥ Vedomost (.txt)
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. OCHILUVCHI SANA ORALIG'I PANELI */}
      {isDateBarOpen && (
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50/70 dark:bg-gray-900/80 border border-amber-200 dark:border-gray-700 space-y-3 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-400">
              ðŸ“… Maosh va ulushlarni qaysi davr uchun hisoblaymiz?
            </span>
            {dateMode !== 'sync' && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDateMode('sync')}
                className="text-[11px] font-black text-red-500 hover:underline"
              >
                âœ• Yuqoridagi asosiy filtr bilan birxillashtirish
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {[
              { id: 'sync', label: 'ðŸ”„ Umumiy filtr bilan birga' },
              { id: 'today', label: 'â˜€ï¸ Bugun (Kunlik ulush)' },
              { id: 'week', label: 'ðŸ“† Oxirgi 7 kun (Haftalik)' },
              { id: 'month', label: 'ðŸ—“ Shu oy (Oylik maosh)' },
              { id: 'last_month', label: "âª O'tgan oy" },
              { id: 'custom', label: "ðŸŽ¯ Sanadan â€” Sanagacha" }
            ].map(tab => (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDateMode(tab.id)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition ${
                  dateMode === tab.id
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700'
                }`}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {dateMode === 'custom' && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-500 mb-1">
                  Boshlanish sanasi (... dan)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={customStart}
                  onChange={e => setCustomStart(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-amber-300 dark:border-gray-700 text-xs font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-500 mb-1">
                  Tugash sanasi (... gacha)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={customEnd}
                  onChange={e => setCustomEnd(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-amber-300 dark:border-gray-700 text-xs font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 3. QISQACHA FONDLAR XULOSASI (4 TA MINI-KARTA) */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/60 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-400 block">
            ðŸ›Ž Jami Yig'ilgan Xizmat Haqi
          </span>
          <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
            {totalsSummary.totalServiceCollected.toLocaleString()} s
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/60 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-400 block">
            ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³ Ofitsiantlar Xizmat Ulushi
          </span>
          <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">
            +{totalsSummary.totalWaiterServiceShare.toLocaleString()} s
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/60 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-gray-400 block">
            ðŸ’¸ Olingan Avans va Ushlanmalar
          </span>
          <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-red-500">
            {totalsSummary.totalAdvances > 0 ? `-${totalsSummary.totalAdvances.toLocaleString()}` : '0'} s
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/20 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/50">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 block">
            ðŸ’° Jami Qo'lga Tegadigan Maosh
          </span>
          <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
            {totalsSummary.totalNetPayable.toLocaleString()} s
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4. ASOSIY OFITSIANTLAR MAOSH VA ULUSH JADVALI */}
      {waiterPayrollRows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-10 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/40 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-gray-400 font-bold text-sm">
          Tanlangan davrda ofitsiantlar sotuvi yoki xodimlar topilmadi.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/50 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
            <thead>
              <tr className="bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 border-b dark:border-gray-700 text-[11px] font-black text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                <th className="p-4">Ofitsiant</th>
                <th className="p-4 text-center">Smena / Cheklar</th>
                <th className="p-4">Jami Savdosi</th>
                <th className="p-4">Yig'ilgan Xizmat (10%)</th>
                <th className="p-4">Xizmatdan Ulushi</th>
                <th className="p-4">Oklad / Bonus</th>
                <th className="p-4">Avans / Jarima</th>
                <th className="p-4 text-right">Qo'lga Tegadigan Maosh</th>
                <th className="p-4 text-center">Amal</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-200/70 dark:divide-gray-700 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold">
              {waiterPayrollRows.map((row, index) => {
                const extraFixedAndBonus =
                  row.salesBonusEarned + row.shiftSalaryEarned + row.fixedSalary + row.manualBonus;

                return (
                  <tr
                    key={row.waiterName}
                    onClick={() => setInspectingWaiter(row)}
                    className="hover:bg-white dark:hover:bg-gray-800 transition cursor-pointer group"
                    title="Bosib shu ofitsiant yopgan barcha cheklarni ko'ring"
                  >
                    {/* 1. Ofitsiant ismi */}
                    <td className="p-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-900/40 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 font-black text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                          #{index + 1}
                        </span>
                        <div>
                          <p className="font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white text-sm sm:text-base group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition">
                            {row.waiterName}
                          </p>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400">
                            Ulush stavkasi: {row.rule.serviceSharePercent}% xizmatdan
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    {/* 2. Smena va Cheklar soni */}
                    <td className="p-4 text-center">
                      <span className="font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white block">
                        {row.ordersCount} ta chek
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">
                        {row.workedDaysCount} kun â€¢ {row.portionsCount} pors
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    {/* 3. Jami Savdosi */}
                    <td className="p-4">
                      <span className="font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white block">
                        {row.totalRevenue.toLocaleString()} s
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">
                        Taomlar: {row.subTotalSales.toLocaleString()} s
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    {/* 4. Yig'ilgan Xizmat Haqi */}
                    <td className="p-4">
                      <span className="font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                        {row.totalServiceFeeGenerated.toLocaleString()} s
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    {/* 5. Ofitsiantning Xizmat Haqidan Sof Ulushi */}
                    <td className="p-4">
                      <span className="font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 block">
                        +{row.serviceShareEarned.toLocaleString()} s
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">
                        ({row.rule.serviceSharePercent}% ulush)
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    {/* 6. Oklad, Kunlik Smena va Savdo Bonusi */}
                    <td className="p-4">
                      {extraFixedAndBonus > 0 ? (
                        <div>
                          <span className="font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 block">
                            +{extraFixedAndBonus.toLocaleString()} s
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">
                            {row.rule.salesBonusPercent > 0 ? `Savdodan ${row.rule.salesBonusPercent}% ` : ''}
                            {row.fixedSalary > 0 ? 'â€¢ Oklad ' : ''}
                            {row.manualBonus > 0 ? 'â€¢ Mukofot' : ''}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-gray-400 text-xs">0 s</span>
                      )}
                    </td>

                    {/* 7. Avans va Jarimalar */}
                    <td className="p-4">
                      {row.totalDeductions > 0 ? (
                        <div>
                          <span className="font-black text-red-500 block">
                            -{row.totalDeductions.toLocaleString()} s
                          </span>
                          {row.cashierAdvances > 0 && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">

        />

        <TableQrModal
          table={qrModalTable}
          cafeName={cafeName}
          onClose={() => setQrModalTable(null)}
        />
      )}
    </>

export default function WaiterCart({
  selectedTable,
  table,
  cart = [],
  setCart,
  currentTableOrder,
  activeOrder,
  existingOrder,
  serviceFee = 10,
  onUpdateQuantity,
  updateQuantity,
  updateQty,
  onRemoveItem,
  removeFromCart,
  onUpdateNote,
  updateItemNote,
  onSubmitOrder,
  submitOrder,
  handleSendOrder,
  onMarkAsDelivered,
  markAsServed,
  onLockPreCheck,
  onCancelEntireOrder
}) {

  const resolvedTable = selectedTable || table || '1-Stol';
  const resolvedOrder = currentTableOrder || activeOrder || existingOrder || null;

  const handleQtyChange = onUpdateQuantity || updateQuantity || updateQty;
  const handleRemove = onRemoveItem || removeFromCart;
  const handleNoteChange = onUpdateNote || updateItemNote;
  const handleSend = onSubmitOrder || submitOrder || handleSendOrder;
  const handleDeliver = onMarkAsDelivered || markAsServed;

  const cartSummary = useMemo(() => {
    const subTotal = safeCart.reduce((sum, item) => {
      if (!item) return sum;
      const p = Number(item.price ?? item.variants?.[0]?.price ?? 0) || 0;
      const q = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 0;
      return sum + p * q;
    }, 0);

      if (!item) return sum;
      return sum + (Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 0);

    const feePct = isTakeaway ? 0 : Number(serviceFee) || 0;

    const finalTotal = subTotal + feeAmount;

    return {
      subTotal,
      totalPortions,
      feePct,
      feeAmount,
      finalTotal
    };

    if (typeof handleQtyChange === 'function') {
      handleQtyChange(itemKey, delta);

        (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [])
          .map(it => {
            const k = it.cartItemId || it.id || it.name;

          .filter(Boolean)
      );
    }

    if (typeof handleRemove === 'function') {
      handleRemove(itemKey);
      return;
    }
    changeItemQty(itemKey, -9999);
  };

      handleNoteChange(itemKey, text);
      return;

        (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(it => {
          const k = it.cartItemId || it.id || it.name;
          return k === itemKey ? { ...it, note: text } : it;
        })
      );
    }
  };

    <div className="w-full lg:w-96 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col justify-between min-h-0 overflow-hidden shrink-0">
      
      {/* 1. SAVATCHA TEPASI VA PRE-CHEK QULF INDIKATORI */}

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <h3 className="font-black text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span>ðŸ›’</span> Buyurtma Savatchasi
              </h3>

                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/40 text-red-600 dark:text-red-300 font-black text-[10px] uppercase border border-red-200 dark:border-red-800">
                  ðŸ”’ Pre-chek ({preCheckCount}x)
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 mt-0.5">
              ðŸª‘ {resolvedTable} â€¢ {cartSummary.totalPortions} porsiya taom
            </p>

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

export default function WaiterMenu({
  menuItems,
  menu,
  menuCategories,
  categories,
  selectedCategory: selectedCategoryProp,
  setSelectedCategory: setSelectedCategoryProp,
  activeCategory: activeCategoryProp,
  setActiveCategory: setActiveCategoryProp,
  searchQuery: searchQueryProp,
  setSearchQuery: setSearchQueryProp,
  searchTerm: searchTermProp,
  setSearchTerm: setSearchTermProp,
  cart = [],
  onAddToCart,
  addToCart
}) {
  // Ichki fallback state (Agar prop uzatilmagan bo'lsa ham mustaqil ishlaydi)
  const [localCategory, setLocalCategory] = useState('Barchasi');
  const [localSearch, setLocalSearch] = useState('');

  const currentCategory = selectedCategoryProp ?? activeCategoryProp ?? localCategory;
  const updateCategory = setSelectedCategoryProp || setActiveCategoryProp || setLocalCategory;

  const currentSearch = searchQueryProp ?? searchTermProp ?? localSearch;
  const updateSearch = setSearchQueryProp || setSearchTermProp || setLocalSearch;

  const handleAddDish = onAddToCart || addToCart;

  const safeMenu = useMemo(() => {
    if (Array.isArray(menuItems) && menuItems.length > 0) return menuItems;
    if (Array.isArray(menu) && menu.length > 0) return menu;
    try {
      const saved = window.localStorage.getItem('pos_menu');
      if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved) || [];
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    return [];
  }, [menuItems, menu]);

  const safeCategories = useMemo(() => {
    const raw =
      Array.isArray(menuCategories) && menuCategories.length > 0
        ? menuCategories
        : Array.isArray(categories) && categories.length > 0
        ? categories
        : Array.from(new Set(safeMenu.map(i => i?.category).filter(Boolean)));
    return raw.includes('Barchasi') ? raw : ['Barchasi', ...raw];
  }, [menuCategories, categories, safeMenu]);

  // Filtrlash
  const filteredDishes = useMemo(() => {
    return safeMenu.filter(dish => {
      if (!dish) return false;
      if (currentCategory !== 'Barchasi' && dish.category !== currentCategory) {
        return false;
      }
      if (String(currentSearch || '').trim() !== '') {
        const q = String(currentSearch).trim().toLowerCase();
        return (
          String(dish.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          String(dish.category || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [safeMenu, currentCategory, currentSearch]);

  // Savatchada qaysi taomdan nechta borligini hisoblash
  const cartCountByDishName = useMemo(() => {
    const map = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(cart)) return map;
    cart.forEach(c => {
      if (!c) return;
      const baseKey = c.baseName || c.name;
      const qty = Number(c.quantity ?? c.qty ?? 1) || 0;
      map[baseKey] = (map[baseKey] || 0) + qty;
      map[c.name] = (map[c.name] || 0) + qty;
    });
    return map;
  }, [cart]);

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
      
      {/* 1. QIDIRUV VA KATEGORIYALAR PANELI */}
      <div className="space-y-3 pb-4 border-b border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 shrink-0">
        <div className="relative">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={currentSearch}
            onChange={e => updateSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="ðŸ” Taom nomini qidiring..."
            className="w-full pl-4 pr-10 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-sm font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500 transition"
          />
          {currentSearch && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => updateSearch('')}
              className="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 font-black"
            >
              âœ•
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Toifalar (Categories) */}
        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto hide-scrollbar pb-1">
          {safeCategories.map(cat => {
            const isActive = currentCategory === cat;
            return (
              <button
                key={cat}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => updateCategory(cat)}
                className={`px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-xs sm:text-sm whitespace-nowrap transition active:scale-95 ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white shadow-md shadow-amber-500/25'
                    : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'
                }`}
              >
                {cat === 'Barchasi' ? 'ðŸ½ Barchasi' : cat}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. TAOMLAR RO'YXATI GRIDI */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto pt-4 pr-1">
        {filteredDishes.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center py-12 text-gray-400">
            <span className="text-5xl mb-2">ðŸ½ï¸</span>
            <p className="font-black text-sm">Taomlar topilmadi</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3.5">
            {filteredDishes.map(dish => {
              const isAvailable = dish.is_available !== false;
              const variants =
                Array.isArray(dish.variants) && dish.variants.length > 0
                  ? dish.variants
                  : [{ id: 'v_def', name: '1 porsiya', price: Number(dish.price || 0) }];

              const hasMultipleVariants = variants.length > 1;
              const mainPrice = Number(variants[0]?.price ?? dish.price ?? 0) || 0;
              const inCartCount = cartCountByDishName[dish.name] || 0;

              return (
                <div
                  key={dish.id}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (!isAvailable) return;
                    if (!hasMultipleVariants && typeof handleAddDish === 'function') {
                      handleAddDish(dish, variants[0]);
                    }
                  }}
                  className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 transition-all flex flex-col justify-between select-none relative ${
                    !isAvailable
                      ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900/40 border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800 opacity-60 cursor-not-allowed'
                      : inCartCount > 0

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import useSticky from '../hooks/useSticky';

// Ovozli bildirishnoma (Taom tayyor bo'lganda yoki mijoz chaqirganda)
const playWaiterBeep = () => {
  try {
    const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AudioCtx) return;
    const ctx = new AudioCtx();
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(784, now);
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1046.5, now + 0.15);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, now);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.5);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + 0.5);
  } catch {
    // ignore audio block
  }
};

const escapeHtml = (str) =>
  String(str ?? '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');

async function sendWaiterSecurityTelegram(linesArray) {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem('pos_telegram_config');
    if (!raw) return;
    const cfg = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!cfg || !cfg.enabled || cfg.notifyOnSecurity === false) return;

    const token = String(cfg.botToken || '').trim();
    const rawIds = String(cfg.chatId || '').trim();
    if (!token || !rawIds) return;

    const text = linesArray.filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    const chatIds = rawIds.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);

    await Promise.all(
      chatIds.map(id =>
        fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/sendMessage`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            chat_id: id,
            text,
            parse_mode: 'HTML',
            disable_web_page_preview: true
          })
        })
      )
    );
  } catch {
    // ignore offline error
  }
}

  const recordAuditLog = useCallback((action, details) => {

      })
    );
  }, [cart, currentTableOrder, selectedTable, currentUser?.name, resolvedCafeName, requestSecurityApproval, syncOrderInDatabase, recordAuditLog]);

    if (!currentTableOrder) return;
    const orderTotal = Number(currentTableOrder.finalTotal || currentTableOrder.total || 0);

      `ðŸ›‘ ${selectedTable} buyurtmasini to'liq bekor qilish va o'chirish (-${orderTotal.toLocaleString()} s)`,

          "Stol buyurtmasi to'liq bekor qilindi (PIN)",
          `${selectedTable} cheki (-${orderTotal.toLocaleString()} s) o'chirildi â€¢ Xodim: ${currentUser?.name || 'Ofitsiant'} â€¢ Sabab: ${reason}`
        );

        sendWaiterSecurityTelegram([
          `ðŸš¨ <b>STOL BUYURTMASI TO'LIQ BEKOR QILINDI (${escapeHtml(resolvedCafeName)})</b>`,
          `ðŸª‘ <b>Joy:</b> ${escapeHtml(selectedTable)}`,
          `ðŸ’° <b>Bekor bo'lgan summa:</b> <b>-${orderTotal.toLocaleString()} so'm</b>`,
          `ðŸ‘¤ <b>Ofitsiant:</b> ${escapeHtml(currentUser?.name || 'Ofitsiant')}`,
          `ðŸ“ <b>Sababi:</b> ${escapeHtml(reason)}`
        ]);
      }
    );
  }, [currentTableOrder, selectedTable, currentUser?.name, resolvedCafeName, requestSecurityApproval, syncOrderInDatabase, recordAuditLog]);

  const handleUpdateNote = useCallback((targetIdentifier, nextNote) => {
    setCart(prev =>
      prev.map((item, idx) => {
        if (!item) return item;
        const isMatch =
          item.cartItemId === targetIdentifier ||
          String(item.id) === String(targetIdentifier) ||
          item.name === targetIdentifier ||
          idx === targetIdentifier;
        return isMatch ? { ...item, note: nextNote } : item;
      })
    );
  }, []);

  // =========================================================
  const handleLockAndPrintPreCheck = () => {
    if (!currentTableOrder) return;

    const nowTime = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const nextPreCheckCount = (Number(currentTableOrder.preCheckCount) || 0) + 1;

    const updatedLockedOrder = {
      ...currentTableOrder,
      isPreCheckPrinted: true,
      preCheckTime: nowTime,
      preCheckCount: nextPreCheckCount,
      preCheckTotalSnapshot: currentTableOrder.finalTotal || currentTableOrder.total,
      paymentMethod: 'PRE-CHEK (HISOB)'
    };

    updateOrders(prev =>
      (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(o =>
        o && String(o.id) === String(currentTableOrder.id)
          ? {
              ...o,
              isPreCheckPrinted: true,
              preCheckTime: nowTime,
              preCheckCount: nextPreCheckCount,
              preCheckTotalSnapshot: o.finalTotal || o.total
            }
          : o
      )
    );

      'Ofitsiant Pre-chek chiqardi (Qulflandi)',
      `${selectedTable}: ${Number(currentTableOrder.finalTotal || currentTableOrder.total || 0).toLocaleString()} so'm (${nextPreCheckCount}-marta)`
    );

    const externalPrinter = onPrintReceipt || setReceiptToPrint;
    if (typeof externalPrinter === 'function') {
      externalPrinter({ order: updatedLockedOrder });
    } else {
      setLocalPreCheckPrintOrder(updatedLockedOrder);

    if (!selectedTable || !Array.isArray(cart) || cart.length === 0) return;

    const normalizedCart = cart.map((item, idx) => {
      const price = Number(item.price ?? item.variants?.[0]?.price ?? 0) || 0;
      const qty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 1;
      return {
        ...item,
        cartItemId: item.cartItemId || `ci_${item.id || idx}_${Date.now()}`,
        price,
        quantity: qty,
        qty,
        sentQty: qty

    const serviceFeeAmount = Math.round((subTotal * feePct) / 100);
    const totalAmount = subTotal + serviceFeeAmount;

    const nowDate = new Date().toLocaleDateString();
    const nowTime = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const activeBooking = reservations?.[selectedTable];

        (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(o =>

                subTotal,
                subtotal: subTotal,
                serviceFee: feePct,
                serviceFeePercent: feePct,
                serviceFeeAmount,

                finalTotal: totalAmount,
                status: 'Yangi'

        'Buyurtma yangilandi',

      );

        createdAt: Date.now(),

        waiterName: currentUser?.name || 'Ofitsiant',

        time: nowTime,
        status: 'Yangi',

        subTotal,
        subtotal: subTotal,
        serviceFee: feePct,
        serviceFeePercent: feePct,
        serviceFeeAmount,
        total: totalAmount,
        finalTotal: totalAmount,
        customerName: activeBooking?.customerName || '',
        customerPhone: activeBooking?.customerPhone || '',
        depositPaid: Number(activeBooking?.depositAmount || 0)
      };

      updateOrders(prev => [newOrder, ...(Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [])]);
      recordAuditLog(
        'Yangi buyurtma ochildi',
        `${selectedTable}: ${totalAmount.toLocaleString()} so'm (${normalizedCart.length} xil taom)`
      );
    }

    if (activeBooking) {
      setReservations(prev => {
        const next = { ...(prev || {}) };
        delete next[selectedTable];
        return next;
      });
    }

    setSelectedTable(null);
    setCart([]);
  };

  const handleMarkAsDelivered = () => {
    if (!currentTableOrder) return;
    updateOrders(prev =>
      (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(o =>
        o && String(o.id) === String(currentTableOrder.id)
          ? { ...o, status: 'Yetkazildi' }
          : o
      )
    );
    setSelectedTable(null);
    setCart([]);
  };

  const handleClearTableCall = (tableName) => {
    updateOrders(prev =>
      (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(o =>
        o && o.table === tableName ? { ...o, hasCall: false } : o
      )
    );
  };

  const handleSaveReservation = (tableName, bookingData) => {

    setReservations(prev => {

  const handleStartTableTimer = (tableName, config) => {
    setTableTimers(prev => ({

      [tableName]: {
        table: tableName,
        serviceTitle: config.serviceTitle,
        hourlyRate: config.hourlyRate,
        startTimeMs: Date.now(),
        accruedMs: 0,
        isPaused: false

      'VIP Taymer yoqildi',

    setTableTimers(prev => {

      if (!current) return prev;
      if (current.isPaused) {

      }
      const addedMs = Math.max(0, Date.now() - Number(current.startTimeMs));

        ...prev,
        [tableName]: { ...current, accruedMs: (Number(current.accruedMs) || 0) + addedMs, isPaused: true }

      );

      const nextFeeAmt = Math.round((nextSubTotal * feePct) / 100);

      updateOrders(prev =>
        (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(o =>

        waiterName: currentUser?.name || 'Ofitsiant',

        subTotal: lineItemPrice,

        serviceFee: feePct,
        serviceFeeAmount: feeAmt,
        total: lineItemPrice + feeAmt,
        finalTotal: lineItemPrice + feeAmt

      updateOrders(prev => [newOrder, ...(Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [])]);

      return next;

    return safeTables.filter(t => t !== 'Olib ketish' && !busySet.has(t));

    e.preventDefault();
    if (!transferOrder || !targetTransferTable) return;

    updateOrders(prev =>
      (Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : []).map(o =>
        o && String(o.id) === String(transferOrder.id) ? { ...o, table: targetTransferTable } : o
      )

    recordAuditLog("Stol ko'chirildi", `${transferOrder.table} -> ${targetTransferTable}`);
    setTransferOrder(null);
    setTargetTransferTable('');

    e.preventDefault();
    if (!splitOrder || !splitTargetTable) return;

    const remainingItems = [];
    const movedItems = [];

    originalItems.forEach((item, idx) => {
      const totalQty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 1;
      const moveQty = Math.min(totalQty, Math.max(0, Number(splitQuantities[idx]) || 0));

      if (moveQty > 0) movedItems.push({ ...item, quantity: moveQty, qty: moveQty, sentQty: moveQty });
      if (totalQty - moveQty > 0) {
        const remQ = totalQty - moveQty;
        remainingItems.push({ ...item, quantity: remQ, qty: remQ, sentQty: remQ });
      }
    });

    if (movedItems.length === 0 || remainingItems.length === 0) {
      alert("Chekni bo'lish uchun kamida 1 ta taomni yangi stolga ajrating va eski stolda ham kamida 1 ta taom qolsin!");
      return;
    }

    const calcTotals = (itemsList, feePct) => {
      const sub = itemsList.reduce((s, i) => s + (Number(i.price) || 0) * (Number(i.quantity) || 1), 0);
      const fee = Math.round((sub * feePct) / 100);
      return { sub, fee, total: sub + fee };

    const remTotals = calcTotals(remainingItems, feePct);
    const movTotals = calcTotals(movedItems, feePct);

      ...splitOrder,
      id: `${Date.now()}`,

        o && String(o.id) === String(splitOrder.id)
          ? {
              ...o,
              items: remainingItems,
              subTotal: remTotals.sub,

              total: remTotals.total,
              finalTotal: remTotals.total

    setSplitOrder(null);
    setSplitTargetTable('');
    setSplitQuantities({});
  };

  // =========================================================
  // 8. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)

          onFinishTimerAndAddToOrder={handleFinishTimerAndAddToOrder}

          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-4 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center">

                setSelectedTable(null);
