import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';

// =========================================================
// SOATBAY TARIF SHABLONLARI (VIP-KABINA / PS5 / BILLIARD)
// =========================================================
const TIMER_PRESETS = [
  { title: 'ðŸ‘‘ VIP-Kabina vaqti', rate: 50000 },
  { title: 'ðŸŽ® PlayStation 5 vaqti', rate: 40000 },
  { title: 'ðŸŽ± Billiard stoli', rate: 60000 },
  { title: 'ðŸŽ¤ Karaoke VIP xona', rate: 100000 }
];

export default function WaiterTableGrid({
  tables = [],
  orders = [],
  reservations = {},
  tableTimers = {},
  selectedTable,
  onSelectTable,
  onClearTableCall,
  onOpenTransferModal,
  onOpenSplitModal,
  onOpenQrModal,
  onSaveReservation,
  onCancelReservation,
  onConvertReservationToOrder,
  onStartTableTimer,
  onPauseResumeTableTimer,
  onFinishTimerAndAddToOrder,
  onCancelTableTimer
}) {
  // =========================================================
  // 1. FILTR, QIDIRUV VA JONLI VAQT STATE'LARI
  // =========================================================
  const [tableFilter, setTableFilter] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'busy' | 'free' | 'reserved' | 'timed' | 'calls'
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [nowMs, setNowMs] = useState(() => Date.now());

  // Bron qilish modali statelari
  const [bookingModalTable, setBookingModalTable] = useState(null);
  const [bookingForm, setBookingForm] = useState({
    customerName: '',
    customerPhone: '',
    time: '19:00',
    guestsCount: '4',
    depositAmount: '',
    note: ''
  });

  // VIP Soatbay Taymer boshlash modali statelari
  const [timerModalTable, setTimerModalTable] = useState(null);
  const [timerForm, setTimerForm] = useState({
    serviceTitle: TIMER_PRESETS[0].title,
    hourlyRate: String(TIMER_PRESETS[0].rate)
  });

  // Har 1 soniyada VIP-taymerlarni yangilab turish
  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setNowMs(Date.now());
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  const safeTables = useMemo(() => {
    const base = Array.isArray(tables) && tables.length > 0
      ? tables
      : Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => `${i + 1}-Stol`);
    return base.includes('Olib ketish') ? base : ['Olib ketish', ...base];
  }, [tables]);

  const safeOrders = Array.isArray(orders) ? orders : [];
  const safeReservations = reservations && typeof reservations === 'object' ? reservations : {};
  const safeTimers = tableTimers && typeof tableTimers === 'object' ? tableTimers : {};

  // =========================================================
  // 2. TAYMER VAQTINI VA PULINI ANIQ HISOBLASH YORDAMCHISI
  // =========================================================
  const computeTimerLiveStats = (timerObj) => {
    if (!timerObj || !timerObj.startTimeMs) return null;

    const accruedMs = Number(timerObj.accruedMs) || 0;
    const activeDeltaMs = timerObj.isPaused
      ? 0
      : Math.max(0, nowMs - Number(timerObj.startTimeMs));

    const totalElapsedSeconds = Math.floor((accruedMs + activeDeltaMs) / 1000);
    const hours = Math.floor(totalElapsedSeconds / 3600);
    const minutes = Math.floor((totalElapsedSeconds % 3600) / 60);
    const seconds = totalElapsedSeconds % 60;

    const formattedClock = `${String(hours).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    const durationHuman = hours > 0 ? `${hours} soat ${minutes} daq` : `${ Math.max(1, minutes) } daq`;

    const rate = Number(timerObj.hourlyRate) || 0;
    // Daqiqasigacha proporsional hisoblab, 500 so'mga yaxlitlaymiz (eng kami 1 daqiqa)
    const rawCost = (Math.max(60, totalElapsedSeconds) / 3600) * rate;
    const roundedCost = Math.max(1000, Math.round(rawCost / 500) * 500);

    return {
      totalElapsedSeconds,
      formattedClock,
      durationHuman,
      currentCost: roundedCost,
      hourlyRate: rate,
      serviceTitle: timerObj.serviceTitle || 'VIP-Kabina vaqti',
      isPaused: Boolean(timerObj.isPaused)
    };
  };

  // =========================================================
  // 3. HAR BIR STOL HOLATINI BIRLASHTIRISH
  // =========================================================
  const tableCardsData = useMemo(() => {
    return safeTables.map(tableName => {
      const activeOrder = safeOrders.find(
        o =>
          o &&
          o.table === tableName &&
          !["To'langan", 'Qarz', 'Arxiv'].includes(o.status)
      );

      const reservation = safeReservations[tableName] || null;
      const timerRaw = safeTimers[tableName] || null;
      const timerLive = computeTimerLiveStats(timerRaw);

      const items = Array.isArray(activeOrder?.items) ? activeOrder.items : [];
      const orderSubTotal = items.reduce((sum, it) => {
        const p = Number(it?.price ?? it?.variants?.[0]?.price ?? 0) || 0;
        const q = Number(it?.quantity ?? it?.qty ?? 1) || 0;
        return sum + p * q;
      }, 0);

      const hasCall = Boolean(activeOrder?.hasCall);
      const isBusy = Boolean(activeOrder);
      const isReserved = Boolean(reservation);
      const isTimed = Boolean(timerLive);

      return {
        tableName,
        isTakeaway: tableName === 'Olib ketish',
        activeOrder,
        reservation,
        timerLive,
        orderSubTotal,
        itemsCount: items.reduce((s, i) => s + (Number(i?.quantity ?? i?.qty ?? 1) || 0), 0),
        hasCall,
        isBusy,
        isReserved,
        isTimed
      };
    });
  }, [safeTables, safeOrders, safeReservations, safeTimers, nowMs]);

  // Ko'rsatkichlar (Badges)
  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      all: tableCardsData.length,
      busy: tableCardsData.filter(t => t.isBusy).length,
      free: tableCardsData.filter(t => !t.isBusy && !t.isReserved && !t.isTakeaway).length,
      reserved: tableCardsData.filter(t => t.isReserved).length,
      timed: tableCardsData.filter(t => t.isTimed).length,
      calls: tableCardsData.filter(t => t.hasCall).length
    };
  }, [tableCardsData]);

  // Filtrlangan stollar
  const filteredTables = useMemo(() => {
    return tableCardsData.filter(t => {
      if (tableFilter === 'busy' && !t.isBusy) return false;
      if (tableFilter === 'free' && (t.isBusy || t.isReserved || t.isTakeaway)) return false;
      if (tableFilter === 'reserved' && !t.isReserved) return false;
      if (tableFilter === 'timed' && !t.isTimed) return false;
      if (tableFilter === 'calls' && !t.hasCall) return false;

      if (searchQuery.trim() !== '') {
        const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
        const matchTable = t.tableName.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchWaiter = String(t.activeOrder?.waiterName || t.activeOrder?.waiter || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchGuest = String(t.reservation?.customerName || t.reservation?.customerPhone || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
        return matchTable || matchWaiter || matchGuest;
      }

      return true;
    });
  }, [tableCardsData, tableFilter, searchQuery]);

  // =========================================================
  // 4. BRON VA TAYMER FORMALARI HANDLERLARI
  // =========================================================
  const handleOpenBookingModal = (e, tableName) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const existing = safeReservations[tableName];
    setBookingModalTable(tableName);
    setBookingForm({
      customerName: existing?.customerName || '',
      customerPhone: existing?.customerPhone || '',
      time: existing?.time || '19:00',
      guestsCount: existing?.guestsCount ? String(existing.guestsCount) : '4',
      depositAmount: existing?.depositAmount ? String(existing.depositAmount) : '',
      note: existing?.note || ''
    });
  };

  const handleSubmitBooking = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!bookingModalTable) return;
    if (!bookingForm.customerName.trim() || !bookingForm.time.trim()) {
      alert("Iltimos, mehmon ismini va kelish vaqtini kiriting!");
      return;
    }

    if (typeof onSaveReservation === 'function') {
      onSaveReservation(bookingModalTable, {
        id: `res_${Date.now()}`,
        table: bookingModalTable,
        customerName: bookingForm.customerName.trim(),
        customerPhone: bookingForm.customerPhone.trim(),
        time: bookingForm.time.trim(),
        guestsCount: Math.max(1, Number(bookingForm.guestsCount) || 1),
        depositAmount: Math.max(0, Number(bookingForm.depositAmount) || 0),
        note: bookingForm.note.trim(),
        createdAt: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
      });
    }
    setBookingModalTable(null);
  };

  const handleOpenTimerModal = (e, tableName) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setTimerModalTable(tableName);
    setTimerForm({
      serviceTitle: TIMER_PRESETS[0].title,
      hourlyRate: String(TIMER_PRESETS[0].rate)
    });
  };

  const handleSubmitTimer = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!timerModalTable) return;
    const rate = Number(timerForm.hourlyRate);
    if (isNaN(rate) || rate <= 0) {
      alert("Iltimos, 1 soatlik tarif narxini to'g'ri kiriting!");
      return;
    }

    if (typeof onStartTableTimer === 'function') {
      onStartTableTimer(timerModalTable, {
        serviceTitle: timerForm.serviceTitle.trim() || 'VIP-Kabina vaqti',
        hourlyRate: Math.round(rate)
      });
    }
    setTimerModalTable(null);
  };

  const getStatusBadgeStyle = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'Yangi':
        return 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-900/50 dark:text-indigo-300';
      case 'Tayyorlanmoqda':
        return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-900/50 dark:text-amber-300';
      case 'Tayyor':
        return 'bg-emerald-500 text-white animate-bounce shadow-md shadow-emerald-500/30';
      case 'Yetkazildi':
        return 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 dark:bg-teal-900/40 dark:text-teal-300';
      default:
        return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700';
    }
  };

  // =========================================================
  // 5. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // =========================================================
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
      
      {/* YUQORI FILTR VA QIDIRUV PANELI */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-3.5 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 flex flex-col lg:flex-row justify-between items-stretch lg:items-center gap-3">
        
        {/* Filtr Tablari */}
        <div className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto hide-scrollbar pb-1 lg:pb-0">
          {[
            { id: 'all', label: `Barchasi (${counts.all})` },
            { id: 'busy', label: `ðŸ”´ Band (${counts.busy})` },
            { id: 'free', label: `ðŸŸ¢ Bo'sh (${counts.free})` },
            { id: 'reserved', label: `ðŸ“Œ Bronlar (${counts.reserved})` },
            { id: 'timed', label: `â±ï¸ VIP Vaqt (${counts.timed})` },
            ...(counts.calls > 0 ? [{ id: 'calls', label: `ðŸ”” Chaqiruv (${counts.calls})` }] : [])
          ].map(tab => (
            <button
              key={tab.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTableFilter(tab.id)}
              className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl font-black text-xs whitespace-nowrap transition active:scale-95 ${
                tableFilter === tab.id
                  ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'
              }`}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Stol yoki Mehmon qidiruvi */}
        <div className="relative w-full lg:w-64">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="ðŸ” Stol yoki bron mehmioni..."
            className="w-full pl-3.5 pr-8 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-xs font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
          />
          {searchQuery && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
              className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 font-black text-xs"
            >
              âœ•
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* STOLLAR VA KABINALAR GRIDI */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {filteredTables.map(card => {
          const {
            tableName,
            isTakeaway,
            activeOrder,
            reservation,
            timerLive,
            orderSubTotal,
            itemsCount,
            hasCall,
            isBusy,
            isReserved,
            isTimed
          } = card;

          const isSelected = selectedTable === tableName;

          return (
            <div
              key={tableName}
              onClick={() => typeof onSelectTable === 'function' && onSelectTable(tableName)}
              className={`rounded-3xl p-4 border-2 transition-all cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between relative overflow-hidden group ${
                hasCall
                  ? 'bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 border-red-500 ring-4 ring-red-500/20 animate-pulse'
                  : isSelected
                  ? 'bg-amber-50/90 dark:bg-amber-900/30 border-amber-500 ring-4 ring-amber-500/20 shadow-lg'
                  : isBusy
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-700/80 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md'
                  : isReserved
                  ? 'bg-purple-50/60 dark:bg-purple-950/25 border-purple-300 dark:border-purple-700/80 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md'
                  : isTimed
                  ? 'bg-cyan-50/60 dark:bg-cyan-950/25 border-cyan-400 dark:border-cyan-700 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 hover:border-emerald-400 dark:hover:border-emerald-600'
              }`}
            >
              {/* 1. TEPA QISM: STOL NOMI, STATUS VA QR TUGMASI */}
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2 mb-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <div
                      className={`w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-black shadow-sm ${
                        isTakeaway
                          ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                          : isBusy
                          ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white'
                          : isReserved
                          ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white'
                          : isTimed
                          ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white'
                          : 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/40 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isTakeaway ? 'ðŸ›ï¸' : isReserved ? 'ðŸ“Œ' : isTimed ? 'â±ï¸' : 'ðŸª‘'}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="font-black text-base sm:text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-white leading-tight">
                        {tableName}
                      </h3>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-400 block">
                        {isBusy
                          ? `ðŸ‘¤ ${activeOrder.waiterName || activeOrder.waiter || 'Ofitsiant'}`
                          : isReserved
                          ? `ðŸ•’ Bron: ${reservation.time}`
                          : isTimed
                          ? 'â±ï¸ Soatbay tarif faol'
                          : "ðŸŸ¢ Bo'sh stol"}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* QR-Kod va Buyurtma Statusi */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    {isBusy && activeOrder?.status && (
                      <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase ${getStatusBadgeStyle(activeOrder.status)}`}>
                        {activeOrder.status === 'Tayyor' ? 'ðŸ”” Tayyor!' : activeOrder.status}
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {!isTakeaway && typeof onOpenQrModal === 'function' && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          onOpenQrModal(tableName);
                        }}
                        className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 hover:bg-amber-100 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-black transition"
                        title="Stol QR-Kodi"
                      >
                        ðŸ“±
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* 2. MIJOZ QO'NG'IROQCHASI (AGAR CHAQIRGAN BO'LSA) */}
                {hasCall && (
                  <div className="mb-3 p-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-red-600 text-white flex justify-between items-center shadow-md">
                    <span className="text-xs font-black flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span>ðŸ””</span> Mijoz chaqirmoqda!
                    </span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={e => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        if (typeof onClearTableCall === 'function') onClearTableCall(tableName);
                      }}
                      className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white text-red-600 font-black text-[10px] uppercase"
                    >
                      Javob berildi âœ“
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 3. ðŸ“Œ BRON (RESERVATION) BLOKI */}
                {isReserved && reservation && (
                  <div className="mb-3 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-purple-100/80 dark:bg-purple-900/30 border border-purple-300 dark:border-purple-700 space-y-1.5">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-600 text-white">
                        ðŸ“Œ BRON: {reservation.time}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-black text-purple-800 dark:text-purple-300">
                        ðŸ‘¥ {reservation.guestsCount} kishi
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <p className="font-black text-xs sm:text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white truncate">
                      ðŸ‘¤ {reservation.customerName}
                      {reservation.customerPhone ? ` (${reservation.customerPhone})` : ''}
                    </p>

                    {Number(reservation.depositAmount) > 0 && (
                      <p className="text-[11px] font-black text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">
                        ðŸ’° Depozit: {Number(reservation.depositAmount).toLocaleString()} so'm
                      </p>
                    )}

                    {reservation.note && (
                      <p className="text-[10px] font-bold italic text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 truncate">
                        * {reservation.note}
                      </p>
                    )}

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 pt-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          if (typeof onConvertReservationToOrder === 'function') {
                            onConvertReservationToOrder(tableName, reservation);
                          }
                        }}
                        className="py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-black text-[10px] uppercase transition"
                      >
                        âœ… Mijoz keldi
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          if (typeof onCancelReservation === 'function') {
                            onCancelReservation(tableName);
                          }
                        }}
                        className="py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 hover:bg-red-50 text-red-600 font-black text-[10px] uppercase border border-red-200 dark:border-gray-700 transition"
                      >
                        âœ• Bekor qilish
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 4. â±ï¸ SOATBAY VIP-KABINA / TARIFIKATOR BLOKI */}
                {isTimed && timerLive && (
                  <div className="mb-3 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-cyan-100/80 dark:bg-cyan-900/30 border border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-700 space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-300 truncate pr-1">
                        {timerLive.serviceTitle}
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-mono font-black text-xs sm:text-sm px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-cyan-700 text-white">
                        {timerLive.formattedClock}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                        Tarif: {(timerLive.hourlyRate / 1000).toLocaleString()}k / soat
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-black text-sm text-cyan-800 dark:text-cyan-300">
                        +{timerLive.currentCost.toLocaleString()} s
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex gap-1.5 pt-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          if (typeof onPauseResumeTableTimer === 'function') {
                            onPauseResumeTableTimer(tableName);
                          }
                        }}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 font-black text-[10px] border border-cyan-300 dark:border-gray-600"
                      >
                        {timerLive.isPaused ? 'â–¶ï¸ Davom' : 'â¸ Pauza'}
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          if (typeof onFinishTimerAndAddToOrder === 'function') {
                            onFinishTimerAndAddToOrder(tableName, timerLive);
                          }
                        }}
                        className="flex-1 py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white font-black text-[10px] uppercase transition"
                      >
                        ðŸ§¾ Chekka qo'shish
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          if (typeof onCancelTableTimer === 'function') {
                            onCancelTableTimer(tableName);
                          }
                        }}
                        className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-600 font-black text-[10px]"
                        title="Taymerni o'chirish"
                      >
                        âœ•
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 5. FAOL BUYURTMA SUMMASI */}
                {isBusy && (
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50/70 dark:bg-gray-900/70 border border-amber-200/70 dark:border-gray-700 mb-3">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">
                      <span>ðŸ½ {itemsCount} porsiya taom</span>
                      <span>ðŸ•’ {activeOrder.time}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase text-gray-400">Joriy hisob:</span>
                      <span className="text-lg font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                        {(orderSubTotal + (timerLive?.currentCost || 0)).toLocaleString()} s
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* 6. PASTKI TEZKOR AMALLAR TUGMALARI */}
              <div className="pt-2 border-t border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700/80 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {isBusy ? (
                  <>
                    {typeof onOpenTransferModal === 'function' && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          onOpenTransferModal(activeOrder);
                        }}
                        className="flex-1 py-2 px-2 rounded-xl bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/40 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 font-black text-[11px] transition"
                      >
                        ðŸ”„ Ko'chirish
                      </button>
                    )}

                    {typeof onOpenSplitModal === 'function' && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          onOpenSplitModal(activeOrder);
                        }}
                        className="flex-1 py-2 px-2 rounded-xl bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 hover:bg-purple-100 dark:hover:bg-purple-900/40 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 font-black text-[11px] transition"
                      >
                        âœ‚ï¸ Bo'lish
                      </button>
                    )}

                    {!isTimed && !isTakeaway && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => handleOpenTimerModal(e, tableName)}
                        className="py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-cyan-900/30 hover:bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300 font-black text-[11px] transition"
                        title="Soatbay VIP taymerni yoqish"
                      >
                        â±ï¸ Vaqt
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  !isTakeaway && (
                    <>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={e => handleOpenBookingModal(e, tableName)}
                        className="flex-1 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-purple-50 dark:bg-purple-900/30 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300 font-black text-[11px] transition"
                      >
                        ðŸ“Œ {isReserved ? 'Bronni tahrirlash' : 'Bron qilish'}
                      </button>

                      {!isTimed && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={e => handleOpenTimerModal(e, tableName)}
                          className="flex-1 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-cyan-900/30 hover:bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300 font-black text-[11px] transition"
                        >
                          â±ï¸ VIP Taymer
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </>
                  )
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* =========================================================
          MODAL 1: ðŸ“Œ STOL BRON QILISH VA DEPOZIT MODALI
          ========================================================= */}
      {bookingModalTable && (
        <div
          onClick={() => setBookingModalTable(null)}
          className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/65 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-end md:items-center justify-center md:p-4 animate-fade-in"
        >
          <div
            onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 w-full md:max-w-md rounded-t-3xl md:rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700"
          >
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b pb-3 dark:border-gray-700">
              <h3 className="font-black text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                <span>ðŸ“Œ</span> {bookingModalTable} â€” Band Qilish (Bron)
              </h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setBookingModalTable(null)}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-500 font-black"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmitBooking} className="space-y-3.5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">
                  Mehmon Ismi *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  autoFocus
                  value={bookingForm.customerName}
                  onChange={e => setBookingForm(prev => ({ ...prev, customerName: e.target.value }))}
                  placeholder="Masalan: Sardor aka"
                  className="w-full p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-black text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-purple-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">
                    Kelish Vaqti *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="time"

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

// O'lchov birliklarini konvertatsiya qilish yordamchisi
const UNIT_FACTORS = {
  gr: { family: 'weight', factor: 1 },
  kg: { family: 'weight', factor: 1000 },
  tonna: { family: 'weight', factor: 1000000 },
  ml: { family: 'volume', factor: 1 },
  litr: { family: 'volume', factor: 1000 }
};

const normalizeUnit = (unitStr) => {
  const u = String(unitStr || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['g', 'gr', 'gram', 'gramm'].includes(u)) return 'gr';
  if (['kg', 'kilogram', 'kilogramm', 'kilo'].includes(u)) return 'kg';
  if (['t', 'ton', 'tonna', 'tn'].includes(u)) return 'tonna';
  if (['ml', 'millilitr'].includes(u)) return 'ml';
  if (['l', 'lt', 'litr', 'liter'].includes(u)) return 'litr';
  return u;
};

// Harakat miqdorini gr / kg / tonna ko'rinishida qo'shimcha ko'rsatish
const getConvertedSubtext = (rawDiff, unitStr) => {
  const norm = normalizeUnit(unitStr);
  const meta = UNIT_FACTORS[norm];
  if (!meta) return null;

  const numericVal = Math.abs(parseFloat(String(rawDiff).replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, '')));
  if (!numericVal || isNaN(numericVal)) return null;

  if (meta.family === 'weight') {
    const inGrams = numericVal * meta.factor;
    const inKg = Number((inGrams / 1000).toFixed(4));
    const inTons = Number((inGrams / 1000000).toFixed(6));
    const inGr = Number(inGrams.toFixed(2));
    return `${inTons} t â€¢ ${inKg.toLocaleString()} kg â€¢ ${inGr.toLocaleString()} gr`;
  }

  if (meta.family === 'volume') {
    const inMl = numericVal * meta.factor;
    const inLitr = Number((inMl / 1000).toFixed(4));
    return `${inLitr.toLocaleString()} litr â€¢ ${Math.round(inMl).toLocaleString()} ml`;
  }

  return null;
};

export default function WarehouseHistoryTab({ 
  inventoryLogs = [],
  setInventoryLogs,
  currentUser,
  requireAdminOverride,
  addAuditLog
}) {
  const safeLogs = Array.isArray(inventoryLogs) ? inventoryLogs : [];

  // ==========================================
  // 1. STATE'LAR (QIDIRUV VA FILTR)
  // ==========================================
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'kirim' | 'chiqim' | 'prep' | 'reviziya'

  // ==========================================
  // 2. LOG MA'LUMOTLARINI XAVFSIZ STANDARTLASHTIRISH
  // ==========================================
  const normalizeLogEntry = (log, index) => {
    if (!log) return null;

    const rawType = String(log.type || 'Harakat');
    const typeLower = rawType.toLowerCase();

    // Mahsulot nomi (itemName yoki productName)
    const itemName = log.itemName || log.productName || "Noma'lum mahsulot";
    // Izoh (note yoki reason)
    const note = log.note || log.reason || '-';
    // Foydalanuvchi
    const user = log.user || log.userName || 'Omborchi';
    // Birlik
    const unit = log.unit || '';

    // Miqdor o'zgarishi (diffAmount yoki amount)
    let diffStr = '';
    if (log.diffAmount !== undefined && log.diffAmount !== null && String(log.diffAmount).trim() !== '') {
      diffStr = String(log.diffAmount).trim();
    } else if (log.amount !== undefined && log.amount !== null) {
      const numAmt = Number(log.amount);
      if (!isNaN(numAmt)) {
        const isDeduction = typeLower.includes('chiqim') || typeLower.includes('sotuv');
        diffStr = isDeduction ? `-${Math.abs(numAmt)}` : `+${Math.abs(numAmt)}`;
      } else {
        diffStr = String(log.amount);
      }
    } else {
      diffStr = '-';
    }

    // Kategoriya va ranglarni aniqlash
    let category = 'chiqim';
    let badgeLabel = rawType;
    let badgeStyle = 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 dark:bg-orange-900/40 dark:text-orange-300';
    let barColor = 'bg-orange-500';

    if (typeLower.includes('tayyorlov') || typeLower.includes('zagatovka')) {
      category = 'prep';
      badgeLabel = rawType;
      badgeStyle = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/40 dark:text-amber-300';
      barColor = 'bg-amber-500';
    } else if (typeLower.includes('kirim')) {
      category = 'kirim';
      badgeLabel = 'ðŸ“¥ Kirim';
      badgeStyle = 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-900/40 dark:text-emerald-300';
      barColor = 'bg-emerald-500';
    } else if (typeLower.includes('reviziya')) {
      category = 'reviziya';
      badgeLabel = 'âš–ï¸ Reviziya';
      badgeStyle = 'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-900/40 dark:text-indigo-300';
      barColor = 'bg-indigo-500';
    } else if (typeLower.includes('sotuv') || typeLower.includes('avto')) {
      category = 'chiqim';
      badgeLabel = 'ðŸ§¾ Sotuv (Avto)';
      badgeStyle = 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900/40 dark:text-purple-300';
      barColor = 'bg-purple-500';
    } else {
      category = 'chiqim';
      badgeLabel = 'ðŸ“¤ Chiqim';
      badgeStyle = 'bg-red-100 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/40 dark:text-red-300';
      barColor = 'bg-red-500';
    }

    const isPositive = diffStr.startsWith('+');
    const isNegative = diffStr.startsWith('-') && diffStr !== '-';

    return {
      id: log.id || `log_${index}`,
      date: log.date || '-',
      rawType,
      category,
      badgeLabel,
      badgeStyle,
      barColor,
      itemName,
      diffStr,
      unit,
      isPositive,
      isNegative,
      convertedSubtext: getConvertedSubtext(diffStr, unit),
      note,
      user
    };
  };

  const normalizedLogs = useMemo(() => {
    return safeLogs.map((l, idx) => normalizeLogEntry(l, idx)).filter(Boolean);
  }, [safeLogs]);

  // ==========================================
  // 3. FILTRLASH MANTIG'I
  // ==========================================
  const filteredLogs = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();

    return normalizedLogs.filter(item => {
      if (typeFilter !== 'all' && item.category !== typeFilter) {
        return false;
      }

      if (!q) return true;

      return (
        item.itemName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.note.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.user.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.date.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.rawType.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    });
  }, [normalizedLogs, typeFilter, searchQuery]);

  // ==========================================

    );
    const content = `OMBORXONA HARAKATLAR TARIXI\nSana: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}\n==========================================================\n\n` + lines.join('\n');

    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `ombor-tarixi-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.txt`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleClearHistory = () => {
    if (typeof setInventoryLogs !== 'function' || safeLogs.length === 0) return;

    const performClear = () => {
      if (window.confirm("Rostdan ham barcha ombor harakatlari tarixini o'chirib tozalamoqchimisiz? (Ombor qoldig'i o'zgarmaydi)")) {
        setInventoryLogs([]);
        if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
          addAuditLog("Ombor tarixi tozalandi", "Ombor harakatlari jurnali tozalandi");
        }
      }
    };

    if (typeof requireAdminOverride === 'function' && currentUser?.role !== 'admin') {
      requireAdminOverride("Ombor harakatlari tarixini tozalash", performClear);
    } else {
      performClear();
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 5. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  if (safeLogs.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="animate-fade-in text-center py-16 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-3xl">
        <span className="text-5xl block mb-3 opacity-50">ðŸ“œ</span>
        <p className="font-bold text-gray-400">Hali hech qanday ombor harakati bajarilmagan.</p>
      </div>

  }

  return (
    <div className="animate-fade-in space-y-4">
      
      {/* YUQORI PANEL: QIDIRUV, FILTR TUGMALARI VA EKSPORT */}
      <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row justify-between items-stretch lg:items-center gap-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/50 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
        
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5 flex-1">
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="ðŸ” Mahsulot, xodim, sana yoki izohni qidiring..."
              className="w-full pl-4 pr-9 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 font-black"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

              { id: 'kirim', label: 'ðŸ“¥ Kirim' },
              { id: 'chiqim', label: 'ðŸ“¤ Chiqim / Sotuv' },

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import WarehouseStockTab from './WarehouseStockTab';
import WarehousePrepTab from './WarehousePrepTab';
import WarehouseHistoryTab from './WarehouseHistoryTab';

// ==========================================
// GLOBAL O'LCHOV BIRLIKLARI VA KONVERTATSIYA MOTORI
// ==========================================
export const MEASUREMENT_UNITS = [
  // Og'irlik birliklari (O'zaro gr <-> kg <-> tonna konvertatsiya bo'ladi)
  { value: 'gr', label: 'Gramm (gr)', short: 'gr', family: 'weight', factorToGrams: 1 },
  { value: 'kg', label: 'Kilogramm (kg)', short: 'kg', family: 'weight', factorToGrams: 1000 },
  { value: 'tonna', label: 'Tonna (t)', short: 't', family: 'weight', factorToGrams: 1000000 },

  // Hajm birliklari (O'zaro ml <-> litr konvertatsiya bo'ladi)
  { value: 'ml', label: 'Millilitr (ml)', short: 'ml', family: 'volume', factorToMl: 1 },
  { value: 'litr', label: 'Litr (l)', short: 'l', family: 'volume', factorToMl: 1000 },

  // Dona va qadoq birliklari (Oshxona va Ombor uchun yangi birliklar)
  { value: 'dona', label: 'Dona (dona)', short: 'dona', family: 'count' },
  { value: 'porsiya', label: 'Porsiya (pors)', short: 'pors', family: 'count' },
  { value: 'boglam', label: "Bog'lam (ko'kat/non)", short: "bog'lam", family: 'count' },
  { value: 'qadoq', label: 'Qadoq / Pachka', short: 'qadoq', family: 'count' },
  { value: 'qop', label: 'Qop', short: 'qop', family: 'count' },
  { value: 'karobka', label: 'Karobka / Blok', short: 'blok', family: 'count' }
];

// Birlik nomini standart shaklga keltirish
export const normalizeUnit = (unitStr) => {
  const u = String(unitStr || 'kg').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['g', 'gr', 'gram', 'gramm'].includes(u)) return 'gr';
  if (['kg', 'kilogram', 'kilogramm', 'kilo'].includes(u)) return 'kg';
  if (['t', 'ton', 'tonna', 'tn'].includes(u)) return 'tonna';
  if (['ml', 'millilitr'].includes(u)) return 'ml';
  if (['l', 'lt', 'litr', 'liter'].includes(u)) return 'litr';
  if (['pors', 'porsiya'].includes(u)) return 'porsiya';
  if (["bog'lam", 'boglam', 'bog'].includes(u)) return 'boglam';
  if (['pachka', 'qadoq'].includes(u)) return 'qadoq';
  if (['blok', 'karobka', 'yashik'].includes(u)) return 'karobka';
  if (['dona', 'ta', 'sht'].includes(u)) return 'dona';
  return u;
};

// Birlik oilasini aniqlash ('weight' | 'volume' | 'count')
export const getUnitFamily = (unitStr) => {
  const norm = normalizeUnit(unitStr);
  const found = MEASUREMENT_UNITS.find(u => u.value === norm);
  return found ? found.family : 'count';
};

// Qiymatni bir o'lchov birligidan ikkinchisiga aniq o'tkazish (Masalan: 500 gr -> 0.5 kg yoki 1.5 tonna -> 1500 kg)
export const convertMeasurement = (amount, fromUnit, toUnit) => {
  const numAmount = Number(amount) || 0;
  const fromNorm = normalizeUnit(fromUnit);
  const toNorm = normalizeUnit(toUnit);

  if (fromNorm === toNorm) return numAmount;

  const fromMeta = MEASUREMENT_UNITS.find(u => u.value === fromNorm);
  const toMeta = MEASUREMENT_UNITS.find(u => u.value === toNorm);

  // Og'irlik konvertatsiyasi: gr <-> kg <-> tonna
  if (fromMeta?.family === 'weight' && toMeta?.family === 'weight') {
    const inGrams = numAmount * fromMeta.factorToGrams;
    return Number((inGrams / toMeta.factorToGrams).toFixed(6));
  }

  // Hajm konvertatsiyasi: ml <-> litr
  if (fromMeta?.family === 'volume' && toMeta?.family === 'volume') {
    const inMl = numAmount * fromMeta.factorToMl;
    return Number((inMl / toMeta.factorToMl).toFixed(6));
  }

  // Agar har xil oila bo'lsa (masalan dona), o'zini qaytaradi
  return numAmount;
};

// Bitta mahsulotni ham Gramm, ham Kilogramm, ham Tonnada formatlab ko'rsatish
export const formatMultiUnitWeight = (amount, baseUnit, displayMode = 'auto') => {
  const num = Number(amount) || 0;
  const normUnit = normalizeUnit(baseUnit);
  const family = getUnitFamily(normUnit);

  if (family === 'weight') {
    const inGrams = convertMeasurement(num, normUnit, 'gr');
    const inKg = convertMeasurement(num, normUnit, 'kg');
    const inTons = convertMeasurement(num, normUnit, 'tonna');

    if (displayMode === 'gr') {
      return `${inGrams.toLocaleString()} gr`;
    }
    if (displayMode === 'kg') {
      return `${inKg.toLocaleString()} kg`;
    }
    if (displayMode === 'tonna') {
      return `${inTons.toLocaleString()} t`;
    }
    if (displayMode === 'all') {
      return `${inTons} t  |  ${inKg.toLocaleString()} kg  |  ${inGrams.toLocaleString()} gr`;
    }

    // 'auto' rejimda asosiy qiymat va qavs ichida qolgan birliklar
    if (normUnit === 'tonna') {
      return `${inTons.toLocaleString()} t (${inKg.toLocaleString()} kg / ${inGrams.toLocaleString()} gr)`;
    }
    if (normUnit === 'kg') {
      return `${inKg.toLocaleString()} kg (${inGrams.toLocaleString()} gr / ${inTons} t)`;
    }
    return `${inGrams.toLocaleString()} gr (${inKg} kg)`;
  }

  if (family === 'volume') {
    const inMl = convertMeasurement(num, normUnit, 'ml');
    const inLitr = convertMeasurement(num, normUnit, 'litr');
    if (displayMode === 'all') {
      return `${inLitr.toLocaleString()} litr  |  ${inMl.toLocaleString()} ml`;
    }
    return normUnit === 'litr'
      ? `${inLitr.toLocaleString()} litr (${inMl.toLocaleString()} ml)`
      : `${inMl.toLocaleString()} ml (${inLitr} litr)`;
  }

  return `${num.toLocaleString()} ${baseUnit || 'dona'}`;
};

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

// O'lchov birliklari va konvertatsiya koeffitsiyentlari
const UNIT_META = [
  { value: 'gr', label: 'Gramm (gr)', short: 'gr', family: 'weight', factor: 1 },
  { value: 'kg', label: 'Kilogramm (kg)', short: 'kg', family: 'weight', factor: 1000 },

const normalizeUnit = (unitStr) => {
  const u = String(unitStr || 'kg').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['g', 'gr', 'gram', 'gramm'].includes(u)) return 'gr';
  if (['kg', 'kilogram', 'kilogramm', 'kilo'].includes(u)) return 'kg';
  if (['t', 'ton', 'tonna', 'tn'].includes(u)) return 'tonna';
  if (['ml', 'millilitr'].includes(u)) return 'ml';
  if (['l', 'lt', 'litr', 'liter'].includes(u)) return 'litr';
  if (['pors', 'porsiya'].includes(u)) return 'porsiya';
  if (["bog'lam", 'boglam', 'bog'].includes(u)) return 'boglam';
  if (['pachka', 'qadoq'].includes(u)) return 'qadoq';
  if (['quti', 'karobka', 'blok'].includes(u)) return 'quti';
  if (['dona', 'ta', 'sht'].includes(u)) return 'dona';
  return u;
};

const getUnitInfo = (unitStr) => {
  const norm = normalizeUnit(unitStr);
  return UNIT_META.find(u => u.value === norm) || {
    value: norm,
    label: norm,
    short: norm,
    family: 'count',
    factor: 1
  };
};

// Qiymatni bir birlikdan ikkinchisiga o'girish (gr <-> kg <-> tonna va ml <-> litr)
const convertUnits = (amount, fromUnit, toUnit) => {
  const num = Number(amount) || 0;
  const fromInfo = getUnitInfo(fromUnit);
  const toInfo = getUnitInfo(toUnit);

  if (fromInfo.value === toInfo.value) return num;
  if (fromInfo.family !== toInfo.family) return num;

  const inSmallest = num * fromInfo.factor;
  return Number((inSmallest / toInfo.factor).toFixed(6));
};

// Og'irlik/hajmni uchala birlikda formatlash
const formatTripleScale = (amount, unit) => {
  const num = Number(amount) || 0;
  const info = getUnitInfo(unit);

  if (info.family === 'weight') {
    const gr = Number(convertUnits(num, info.value, 'gr').toFixed(2));
    const kg = Number(convertUnits(num, info.value, 'kg').toFixed(4));
    const t = Number(convertUnits(num, info.value, 'tonna').toFixed(6));
    return `${t} t  |  ${kg.toLocaleString()} kg  |  ${gr.toLocaleString()} gr`;
  }

  if (info.family === 'volume') {
    const ml = Number(convertUnits(num, info.value, 'ml').toFixed(2));
    const l = Number(convertUnits(num, info.value, 'litr').toFixed(4));
    return `${l.toLocaleString()} litr  |  ${ml.toLocaleString()} ml`;
  }

  return `${num.toLocaleString()} ${info.short}`;
};

export default function WarehousePrepTab({ 
  inventory = [], 
  setInventory,
  setInventoryLogs,
  currentUser,
  addAuditLog 
}) {
  const safeInventory = Array.isArray(inventory) ? inventory : [];

  // ==========================================
  // 1. STATE'LAR
  // ==========================================
  // Ishlab chiqariladigan (tayyorlanadigan) mahsulot va uning kiritish birligi
  const [targetItemId, setTargetItemId] = useState('');
  const [produceQty, setProduceQty] = useState('');
  const [produceUnit, setProduceUnit] = useState('kg');
  const [prepNote, setPrepNote] = useState('');
  
  // Sarflanadigan xomashyolar ro'yxati (Retsept) â€” har birining o'z birligi bor
  const [ingredients, setIngredients] = useState([
    { inventoryId: '', qty: '', inputUnit: 'kg' }
  ]);

  // Tanlangan tayyorlanuvchi mahsulot obyekti
  const targetItem = useMemo(() => {
    return safeInventory.find(i => i && String(i.id) === String(targetItemId)) || null;
  }, [safeInventory, targetItemId]);

  // ==========================================
  // 2. FORMA BOSHQARUV FUNKSIYALARI
  // ==========================================
  const handleSelectTargetItem = (newId) => {
    setTargetItemId(newId);
    const found = safeInventory.find(i => i && String(i.id) === String(newId));
    if (found) {
      setProduceUnit(normalizeUnit(found.unit || 'kg'));
    }
    // Agar xomashyolar ichida shu mahsulot tanlangan bo'lsa, uni tozalaymiz (o'zidan o'zini yasamaslik uchun)
    setIngredients(prev =>
      prev.map(ing =>
        String(ing.inventoryId) === String(newId)
          ? { inventoryId: '', qty: '', inputUnit: 'kg' }
          : ing
      )
    );
  };

  const handleAddIngredient = () => {
    setIngredients(prev => [...prev, { inventoryId: '', qty: '', inputUnit: 'kg' }]);
  };

  const handleRemoveIngredient = (index) => {
    if (ingredients.length <= 1) return;
    setIngredients(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handleIngredientChange = (index, field, value) => {
    setIngredients(prev =>
      prev.map((ing, i) => {
        if (i !== index) return ing;

        // Agar xomashyo o'zgarsa, uning standart birligini avtomatik qo'yamiz
        if (field === 'inventoryId') {
          const selectedInv = safeInventory.find(inv => inv && String(inv.id) === String(value));
          const defaultUnit = selectedInv ? normalizeUnit(selectedInv.unit) : 'kg';
          return { ...ing, inventoryId: value, inputUnit: defaultUnit };
        }

        return { ...ing, [field]: value };
      })
    );
  };

  // Ma'lum bir mahsulot uchun mos tushadigan birliklar ro'yxati (masalan: gr, kg, tonna)
  const getCompatibleUnitsForItem = (invItem) => {
    if (!invItem) return UNIT_META;
    const itemInfo = getUnitInfo(invItem.unit);
    if (itemInfo.family === 'weight' || itemInfo.family === 'volume') {
      return UNIT_META.filter(u => u.family === itemInfo.family);
    }
    return [itemInfo];
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 3. TAYYORLOV AKTINI BAJARISH VA VALIDATSIYA
  // ==========================================
  const handleProduce = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!targetItem || !produceQty || Number(produceQty) <= 0) {
      alert("Iltimos, tayyorlanadigan mahsulotni va miqdorini to'g'ri kiriting!");
      return;
    }

    // 1. Har bir qator to'ldirilganini tekshiramiz va bir xil xomashyolarni umumlashtiramiz
    const requiredByItemId = {};

    for (let i = 0; i < ingredients.length; i++) {
      const ing = ingredients[i];
      const rawQty = Number(ing.qty);

      if (!ing.inventoryId || isNaN(rawQty) || rawQty <= 0) {
        alert(`${i + 1}-qatordagi xomashyo va sarf miqdorini to'g'ri kiriting!`);
        return;
      }

      const invItem = safeInventory.find(inv => inv && String(inv.id) === String(ing.inventoryId));
      if (!invItem) {
        alert(`${i + 1}-qatordagi xomashyo omborda topilmadi!`);
        return;
      }

      const itemBaseUnit = normalizeUnit(invItem.unit);
      const convertedNeedInBase = convertUnits(rawQty, ing.inputUnit || itemBaseUnit, itemBaseUnit);

      const key = String(invItem.id);
      if (!requiredByItemId[key]) {
        requiredByItemId[key] = {
          item: invItem,
          baseUnit: itemBaseUnit,
          totalNeededInBase: 0,
          inputDetails: []
        };
      }
      requiredByItemId[key].totalNeededInBase = Number(
        (requiredByItemId[key].totalNeededInBase + convertedNeedInBase).toFixed(6)
      );
      requiredByItemId[key].inputDetails.push(`${rawQty} ${ing.inputUnit || itemBaseUnit}`);
    }

    // 2. Omborda qoldiq yetarli ekanligini tekshiramiz
    for (const key of Object.keys(requiredByItemId)) {
      const req = requiredByItemId[key];
      const availableInBase = Number(req.item.quantity ?? req.item.qty ?? 0) || 0;

      if (availableInBase < req.totalNeededInBase) {
        alert(
          `â›”ï¸ Diqqat! "${req.item.name}" xomashyosi omborda yetarli emas!\n\n` +
          `Talab qilingan: ${req.totalNeededInBase} ${req.baseUnit} (${req.inputDetails.join(' + ')})\n` +
          `Ombordagi qoldiq: ${availableInBase} ${req.baseUnit}`
        );
        return;
      }
    }

    const targetBaseUnit = normalizeUnit(targetItem.unit);
    const producedInTargetBase = convertUnits(produceQty, produceUnit, targetBaseUnit);

    if (
      !window.confirm(
        `Tayyorlov aktini tasdiqlaysizmi?\n\n` +
        `âž• Kirim: +${producedInTargetBase} ${targetBaseUnit} "${targetItem.name}"\n` +
        `âž– Xomashyolar ombordan avtomatik yechiladi.`
      )
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const newInventory = [...safeInventory];
    const logs = [];
    const dateNow = new Date().toLocaleString();

    // 3. Xomashyolarni ombordan minus qilish
    Object.values(requiredByItemId).forEach(req => {
      const invIdx = newInventory.findIndex(i => i && String(i.id) === String(req.item.id));
      if (invIdx === -1) return;

      const usedItem = newInventory[invIdx];
      const oldQty = Number(usedItem.quantity ?? usedItem.qty ?? 0) || 0;
      const newQty = Math.max(0, Number((oldQty - req.totalNeededInBase).toFixed(6)));

      newInventory[invIdx] = { 
        ...usedItem, 
        quantity: newQty,
        qty: newQty,
        lastUpdated: dateNow
      };

      const detailStr = req.inputDetails.join(' + ');
      logs.push({
        id: `${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
        date: dateNow,
        type: 'Chiqim (Tayyorlov)',
        itemName: usedItem.name,
        productName: usedItem.name,
        diffAmount: `-${req.totalNeededInBase}`,
        amount: req.totalNeededInBase,
        unit: req.baseUnit,
        note: `"${targetItem.name}" tayyorlash uchun sarf (${detailStr})${prepNote ? ` â€¢ ${prepNote}` : ''}`,
        reason: `"${targetItem.name}" tayyorlash uchun sarf (${detailStr})`,
        user: currentUser?.name || 'Omborchi'
      });
    });

    // 4. Tayyorlangan mahsulotni omborga plus qilish
    const targetIdx = newInventory.findIndex(i => i && String(i.id) === String(targetItem.id));
    if (targetIdx !== -1) {
      const currentTargetQty = Number(newInventory[targetIdx].quantity ?? newInventory[targetIdx].qty ?? 0) || 0;
      const updatedTargetQty = Number((currentTargetQty + producedInTargetBase).toFixed(6));

      newInventory[targetIdx] = {
        ...newInventory[targetIdx],
        quantity: updatedTargetQty,
        qty: updatedTargetQty,
        lastUpdated: dateNow
      };

      const enteredUnitNote = produceUnit !== targetBaseUnit ? ` (${produceQty} ${produceUnit})` : '';
      logs.push({
        id: `${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
        date: dateNow,
        type: 'Kirim (Tayyorlov)',
        itemName: targetItem.name,
        productName: targetItem.name,
        diffAmount: `+${producedInTargetBase}`,
        amount: producedInTargetBase,
        unit: targetBaseUnit,
        note: `Ishlab chiqarish akti${enteredUnitNote}${prepNote ? ` â€¢ ${prepNote}` : ''}`,
        reason: `Ishlab chiqarish akti${enteredUnitNote}`,
        user: currentUser?.name || 'Omborchi'
      });
    }

    // 5. Bazani yangilash
    if (typeof setInventory === 'function') {
      setInventory(newInventory);
    }
    if (typeof setInventoryLogs === 'function') {
      setInventoryLogs(prev => [...logs, ...(Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [])]);
    }
    
    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
      addAuditLog(
        "Tayyorlov Akti", 
        `+${producedInTargetBase} ${targetBaseUnit} "${targetItem.name}" ishlab chiqarildi`
      );
    }

    // Formani tozalash
    setTargetItemId('');
    setProduceQty('');
    setPrepNote('');
    setIngredients([{ inventoryId: '', qty: '', inputUnit: 'kg' }]);
    alert(`âœ… Tayyorlov akti muvaffaqiyatli bajarildi!\n+${producedInTargetBase} ${targetBaseUnit} "${targetItem.name}" omborga kirim qilindi.`);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 4. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  const targetCompatibleUnits = getCompatibleUnitsForItem(targetItem);

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-3xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 p-6 md:p-8 max-w-4xl mx-auto animate-fade-in shadow-sm">
      
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white flex items-center gap-2">
          <span>ðŸ”ª</span> Tayyorlov Akti (Ishlab chiqarish va Konvertatsiya)
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-gray-500 mt-1">
          Ombordagi xomashyolarni gramm, kilogramm yoki tonnada sarflab, yangi yarim-tayyor mahsulot (masalan: Qiyma, Xamir, Sous) yaratish bo'limi.
        </p>
      </div>

      {safeInventory.length < 2 ? (
        <div className="p-8 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/20 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 text-center space-y-2">
          <span className="text-4xl block">ðŸ“¦</span>
          <h3 className="font-black text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300">
            Tayyorlov akti uchun omborda kamida 2 xil mahsulot bo'lishi kerak
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
            Avval "Ombor Qoldig'i" bo'limida xomashyolarni va tayyorlanadigan mahsulot nomini yarating.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleProduce} className="space-y-8">
          
          {/* =======================================================
              QISM 1: NIMA ISHLAB CHIQARILYAPTI?
              ======================================================= */}
          <div className="bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/10 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 dark:border-emerald-900/30 space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-black text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-400 uppercase text-xs tracking-widest">
              1. Tayyorlanayotgan Mahsulot (Omborga +Kirim bo'ladi)
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-4">
              {/* Mahsulot tanlash */}
              <div className="md:col-span-6">
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-2">
                  Mahsulotni tanlang *
                </label>
                <select 
                  value={targetItemId} 
                  onChange={e => handleSelectTargetItem(e.target.value)} 
                  required
                  className="w-full p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <option value="">-- Tanlang --</option>
                  {safeInventory.map(i => (
                    <option key={i.id} value={String(i.id)}>
                      {i.name} (Joriy qoldiq: {i.quantity} {i.unit})
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              {/* Miqdor */}
              <div className="md:col-span-3">
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-2">
                  Ishlab chiqarish miqdori *
                </label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  value={produceQty} 
                  onChange={e => setProduceQty(e.target.value)} 
                  required 
                  min="0.0001" 
                  step="any"
                  placeholder="Masalan: 5"
                  className="w-full p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-black text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                />
              </div>

              {/* O'lchov birligi (gr / kg / tonna) */}
              <div className="md:col-span-3">
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-2">
                  O'lchov birligi *
                </label>
                <select
                  value={produceUnit}
                  onChange={e => setProduceUnit(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={!targetItem}
                  className="w-full p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 outline-none cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {targetCompatibleUnits.map(u => (
                    <option key={u.value} value={u.value}>{u.label}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Jonli konvertatsiya ko'rsatkichi */}
            {targetItem && Number(produceQty) > 0 && (
              <div className="bg-white/80 dark:bg-gray-900/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/50 flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black">
                <span className="text-gray-500">
                  âš–ï¸ Uchlik o'lchovda: <strong className="text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{formatTripleScale(produceQty, produceUnit)}</strong>
                </span>
                <span className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
                  Ombor birligida qo'shiladi: +{convertUnits(produceQty, produceUnit, targetItem.unit).toLocaleString()} {targetItem.unit}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* =======================================================
              QISM 2: NIMALAR SARFLANYAPTI? (RETSEPT XOMASHYOLARI)
              ======================================================= */}
          <div className="bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/10 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-amber-100 dark:border-amber-900/30 space-y-4">

              <button 
                type="button"

                className="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 bg-amber-200/70 dark:bg-amber-900/40 hover:bg-amber-200 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-black transition active:scale-95"

                  ? convertUnits(ing.qty, ing.inputUnit, selectedIngItem.unit)
                  : 0;
                const currentStock = Number(selectedIngItem?.quantity ?? selectedIngItem?.qty ?? 0) || 0;
                const isEnough = !selectedIngItem || neededInBase <= currentStock;

                return (
                  <div

                      !isEnough 
                        ? 'border-red-400 dark:border-red-600' 
                        : 'border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700'
                    }`}

                      {/* Xomashyo tanlash */}

                          Xomashyo #{idx + 1} *
                        </label>
                        <select 
                          value={ing.inventoryId}
                          onChange={e => handleIngredientChange(idx, 'inventoryId', e.target.value)}
                          required
                          className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-bold text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <option value="">-- Xomashyoni tanlang --</option>
                          {safeInventory
                            .filter(i => i && String(i.id) !== String(targetItemId))
                            .map(i => (
                              <option key={i.id} value={String(i.id)}>
                                {i.name} â€” Qoldiq: {i.quantity} {i.unit}
                              </option>
                            ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>

                      {/* Sarf miqdori */}
                      <div className="w-full sm:w-36 shrink-0">
                        <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-1">
                          Sarf miqdori *
                        </label>
                        <input 
                          type="number" 
                          value={ing.qty}
                          onChange={e => handleIngredientChange(idx, 'qty', e.target.value)}
                          required 
                          min="0.0001" 
                          step="any"
                          placeholder="Miqdor"
                          className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-black text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                        />
                      </div>

                      {/* Sarf birligi (gr / kg / tonna) */}
                      <div className="w-full sm:w-40 shrink-0">
                        <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-1">
                          Birligi (gr/kg/t) *
                        </label>
                        <select
                          value={ing.inputUnit}
                          onChange={e => handleIngredientChange(idx, 'inputUnit', e.target.value)}
                          disabled={!selectedIngItem}
                          className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-black text-sm text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 outline-none cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                        >

                            <option key={u.value} value={u.value}>{u.label}</option>
                          ))}
                        </select>

                      {ingredients.length > 1 && (
                        <button 
                          type="button" 
                          onClick={() => handleRemoveIngredient(idx)}

                          title="Xomashyoni o'chirish"

                          âœ•
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Xomashyo konvertatsiyasi va qoldiq tekshiruvi */}
                    {selectedIngItem && Number(ing.qty) > 0 && (
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-2 pt-1 text-[11px] font-black">
                        <span className="text-gray-400">
                          ðŸ”„ {formatTripleScale(ing.qty, ing.inputUnit)}
                        </span>
                        <span className={isEnough ? 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400' : 'text-red-600 animate-pulse'}>
                          {isEnough 
                            ? `Ombordan ayriladi: -${neededInBase} ${selectedIngItem.unit} (Qoladi: ${Number((currentStock - neededInBase).toFixed(4))} ${selectedIngItem.unit})`
                            : `â›”ï¸ Yetarli emas! Kerak: ${neededInBase} ${selectedIngItem.unit}, Omborda bor: ${currentStock} ${selectedIngItem.unit}`}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* QO'SHIMCHA IZOH */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-2 uppercase tracking-wider">
              Tayyorlov izohi (Ixtiyoriy)
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={prepNote}
              onChange={e => setPrepNote(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Masalan: Ertalabki smena uchun somsa xamiri..."
              className="w-full p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 font-bold text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* TASDIQLASH TUGMASI */}
          <button 
            type="submit"
            className="w-full bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white font-black py-4 rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/30 transition active:scale-95 uppercase tracking-widest text-base sm:text-lg"
          >
            ðŸ”ª Tayyorlov Aktini Tasdiqlash
          </button>

        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
