import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

// Ombor uchun tayyor toifalar ro'yxati
const inventoryCategories = [
  "Go'sht va parrandalar",
  "Sut va yog' mahsulotlari",
  "Sabzavot va mevalar",
  "Don, un va yormalar",
  "Ziravorlar va souslar",
  "Ichimliklar",
  "Xo'jalik mollari",
  "Boshqa"
];

// O'lchov birliklari va konvertatsiya koeffitsiyentlari
const UNIT_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'gr', label: 'Gramm (gr)', family: 'weight', factorToBase: 1 },
  { value: 'kg', label: 'Kilogramm (kg)', family: 'weight', factorToBase: 1000 },
  { value: 'tonna', label: 'Tonna (t)', family: 'weight', factorToBase: 1000000 },
  { value: 'ml', label: 'Millilitr (ml)', family: 'volume', factorToBase: 1 },
  { value: 'litr', label: 'Litr (l)', family: 'volume', factorToBase: 1000 },
  { value: 'dona', label: 'Dona', family: 'count', factorToBase: 1 },
  { value: 'porsiya', label: 'Porsiya (pors)', family: 'count', factorToBase: 1 },
  { value: 'boglam', label: "Bog'lam (ko'kat/non)", family: 'count', factorToBase: 1 },
  { value: 'qadoq', label: 'Qadoq / Pachka', family: 'count', factorToBase: 1 },
  { value: 'qop', label: 'Qop', family: 'count', factorToBase: 1 },
  { value: 'quti', label: 'Quti / Karobka', family: 'count', factorToBase: 1 }
];

const normalizeUnitCode = (unitStr) => {
  const u = String(unitStr || 'kg').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['g', 'gr', 'gram', 'gramm'].includes(u)) return 'gr';
  if (['kg', 'kilogram', 'kilogramm', 'kilo'].includes(u)) return 'kg';
  if (['t', 'ton', 'tonna', 'tn'].includes(u)) return 'tonna';
  if (['ml', 'millilitr'].includes(u)) return 'ml';
  if (['l', 'lt', 'litr', 'liter'].includes(u)) return 'litr';
  if (['pors', 'porsiya'].includes(u)) return 'porsiya';
  if (["bog'lam", 'boglam', 'bog'].includes(u)) return 'boglam';
  if (['pachka', 'qadoq'].includes(u)) return 'qadoq';
  if (['quti', 'karobka', 'blok'].includes(u)) return 'quti';
  if (['dona', 'ta', 'sht'].includes(u)) return 'dona';
  return u;
};

const getUnitMeta = (unitStr) => {
  const norm = normalizeUnitCode(unitStr);
  return UNIT_OPTIONS.find(u => u.value === norm) || {
    value: norm,
    label: norm,
    family: 'count',
    factorToBase: 1
  };
};

// Qiymatni bir birlikdan ikkinchi birlikka o'girish (gr <-> kg <-> tonna va ml <-> litr)
const convertValueBetweenUnits = (val, fromUnit, toUnit) => {
  const num = Number(val) || 0;
  const fromMeta = getUnitMeta(fromUnit);
  const toMeta = getUnitMeta(toUnit);

  if (fromMeta.value === toMeta.value) return num;
  if (fromMeta.family !== toMeta.family) return num;

  const inBase = num * fromMeta.factorToBase;
  return Number((inBase / toMeta.factorToBase).toFixed(6));
};

// Birlik narxini bir birlikdan ikkinchi birlikka o'girish (Masalan: 1 kg = 100,000 s -> 1 gr = 100 s)
const convertUnitPriceBetweenUnits = (pricePerFromUnit, fromUnit, toUnit) => {
  const numPrice = Number(pricePerFromUnit) || 0;
  const fromMeta = getUnitMeta(fromUnit);
  const toMeta = getUnitMeta(toUnit);

  if (fromMeta.value === toMeta.value) return numPrice;
  if (fromMeta.family !== toMeta.family) return numPrice;

  const pricePerBase = numPrice / fromMeta.factorToBase;
  return Number((pricePerBase * toMeta.factorToBase).toFixed(4));
};

const getMultiScaleBreakdown = (qty, unit) => {
  const num = Number(qty) || 0;
  const meta = getUnitMeta(unit);

  if (meta.family === 'weight') {
    return {
      isConvertible: true,
      family: 'weight',
      gr: Number(convertValueBetweenUnits(num, meta.value, 'gr').toFixed(2)),
      kg: Number(convertValueBetweenUnits(num, meta.value, 'kg').toFixed(4)),
      tonna: Number(convertValueBetweenUnits(num, meta.value, 'tonna').toFixed(6))
    };
  }

  if (meta.family === 'volume') {
    return {
      isConvertible: true,
      family: 'volume',
      ml: Number(convertValueBetweenUnits(num, meta.value, 'ml').toFixed(2)),
      litr: Number(convertValueBetweenUnits(num, meta.value, 'litr').toFixed(4))
    };
  }

  return { isConvertible: false, family: 'count' };
};

export default function WarehouseStockTab({ 
  inventory = [], 
  setInventory, 
  setInventoryLogs, 
  currentUser,
  addAuditLog,
  displayUnitMode = 'all'
}) {
  const safeInventory = Array.isArray(inventory) ? inventory : [];

  // ==========================================
  // 1. QIDIRUV VA FILTR STATE'LARI
  // ==========================================
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState('Barchasi');
  const [stockFilter, setStockFilter] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'low' | 'out'

  // ==========================================
  // 2. MAHSULOT QO'SHISH VA TAHRIRLASH MODALI
  // ==========================================
  const [isItemModalOpen, setIsItemModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState(null);
  
  const [itemData, setItemData] = useState({ 
    name: '', 
    category: inventoryCategories[0], 
    unit: 'kg', 
    quantity: '', 
    minLimit: '',
    costPrice: '' // YANGI: 1 birlik xarid narxi (so'mda)
  });

  // Ombordagi barcha mahsulotlarning umumiy puldagi qiymati
  const totalWarehouseValuation = useMemo(() => {
    return safeInventory.reduce((sum, item) => {
      if (!item) return sum;
      const qty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 0) || 0;
      const cost = Number(item.costPrice ?? item.price ?? 0) || 0;
      return sum + Math.round(qty * cost);
    }, 0);
  }, [safeInventory]);

  const handleOpenItemModal = (item = null) => {
    if (item) {
      setEditingItem(item);
      setItemData({ 
        name: item.name || '', 
        category: item.category || inventoryCategories[7], 
        unit: normalizeUnitCode(item.unit || 'kg'), 
        quantity: item.quantity !== undefined ? String(item.quantity) : '0', 
        minLimit: item.minLimit !== undefined ? String(item.minLimit) : '0',
        costPrice: item.costPrice !== undefined ? String(item.costPrice) : (item.price ? String(item.price) : '')
      });
    } else {
      setEditingItem(null);
      setItemData({ 
        name: '', 
        category: inventoryCategories[0],
        unit: 'kg', 
        quantity: '', 
        minLimit: '',
        costPrice: ''
      });
    }
    setIsItemModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Birlik o'zgartirilsa (masalan kg -> gr), qoldiq, limit va birlik narxini avtomatik konvertatsiya qilish
  const handleModalUnitChange = (nextUnitRaw) => {
    const nextUnit = normalizeUnitCode(nextUnitRaw);
    const prevUnit = normalizeUnitCode(itemData.unit);
    const prevMeta = getUnitMeta(prevUnit);
    const nextMeta = getUnitMeta(nextUnit);

    if (prevMeta.family === nextMeta.family && prevUnit !== nextUnit) {
      const convertedQty = itemData.quantity !== '' 
        ? String(convertValueBetweenUnits(itemData.quantity, prevUnit, nextUnit)) 
        : '';
      const convertedMin = itemData.minLimit !== '' 
        ? String(convertValueBetweenUnits(itemData.minLimit, prevUnit, nextUnit)) 
        : '';
      const convertedCost = itemData.costPrice !== ''
        ? String(convertUnitPriceBetweenUnits(itemData.costPrice, prevUnit, nextUnit))
        : '';

      setItemData(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        unit: nextUnit,
        quantity: convertedQty,
        minLimit: convertedMin,
        costPrice: convertedCost
      }));
    } else {
      setItemData(prev => ({ ...prev, unit: nextUnit }));
    }
  };

  const handleSaveItem = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const name = (itemData.name || '').trim();
    const category = itemData.category || inventoryCategories[0];
    const normUnit = normalizeUnitCode(itemData.unit);
    const min = Number(itemData.minLimit);
    const qty = Number(itemData.quantity);
    const cost = Number(itemData.costPrice || 0);

    if (!name || isNaN(min) || isNaN(qty) || min < 0 || qty < 0 || isNaN(cost) || cost < 0) {
      alert("Iltimos, ma'lumotlarni to'g'ri (manfiy bo'lmagan) raqamlarda kiriting!"); 
      return;
    }

    const isDuplicate = safeInventory.some(
      i => i && String(i.name || '').trim().toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase() && i.id !== editingItem?.id
    );
    if (isDuplicate) {
      alert(`â›”ï¸ "${name}" nomli mahsulot omborda allaqachon mavjud!`);
      return;
    }

    const cleanQty = Number(qty.toFixed(6));
    const cleanMin = Number(min.toFixed(6));
    const cleanCost = Number(cost.toFixed(2));

    if (editingItem) {
      if (typeof setInventory === 'function') {
        setInventory(prev => prev.map(i => 
          i.id === editingItem.id 
            ? { 
                ...i, 
                name, 
                category, 
                unit: normUnit, 
                quantity: cleanQty, 
                qty: cleanQty,
                minLimit: cleanMin,
                costPrice: cleanCost,
                lastUpdated: new Date().toLocaleString()
              } 
            : i
        ));
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog(
          "Ombor mahsuloti tahrirlandi", 
          `${name} (${cleanQty} ${normUnit} â€¢ Tannarxi: ${cleanCost.toLocaleString()} s/${normUnit})`
        );
      }
    } else {
      const newItemObj = { 
        id: Date.now().toString(), 
        name, 
        category, 
        unit: normUnit, 
        quantity: cleanQty, 
        qty: cleanQty,
        minLimit: cleanMin, 
        costPrice: cleanCost,
        lastUpdated: new Date().toLocaleString() 
      };

      if (typeof setInventory === 'function') {
        setInventory(prev => [...prev, newItemObj]);
      }

      if (typeof setInventoryLogs === 'function') {
        setInventoryLogs(prev => [
          {
            id: `${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
            date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
            type: 'Kirim',
            itemName: name,
            productName: name,
            diffAmount: `+${cleanQty}`,
            amount: cleanQty,
            unit: normUnit,
            note: `Boshlang'ich qoldiq (Narxi: ${cleanCost.toLocaleString()} s/${normUnit})`,
            reason: `Boshlang'ich qoldiq`,
            user: currentUser?.name || 'Omborchi'
          },
          ...(Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [])
        ]);
      }

      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog(
          "Omborga yangi mahsulot qo'shildi", 
          `${name} (${cleanQty} ${normUnit} â€¢ ${cleanCost.toLocaleString()} s/${normUnit})`
        );
      }
    }
    setIsItemModalOpen(false);
  };

  const handleDeleteInventory = (id, name) => {
    if (window.confirm(`Rostdan ham "${name}" mahsulotini ombordan butunlay o'chirilsinmi?`)) {
      if (typeof setInventory === 'function') {
        setInventory(prev => prev.filter(i => i.id !== id));
      }
      if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function') {
        addAuditLog("Ombordan mahsulot o'chirildi", `"${name}" ombor ro'yxatidan o'chirildi`);
      }
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 3. KIRIM, CHIQIM VA REVIZIYA MODALI (XARID NARXI BILAN)
  // ==========================================
  const [isUpdateModalOpen, setIsUpdateModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [updateType, setUpdateType] = useState('kirim'); // 'kirim' | 'chiqim' | 'reviziya'
  const [selectedStockItem, setSelectedStockItem] = useState(null);
  const [updateAmount, setUpdateAmount] = useState('');
  const [updateInputUnit, setUpdateInputUnit] = useState('kg');
  const [updateUnitPrice, setUpdateUnitPrice] = useState(''); // Kirim qilinayotgan birlik narxi
  const [updateNote, setUpdateNote] = useState(''); 

  const handleOpenUpdateModal = (item, type) => {
    const baseUnit = normalizeUnitCode(item.unit || 'kg');
    setSelectedStockItem(item);
    setUpdateType(type);
    setUpdateInputUnit(baseUnit);
    setUpdateAmount(type === 'reviziya' ? String(item.quantity ?? 0) : ''); 
    setUpdateUnitPrice(item.costPrice ? String(item.costPrice) : '');
    setUpdateNote('');
    setIsUpdateModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleUpdateUnitSwitch = (newUnit) => {
    if (updateType === 'reviziya' && updateAmount !== '') {
      const converted = convertValueBetweenUnits(updateAmount, updateInputUnit, newUnit);
      setUpdateAmount(String(converted));
    }
    if (updateUnitPrice !== '') {
      const convertedPrice = convertUnitPriceBetweenUnits(updateUnitPrice, updateInputUnit, newUnit);
      setUpdateUnitPrice(String(convertedPrice));
    }
    setUpdateInputUnit(newUnit);
  };

  const handleSaveUpdate = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedStockItem) return;

    const rawEnteredAmount = Number(updateAmount);
    if (isNaN(rawEnteredAmount) || rawEnteredAmount < 0) {
      alert("Iltimos, miqdorni to'g'ri raqamlarda kiriting!"); 
      return;
    }

    const itemBaseUnit = normalizeUnitCode(selectedStockItem.unit || 'kg');
    const currentStockQty = Number(selectedStockItem.quantity ?? selectedStockItem.qty ?? 0) || 0;
    const currentCostPrice = Number(selectedStockItem.costPrice ?? 0) || 0;

    const amountInBaseUnit = convertValueBetweenUnits(rawEnteredAmount, updateInputUnit, itemBaseUnit);

    let diffInBase = 0;
    let newQtyInBase = 0;
    let nextCostPriceInBase = currentCostPrice;

    if (updateType === 'kirim') {
      if (amountInBaseUnit <= 0) return;
      newQtyInBase = Number((currentStockQty + amountInBaseUnit).toFixed(6));
      diffInBase = Number(amountInBaseUnit.toFixed(6));

      // Agar yangi xarid narxi kiritilgan bo'lsa, asosiy birlikka o'girib yangilaymiz
      if (updateUnitPrice !== '' && Number(updateUnitPrice) > 0) {
        nextCostPriceInBase = convertUnitPriceBetweenUnits(
          Number(updateUnitPrice),
          updateInputUnit,
          itemBaseUnit
        );
      }
    } else if (updateType === 'chiqim') {
      if (amountInBaseUnit <= 0) return;
      if (amountInBaseUnit > currentStockQty) {
        alert(`â›”ï¸ Omborda yetarli qoldiq yo'q! Joriy qoldiq: ${currentStockQty} ${itemBaseUnit}`);
        return;
      }
      newQtyInBase = Number(Math.max(0, currentStockQty - amountInBaseUnit).toFixed(6));
      diffInBase = -Number(amountInBaseUnit.toFixed(6));
    } else {
      newQtyInBase = Number(amountInBaseUnit.toFixed(6));
      diffInBase = Number((newQtyInBase - currentStockQty).toFixed(6));
    }

    if (typeof setInventory === 'function') {
      setInventory(prev => prev.map(i => 
        i.id === selectedStockItem.id 
          ? { 
              ...i, 
              quantity: newQtyInBase, 
              qty: newQtyInBase,
              costPrice: nextCostPriceInBase,
              lastUpdated: new Date().toLocaleString() 
            } 
          : i
      ));
    }

    if (typeof setInventoryLogs === 'function' && diffInBase !== 0) {
      const enteredLabel = updateInputUnit !== itemBaseUnit 
        ? ` (Kiritildi: ${rawEnteredAmount} ${updateInputUnit})` 
        : '';
      const priceLabel = updateType === 'kirim' && nextCostPriceInBase > 0
        ? ` â€¢ Xarid narxi: ${nextCostPriceInBase.toLocaleString()} s/${itemBaseUnit}`
        : '';

      const logTypeLabel = 
        updateType === 'kirim' ? 'Kirim' : 
        updateType === 'chiqim' ? 'Chiqim' : 'Reviziya';

      const defaultNote = 
        updateType === 'kirim' ? `Ombor kirimi${enteredLabel}${priceLabel}` : 
        updateType === 'chiqim' ? `Ombor chiqimi / Spisaniya${enteredLabel}` : 
        `Qoldiq reviziya qilindi${enteredLabel}`;

      const logEntry = {
        id: `${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
        date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
        type: logTypeLabel,
        itemName: selectedStockItem.name,
        productName: selectedStockItem.name,
        diffAmount: diffInBase > 0 ? `+${diffInBase}` : `${diffInBase}`,
        amount: Math.abs(diffInBase),
        unit: itemBaseUnit,
        note: updateNote ? `${updateNote}${enteredLabel}${priceLabel}` : defaultNote,
        reason: updateNote ? `${updateNote}${enteredLabel}${priceLabel}` : defaultNote,
        user: currentUser?.name || 'Omborchi'
      };
      setInventoryLogs(prev => [logEntry, ...(Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [])]);
    }

    if (typeof addAuditLog === 'function' && diffInBase !== 0) {
      addAuditLog(
        `Ombor: ${updateType.toUpperCase()}`,
        `${selectedStockItem.name}: ${diffInBase > 0 ? `+${diffInBase}` : diffInBase} ${itemBaseUnit} (Yangi qoldiq: ${newQtyInBase} ${itemBaseUnit})`
      );
    }

    setIsUpdateModalOpen(false);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 4. FILTRLANGAN RO'YXAT VA YORDAMCHI RENDER
  // ==========================================
  const filteredInventory = useMemo(() => {
    return safeInventory.filter(item => {
      if (!item) return false;

      if (selectedCategory !== 'Barchasi' && (item.category || 'Boshqa') !== selectedCategory) {
        return false;
      }

      const qty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 0) || 0;
      const min = Number(item.minLimit ?? 0) || 0;

      if (stockFilter === 'low' && (qty > min || qty <= 0)) return false;
      if (stockFilter === 'out' && qty > 0) return false;

      if (searchQuery.trim() !== '') {
        return String(item.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase());
      }

      return true;
    });
  }, [safeInventory, selectedCategory, stockFilter, searchQuery]);

  const renderMultiUnitBadges = (item) => {
    const qty = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 0) || 0;
    const unit = normalizeUnitCode(item.unit || 'kg');
    const breakdown = getMultiScaleBreakdown(qty, unit);

    if (breakdown.family === 'weight') {
      if (displayUnitMode === 'gr') {
        return (
          <div className="font-black text-lg text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">
            {breakdown.gr.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-xs">gr</span>
          </div>
        );
      }
      if (displayUnitMode === 'kg') {
        return (
          <div className="font-black text-lg text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
            {breakdown.kg.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-xs">kg</span>
          </div>
        );
      }
      if (displayUnitMode === 'tonna') {
        return (
          <div className="font-black text-lg text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
            {breakdown.tonna.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-xs">tonna</span>
          </div>
        );
      }

      return (
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="font-black text-lg">
            {qty.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase">{unit}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-black">
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/30 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 border border-amber-200/60 dark:border-amber-800/50">
              {breakdown.tonna} t
            </span>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-200/60 dark:border-emerald-800/50">
              {breakdown.kg.toLocaleString()} kg
            </span>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-200/60 dark:border-indigo-800/50">
              {breakdown.gr.toLocaleString()} gr
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    if (breakdown.family === 'volume') {
      return (
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="font-black text-lg">
            {qty.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase">{unit}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-black">
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300">
              {breakdown.litr.toLocaleString()} litr
            </span>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-900/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300">
              {breakdown.ml.toLocaleString()} ml
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return (
      <div className="font-black text-lg">
        {qty.toLocaleString()} <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">{unit}</span>
      </div>
    );
  };

  const itemModalBreakdown = getMultiScaleBreakdown(itemData.quantity, itemData.unit);
  const selectedItemFamily = selectedStockItem ? getUnitMeta(selectedStockItem.unit).family : 'count';
  const compatibleUpdateUnits = UNIT_OPTIONS.filter(u => u.family === selectedItemFamily);

  // ==========================================
  // 5. RENDER (UI DIZAYN)
  // ==========================================
  return (
    <div className="animate-fade-in space-y-4">
      
      {/* OMBOR MOLIYAVIY QIYMATI BANNERI */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 text-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="text-3xl">ðŸ’°</span>
          <div>
            <p className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-100">
              Ombordagi Jami Xomashyolar Qiymati (Aktivlar)
            </p>
            <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black">
              {totalWarehouseValuation.toLocaleString()} so'm
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span className="text-xs font-bold bg-white/20 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl">
          ðŸ§® Taomlar tannarxi shu narxlardan avtomatik hisoblanadi
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* YUQORI BOSHQARUV PANELI: QIDIRUV, FILTR VA YANGI QO'SHISH */}
      <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row justify-between items-stretch lg:items-center gap-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/50 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
        
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5 flex-1">
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="ðŸ” Mahsulot nomini qidiring..."
              className="w-full pl-4 pr-9 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white outline-none focus:border-purple-500"
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 font-black"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <select
            value={selectedCategory}
            onChange={e => setSelectedCategory(e.target.value)}
            className="px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-xs font-black text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200 outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="Barchasi">ðŸ“ Barcha toifalar ({safeInventory.length})</option>
            {inventoryCategories.map(cat => (
              <option key={cat} value={cat}>{cat}</option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <div className="flex bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-gray-800 p-1 rounded-xl">
            {[
              { id: 'all', label: 'Barchasi' },
              { id: 'low', label: 'âš ï¸ Kam qoldi' },
              { id: 'out', label: 'ðŸ›‘ Tugagan' }
            ].map(f => (
              <button
                key={f.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setStockFilter(f.id)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-black whitespace-nowrap transition ${
                  stockFilter === f.id

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export function useSticky(key, defaultValue) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const stickyValue = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
      return stickyValue !== null ? JSON.parse(stickyValue) : defaultValue;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`useSticky o'qishda xato (${key}):`, error);
      return defaultValue;
    }
  });

  // 1. O'zgarish bo'lganda localStorage'ga yozish va shu oynadagi komponentlarga bildirish
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(value);
      const currentInStorage = window.localStorage.getItem(key);

      if (currentInStorage !== serialized) {
        window.localStorage.setItem(key, serialized);
        window.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent('local-storage-update', { detail: { key, newValue: value } })
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`useSticky yozishda xato (${key}):`, error);
    }
  }, [key, value]);

  // 2. Boshqa oynadan (tabdan) kelgan o'zgarishlarni F5 bosmasdan jonli ushlash
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleStorageChange = (event) => {
      if (event.key === key && event.newValue !== null) {
        try {
          setValue(JSON.parse(event.newValue));
        } catch (err) {
          console.error(err);
        }
      }
    };

    const handleCustomEvent = (event) => {
      if (event.detail?.key === key) {
        setValue(event.detail.newValue);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('storage', handleStorageChange);
    window.addEventListener('local-storage-update', handleCustomEvent);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('storage', handleStorageChange);
      window.removeEventListener('local-storage-update', handleCustomEvent);
    };
  }, [key]);

  return [value, setValue];
}

export default useSticky;

// src/utils/telegramNotifier.js

export const DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG = {
  enabled: false,
  botToken: '',
  chatId: '',
  notifyOnZReport: true,       // Smena yopilganda (Z-Otchyot)
  notifyOnDebt: true,          // Chek qarzga yozilganda
  notifyOnDiscount: true,      // Chegirma berilganda
  notifyOnLowStock: true,      // Omborda xomashyo kam qolganda
  notifyOnEverySale: false,    // Har bir oddiy sotuvda
  notifyOnSecurity: true       // Muhim o'chirish va xavfsizlik amallarida
};

// LocalStorage'dan Telegram sozlamalarini xavfsiz o'qish
export const getTelegramConfig = () => {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem('pos_telegram_config');
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return { ...DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG, ...(parsed || {}) };
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_TELEGRAM_CONFIG;
  }
};

// HTML belgilarni xavfsiz kodlash (Taom nomida <, >, & bo'lsa xato bermasligi uchun)
const escapeHtml = (str) => {
  return String(str ?? '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
};

// Asosiy xabar yuboruvchi funksiya (Bir yoki vergul bilan ajratilgan bir nechta Chat ID ga yuboradi)
export async function sendTelegramMessage(htmlText, eventType = 'general', customConfig = null) {
  const config = customConfig || getTelegramConfig();

  if (!config.enabled && eventType !== 'test') {
    return { ok: false, skipped: true, reason: "Telegram xabarnoma o'chirilgan" };
  }

  // Hodisa turiga qarab ruxsatni tekshirish
  if (eventType === 'z_report' && !config.notifyOnZReport) return { ok: false, skipped: true };
  if (eventType === 'debt' && !config.notifyOnDebt) return { ok: false, skipped: true };
  if (eventType === 'discount' && !config.notifyOnDiscount) return { ok: false, skipped: true };
  if (eventType === 'low_stock' && !config.notifyOnLowStock) return { ok: false, skipped: true };
  if (eventType === 'sale' && !config.notifyOnEverySale) return { ok: false, skipped: true };
  if (eventType === 'security' && !config.notifyOnSecurity) return { ok: false, skipped: true };

  const token = String(config.botToken || '').trim();
  const rawChatIds = String(config.chatId || '').trim();

  if (!token || !rawChatIds) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Bot Token yoki Chat ID kiritilmagan!' };
  }

  // Vergul bilan bir nechta Chat ID yozilgan bo'lsa, har biriga yuboradi
  const chatIds = rawChatIds
    .split(',')
    .map(id => id.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);

  try {
    const results = await Promise.all(
      chatIds.map(async (targetChatId) => {
        const response = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/sendMessage`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            chat_id: targetChatId,
            text: htmlText,
            parse_mode: 'HTML',
            disable_web_page_preview: true
          })
        });
        return response.json();
      })
    );

    const firstError = results.find(r => !r.ok);
    if (firstError) {
      return { ok: false, error: firstError.description || 'Telegram API xatosi' };
    }

    return { ok: true, results };
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Telegram xabarini yuborishda internet xatosi:', err);
    return { ok: false, error: "Internet aloqasi yo'q yoki Telegram serveriga ulanib bo'lmadi." };
  }
}

// Botga /start bosgan rahbarning Chat ID sini avtomatik topib berish
export async function detectTelegramChatId(botToken) {
  const cleanToken = String(botToken || '').trim();
  if (!cleanToken) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Avval Bot Tokenni kiriting!' };
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${cleanToken}/getUpdates`);
    const data = await res.json();

    if (!data.ok) {
      return { ok: false, error: data.description || "Bot Token noto'g'ri!" };
    }

    const updates = Array.isArray(data.result) ? data.result : [];
    if (updates.length === 0) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: "Xabarlar topilmadi! Telegramda botingizga kirib '/start' tugmasini bosing va qayta urinib ko'ring."
      };
    }

    // Eng oxirgi yozgan foydalanuvchi yoki guruhning Chat ID sini olamiz
    for (let i = updates.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const msg = updates[i].message || updates[i].my_chat_member || updates[i].channel_post;
      if (msg && msg.chat && msg.chat.id) {
        const chatTitle = msg.chat.title || `${msg.chat.first_name || ''} ${msg.chat.last_name || ''}`.trim() || msg.chat.username || 'Foydalanuvchi';
        return {
          ok: true,
          chatId: String(msg.chat.id),
          chatName: chatTitle
        };
      }
    }

    return { ok: false, error: "Chat ID topilmadi. Botga '/start' deb yozib qayta bosing." };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: "Internet aloqasini tekshiring!" };
  }
}

export async function notifyOrderClosedTelegram({
  order,
  totals,
  cafeName = 'Premium Cafe',
  lowStockAlerts = []
}) {
  if (!order) return;

  const discountAmt = Number(totals?.discountAmount ?? order.discountAmount ?? 0) || 0;
  const finalAmt = Number(totals?.finalTotal ?? order.finalTotal ?? order.total ?? 0) || 0;
  const subTotalAmt = Number(totals?.subTotal ?? order.subTotal ?? finalAmt) || 0;
  const serviceAmt = Number(totals?.serviceFeeAmount ?? order.serviceFeeAmount ?? 0) || 0;

  const shouldSendOrderMsg =
    (isDebt && config.notifyOnDebt) ||
    (discountAmt > 0 && config.notifyOnDiscount) ||
    config.notifyOnEverySale;

  if (shouldSendOrderMsg) {

    const itemsList = Array.isArray(order.items)

          })
          .join('\n')
      : '';

      isDebt ? `âš ï¸ <b>Qarzdor mijoz:</b> ${escapeHtml(order.debtorName || order.paymentDetails?.debtorName || "Kiritilmagan")}` : null,
      `â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”`,
      `ðŸ½ <b>Taomlar:</b>\n${itemsList}`,
      `â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”â”`,

      ),

    const newLogs = [];

    cartItems.forEach(orderItem => {
      const menuObj = menuItems.find(m => m.id === orderItem.id || m.name === orderItem.baseName || m.name === orderItem.name);
      const recipe = menuObj?.recipe || menuObj?.ingredients;

      if (Array.isArray(recipe) && recipe.length > 0) {
        const itemQty = Number(orderItem.quantity || orderItem.qty || 1);
        recipe.forEach(ing => {
          const invIdx = newInventory.findIndex(
            i => String(i.id) === String(ing.inventoryId ?? ing.productId ?? ing.id) || i.name === ing.name
          );
          if (invIdx !== -1) {
            const deductAmount = Number(ing.qty ?? ing.amount ?? ing.quantity ?? 0) * itemQty;
            newInventory[invIdx] = {
              ...newInventory[invIdx],
              quantity: Math.max(0, Number(newInventory[invIdx].quantity || 0) - deductAmount)
            };
            invUpdated = true;
            newLogs.push({
              id: `${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
              date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
              itemName: newInventory[invIdx].name,
              productName: newInventory[invIdx].name,
              type: 'chiqim_avto',
              diffAmount: `-${deductAmount}`,
              amount: deductAmount,
              unit: newInventory[invIdx].unit || 'dona',
              note: `Sotuv (${tableLabel}: ${orderItem.name} x${itemQty})`,
              reason: `Sotuv (${tableLabel}: ${orderItem.name} x${itemQty})`,
              user: currentUser?.name || 'QR-Mijoz'
            });
          }
        });
      }
    });

    if (invUpdated) {
      setInventory(newInventory);
      if (newLogs.length > 0) {
        setInventoryLogs(prev => [...newLogs, ...(Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [])]);
      }
    }
  };

  // Universal Buyurtma Qabul Qilish (Ham WaiterMode, ham CustomerQRView uchun)
  const handleOrderSubmit = (arg1, arg2) => {
    let targetTable = guestTable || '1-Stol';
    let incomingItems = [];

    if (Array.isArray(arg1)) {
      incomingItems = arg1;
      if (typeof arg2 === 'string' && arg2.trim() !== '') {
        targetTable = arg2;
      }
    } else if (typeof arg1 === 'string') {
      targetTable = arg1;
      incomingItems = Array.isArray(arg2) ? arg2 : [];
    } else if (arg1 && typeof arg1 === 'object' && Array.isArray(arg1.items)) {
      targetTable = arg1.table || targetTable;
      incomingItems = arg1.items;
    }

    if (incomingItems.length === 0) return;

    const normalizedItems = incomingItems.map(item => {
      const q = Number(item.quantity ?? item.qty ?? 1) || 1;
      return {
        ...item,
        cartItemId: item.cartItemId || `${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
        price: Number(item.price ?? item.variants?.[0]?.price ?? 0),
        quantity: q,
        qty: q,
        sentQty: q
      };
    });

    deductInventoryForItems(normalizedItems, targetTable);

    setOrders(prevOrders => {
      const safeOrders = Array.isArray(prevOrders) ? prevOrders : [];
      
      const existingOrderIndex = safeOrders.findIndex(
        o => o && o.table === targetTable && !["To'langan", 'Qarz', 'Arxiv'].includes(o.status)
      );

      if (existingOrderIndex !== -1 && targetTable !== 'Olib ketish') {
        const updatedOrders = [...safeOrders];
        const existingOrder = updatedOrders[existingOrderIndex];
        const combinedItems = [...(existingOrder.items || []), ...normalizedItems];

        const newSubtotal = combinedItems.reduce(
          (sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.price || 0) * Number(it.quantity || it.qty || 1)),
          0
        );
        const newServiceFeeAmount = Math.round((newSubtotal * serviceFee) / 100);
        const newTotal = newSubtotal + newServiceFeeAmount;

        updatedOrders[existingOrderIndex] = {
          ...existingOrder,
          items: combinedItems,
          subTotal: newSubtotal,
          subtotal: newSubtotal,
          serviceFeeAmount: newServiceFeeAmount,
          total: newTotal,
          finalTotal: newTotal,
          status: 'Yangi',
          updatedAt: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
        };

        return updatedOrders;
      }

      const subtotal = normalizedItems.reduce(
        (sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.price || 0) * Number(it.quantity || it.qty || 1)),
        0
      );
      const isTakeaway = targetTable === 'Olib ketish';
      const serviceFeeAmount = isTakeaway ? 0 : Math.round((subtotal * serviceFee) / 100);
      const total = subtotal + serviceFeeAmount;

      const newOrder = {
        id: Date.now().toString(),
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        table: targetTable,
        waiter: currentUser?.name || 'QR-Menyu (Mijoz)',
        waiterName: currentUser?.name || 'QR-Menyu (Mijoz)',
        items: normalizedItems,
        subTotal: subtotal,
        subtotal,
        serviceFee: isTakeaway ? 0 : serviceFee,
        serviceFeePercent: isTakeaway ? 0 : serviceFee,
        serviceFeeAmount,
        total,
        finalTotal: total,
        status: 'Yangi',
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
        date: new Date().toLocaleDateString()
      };

      return [newOrder, ...safeOrders];
    });

    addAuditLog("Buyurtma yaratildi", `${targetTable} uchun ${normalizedItems.length} xil taom qo'shildi`);
  };

  // Buyurtma statusini yangilash
  const handleUpdateStatus = (orderId, newStatus, extraData = {}) => {
    setOrders(prev => {
      const safePrev = Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [];
      return safePrev.map(o => {
        if (String(o.id) === String(orderId)) {
          return {
            ...o,
            ...extraData,
            status: newStatus,
            updatedAt: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
          };
        }
        return o;
      });
    });
    addAuditLog("Status o'zgardi", `Buyurtma #${String(orderId).slice(-4)} -> ${newStatus}`);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 6. STOLNI KO'CHIRISH VA CHEKNI BO'LISH
  // ==========================================
  const handleTransferTable = (fromTable, toTable) => {
    if (!fromTable || !toTable || fromTable === toTable) return;

    setOrders(prev => {
      const safePrev = Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [];
      return safePrev.map(o => {
        if (o.table === fromTable && !["To'langan", 'Qarz', 'Arxiv'].includes(o.status)) {
          return { ...o, table: toTable };
        }
        return o;
      });
    });

    addAuditLog("Stol ko'chirildi", `${fromTable} -> ${toTable}`);
  };

  const handleSplitOrder = (orderId, itemsToMove, targetTable) => {
    if (!orderId || !Array.isArray(itemsToMove) || itemsToMove.length === 0 || !targetTable) return;

    setOrders(prev => {
      const safePrev = Array.isArray(prev) ? [...prev] : [];
      const sourceIndex = safePrev.findIndex(o => String(o.id) === String(orderId));
      if (sourceIndex === -1) return safePrev;

      const sourceOrder = safePrev[sourceIndex];
      const movedIds = new Set(itemsToMove.map(i => i.cartItemId || i.id));
      const remainingItems = (sourceOrder.items || []).filter(
        i => !movedIds.has(i.cartItemId || i.id)
      );

      const remSubtotal = remainingItems.reduce(
        (s, it) => s + (Number(it.price || 0) * Number(it.quantity || it.qty || 1)),
        0
      );
      const remService = Math.round((remSubtotal * serviceFee) / 100);

      safePrev[sourceIndex] = {
        ...sourceOrder,
        items: remainingItems,
        subTotal: remSubtotal,
        subtotal: remSubtotal,
        serviceFeeAmount: remService,
        total: remSubtotal + remService,
        finalTotal: remSubtotal + remService
      };

      const moveSubtotal = itemsToMove.reduce(
        (s, it) => s + (Number(it.price || 0) * Number(it.quantity || it.qty || 1)),
        0
      );
      const moveService = Math.round((moveSubtotal * serviceFee) / 100);

      const newSplitOrder = {
        id: Date.now().toString(),
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        table: targetTable,
        waiter: sourceOrder.waiter,
        waiterName: sourceOrder.waiterName || sourceOrder.waiter,
        items: itemsToMove,
        subTotal: moveSubtotal,
        subtotal: moveSubtotal,
        serviceFee,
        serviceFeePercent: serviceFee,
        serviceFeeAmount: moveService,
        total: moveSubtotal + moveService,
        finalTotal: moveSubtotal + moveService,
        status: sourceOrder.status,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
        date: new Date().toLocaleDateString()
      };

      return [newSplitOrder, ...safePrev];
    });

    addAuditLog("Chek bo'lindi", `Buyurtma #${String(orderId).slice(-4)} dan ${targetTable} ga bo'lindi`);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 7. OFITSIANT CHAQIRUVI (BELL / QO'NG'IROQCHA)
  // ==========================================
  const handleCallWaiter = (tableNameFromChild) => {
    const targetTable = typeof tableNameFromChild === 'string' && tableNameFromChild.trim() !== ''
      ? tableNameFromChild
      : (guestTable || '1-Stol');

    setWaiterCalls(prev => {
      const safePrev = Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [];
      const alreadyExists = safePrev.some(
        c => (typeof c === 'object' ? c.table === targetTable : c === targetTable)
      );
      if (alreadyExists) return safePrev;

      return [
        ...safePrev,
        {
          id: Date.now(),
          table: targetTable,
          time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
        }
      ];
    });
  };

  const handleClearCall = (callIdOrTable) => {
    setWaiterCalls(prev => {
      const safePrev = Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [];
      return safePrev.filter(
        c => c?.id !== callIdOrTable && c?.table !== callIdOrTable && c !== callIdOrTable
      );
    });
  };

  // ==========================================
  // 8. EKRANLARNI YO'NALTIRISH (ROUTING)
  // ==========================================

  // A) MIJOZ QR-MENYU REJIMI
  if (view === 'guest_qr') {
    return (
      <CustomerQRView
        cafeName={cafeName}
        table={guestTable}
        menuItems={menuItems}
        menuCategories={menuCategories}
        orders={orders}
        serviceFee={serviceFee}
        isDarkMode={isDarkMode}
        onSubmitOrder={(cartItems, tbl) => handleOrderSubmit(tbl || guestTable, cartItems)}
        onCallWaiter={(tbl) => handleCallWaiter(tbl || guestTable)}
        onExitGuest={() => setView('login')}
      />
    );
  }

  // B) QULF EKRANI (LOGIN REJIMI)
  if (view === 'login' || !currentUser) {
    return (
      <LockScreen
        cafeName={cafeName}
        users={users}
        tables={tables}
        orders={orders}
        isDarkMode={isDarkMode}
        setIsDarkMode={setIsDarkMode}
        onLogin={handleLogin}
        onGuestLogin={handleGuestLogin}
        onOpenQrMenu={handleGuestLogin}
      />
    );
  }

  // C) ASOSIY ISHCHI DASTUR REJIMI
  const currentActiveView = currentUser.role === 'admin' ? adminActiveView : currentUser.role;

  return (
    <div className={`min-h-screen transition-colors duration-300 ${
      isDarkMode ? 'dark bg-gray-900 text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-900'
    }`}>
      
      {/* --- GLOBAL TOP NAVBAR --- */}
      <header className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 px-4 md:px-6 py-3 flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-3 sticky top-0 z-40 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-black shadow-md shadow-indigo-600/30">
            ðŸ½ï¸
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="font-black text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-white leading-tight">
              {cafeName}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">
              {currentUser.name} â€¢ <span className="uppercase text-indigo-500">{currentUser.role}</span>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Admin uchun barcha ekranlarni ko'rish va boshqarish tablari */}
        {currentUser.role === 'admin' && (
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900/60 p-1.5 rounded-2xl overflow-x-auto hide-scrollbar">
            {[
              { id: 'admin', label: 'âš™ï¸ Boshqaruv' },
              { id: 'stats', label: 'ðŸ“Š Statistika' },
              { id: 'warehouse', label: 'ðŸ“¦ Ombor' },
              { id: 'waiter', label: `ðŸ“ Ofitsiant ${waiterCalls.length > 0 ? `(${waiterCalls.length})` : ''}` },
              { id: 'cashier', label: 'ðŸ’° Kassa' },
              { id: 'kitchen', label: 'ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ³ Oshxona' }
            ].map(tab => (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => setAdminActiveView(tab.id)}
                className={`px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl font-black text-xs whitespace-nowrap transition active:scale-95 ${
                  adminActiveView === tab.id
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                    : 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-white dark:hover:bg-gray-800'
                }`}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </nav>
        )}

        {/* O'ng taraf: Avto-Qulf sozlamasi (Telefon/Kompyuter uslubida), Tungi rejim va Chiqish */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <AutoLockControl
            currentUser={currentUser}
            users={users}
            cafeName={cafeName}
            onSwitchUser={handleLogin}
            onLogout={handleLogout}
          />

          <button
            onClick={() => setIsDarkMode(!isDarkMode)}
            className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center text-lg transition active:scale-90"
            title="Tungi / Kunduzgi rejim"
          >
            {isDarkMode ? 'â˜€ï¸' : 'ðŸŒ™'}
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/30 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 font-black px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm transition active:scale-95"
          >
            Chiqish ðŸ”’
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* --- ASOSIY ISHCHI MODULLAR --- */}
      <main className="p-3 md:p-5 max-w-[1600px] mx-auto">
        
        {/* 1. OFITSIANT TERMINALI */}
        {currentActiveView === 'waiter' && (
          <WaiterMode
            menuItems={menuItems}
            menuCategories={menuCategories}
            tables={tables}
            orders={orders}
            setOrders={setOrders}
            cafeName={cafeName}
            onOrderSubmit={handleOrderSubmit}
            onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
            onTransferTable={handleTransferTable}
            onSplitOrder={handleSplitOrder}
            onShowQrTable={handleShowQrTable}
            onPrintReceipt={(orderObj) => setReceiptToPrint(orderObj)}
            waiterCalls={waiterCalls}
            onClearCall={handleClearCall}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            waiterCanSeeSales={currentUser.role === 'admin' || currentUser.canSeeSales}
            serviceFee={serviceFee}
            requireAdminOverride={requireAdminOverride}
            addAuditLog={addAuditLog}
          />
        )}

        {/* 2. KASSA TERMINALI */}
        {currentActiveView === 'cashier' && (
          <CashierMode
            orders={orders}
            setOrders={setOrders}
            tables={tables}
            menuItems={menuItems}
            inventory={inventory}
            setInventory={setInventory}
            inventoryLogs={inventoryLogs}
            setInventoryLogs={setInventoryLogs}
            expenses={expenses}
            setExpenses={setExpenses}
            customers={customers}
            setCustomers={setCustomers}
            cafeName={cafeName}
            serviceFee={serviceFee}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
            onPrintReceipt={(orderObj) => setReceiptToPrint(orderObj)}
            setReceiptToPrint={setReceiptToPrint}
            requireAdminOverride={requireAdminOverride}
            addAuditLog={addAuditLog}
          />
        )}

        {/* 3. OSHXONA EKRANI (KDS) */}
        {currentActiveView === 'kitchen' && (
          <KitchenMode
            orders={orders}
            setOrders={setOrders}
            menuItems={menuItems}
            setMenuItems={setMenuItems}
            menuCategories={menuCategories}
            onUpdateStatus={handleUpdateStatus}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            addAuditLog={addAuditLog}
          />
        )}

        {/* 4. OMBORXONA BO'LIMI */}
        {currentActiveView === 'warehouse' && (
          <WarehouseMode
            inventory={inventory}
            setInventory={setInventory}
            inventoryLogs={inventoryLogs}
            setInventoryLogs={setInventoryLogs}
            menuItems={menuItems}
            setMenuItems={setMenuItems}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            requireAdminOverride={requireAdminOverride}
            addAuditLog={addAuditLog}
          />
        )}

        {/* 5. STATISTIKA VA ANALITIKA */}
        {currentActiveView === 'stats' && (
          <StatsMode
            orders={orders}
            setOrders={setOrders}
            menuItems={menuItems}
            users={users}
            inventory={inventory}
            inventoryLogs={inventoryLogs}
            expenses={expenses}
            setExpenses={setExpenses}
            serviceFee={serviceFee}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            auditLogs={auditLogs}
            setAuditLogs={setAuditLogs}
            addAuditLog={addAuditLog}
          />
        )}

        {/* 6. ADMIN BOSHQARUV PANELI */}
        {currentActiveView === 'admin' && (
          <AdminPanel
            cafeName={cafeName}
            setCafeName={setCafeName}
            serviceFee={serviceFee}
            setServiceFee={setServiceFee}
            menuItems={menuItems}
            setMenuItems={setMenuItems}
            menuCategories={menuCategories}
            setMenuCategories={setMenuCategories}
            users={users}
            setUsers={setUsers}
            orders={orders}
            setOrders={setOrders}
            tables={tables}
            setTables={setTables}
            inventory={inventory}
            setInventory={setInventory}
            inventoryLogs={inventoryLogs}
            setInventoryLogs={setInventoryLogs}
            auditLogs={auditLogs}
            setAuditLogs={setAuditLogs}
            isOverrideEnabled={isOverrideEnabled}
            setIsOverrideEnabled={setIsOverrideEnabled}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            requireAdminOverride={requireAdminOverride}
            addAuditLog={addAuditLog}
          />
        )}

        {/* Zaxira: CustomerMode */}
        {currentActiveView === 'customer' && (
          <CustomerMode
            cafeName={cafeName}
            table={guestTable}
            tableNumber={guestTable}
            menuItems={menuItems}
            menuCategories={menuCategories}
            orders={orders}
            serviceFee={serviceFee}
            isDarkMode={isDarkMode}
            onSubmitOrder={(cartItems, tbl) => handleOrderSubmit(tbl || guestTable, cartItems)}
            onCallWaiter={(tbl) => handleCallWaiter(tbl || guestTable)}
            onExitGuest={() => setView('login')}
          />
        )}

      </main>

      {/* --- GLOBAL MODALLAR --- */}
      {overrideRequest && (
        <ManagerOverrideModal
          request={overrideRequest}
          actionName={overrideRequest.actionName}
          users={users}
          onSuccess={overrideRequest.onSuccess}
          onCancel={() => setOverrideRequest(null)}
          onClose={() => setOverrideRequest(null)}
        />
      )}

      {receiptToPrint && (
        <PrintReceiptModal
          order={receiptToPrint}
          receipt={receiptToPrint}
          receiptData={receiptToPrint}
          cafeName={cafeName}
          serviceFee={serviceFee}
          onClose={() => setReceiptToPrint(null)}
        />
      )}

    </div>
  );
}
